000000r 2               CLK_CPS         = 3579545   ; ~3.58 MHz
000000r 2               CLK_CPMS        = (CLK_CPS / 1000) + 1
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; ***  ONBOARD SERIAL ADAPTER, 65C51  ***
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ROCKWELL_ACIA   = 1
000000r 2               ACIA_USE_VIA_TIMER = 0
000000r 2               
000000r 2               SR_2400         = $0A
000000r 2               SR_4800         = $0C
000000r 2               SR_9600         = $0E
000000r 2               SR_19200        = $0F
000000r 2               SR_115200       = $00
000000r 2               
000000r 2               SR_SELECT       = SR_19200
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .if SR_SELECT = SR_2400
000000r 2               SERIAL_RATE     = 2400
000000r 2               .elseif SR_SELECT = SR_4800
000000r 2               SERIAL_RATE     = 4800
000000r 2               .elseif SR_SELECT = SR_9600
000000r 2               SERIAL_RATE     = 9600
000000r 2               .elseif SR_SELECT = SR_19200
000000r 2               SERIAL_RATE     = 19200
000000r 2               .else
000000r 2               SERIAL_RATE     = 115200
000000r 2               .endif
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .if ROCKWELL_ACIA = 0
000000r 2                   .if ACIA_USE_VIA_TIMER = 0
000000r 2               SWT_INNER_LOOP_CYCLES = 5
000000r 2               BITS_PER_CHAR   = 10          ; 8 + start + stop.
000000r 2               SWT             = ((((CLK_CPS / SERIAL_RATE) + 1) * BITS_PER_CHAR) / SWT_INNER_LOOP_CYCLES) + 1
000000r 2                   .else
000000r 2               BITS_PER_CHAR   = 10          ; 8 + start + stop.
000000r 2               HWT_OVERHEAD    = 50
000000r 2               SWT             = (((CLK_CPS / SERIAL_RATE) + 1) * BITS_PER_CHAR) - HWT_OVERHEAD
000000r 2                   .endif
000000r 2               
000000r 2               SWT_SELECT_L    = SWT .MOD 256
000000r 2               SWT_SELECT_H    = SWT / 256
000000r 2               .endif
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; ***  END OF ONBOARD SERIAL ADAPTER  ***

000000r 2               ; SPI DATA BITS
000000r 2               SPI_BIT_CLK     = 1     ; bit 0, so INC/DEC cycle the clock
000000r 2               SPI_BIT_CSB     = 2     ; bit 1
000000r 2               SPI_BIT_MOSI    = 4     ; bit 2
000000r 2               SPI_BIT_CS_1    = 8     ; bit 3
000000r 2               SPI_BIT_CS_2    = $10   ; bit 4
000000r 2               SPI_BIT_CS_4    = $20   ; bit 5
000000r 2               SPI_BIT_CS_8    = $40   ; bit 6
000000r 2               SPI_BIT_MISO    = $80   ; bit 7, so BIT opcode stores MISO in N
000000r 2               
000000r 2               SPI_DEV_0       = 0
000000r 2               SPI_DEV_1       = SPI_BIT_CS_1
000000r 2               SPI_DEV_2       = SPI_BIT_CS_2
000000r 2               SPI_DEV_3       = SPI_BIT_CS_2 | SPI_BIT_CS_1
000000r 2               SPI_DEV_4       = SPI_BIT_CS_4
000000r 2               SPI_DEV_5       = SPI_BIT_CS_4 | SPI_BIT_CS_1
000000r 2               SPI_DEV_6       = SPI_BIT_CS_4 | SPI_BIT_CS_2
000000r 2               SPI_DEV_7       = SPI_BIT_CS_4 | SPI_BIT_CS_2 | SPI_BIT_CS_1
000000r 2               
000000r 2               SPI_DEV_8       = SPI_BIT_CS_8 | SPI_DEV_0
000000r 2               SPI_DEV_9       = SPI_BIT_CS_8 | SPI_DEV_1
000000r 2               SPI_DEV_A       = SPI_BIT_CS_8 | SPI_DEV_2
000000r 2               SPI_DEV_B       = SPI_BIT_CS_8 | SPI_DEV_3
000000r 2               SPI_DEV_C       = SPI_BIT_CS_8 | SPI_DEV_4
000000r 2               SPI_DEV_D       = SPI_BIT_CS_8 | SPI_DEV_5
000000r 2               SPI_DEV_E       = SPI_BIT_CS_8 | SPI_DEV_6
000000r 2               SPI_DEV_F       = SPI_BIT_CS_8 | SPI_DEV_7
000000r 2               
000000r 2               SPI_DDR_BITS    = SPI_BIT_CLK | SPI_BIT_CSB | SPI_BIT_MOSI | SPI_DEV_F
000000r 2

000000r 2               ; write a byte in A to the IO PORT
000000r 2               .macro IO_PORT_WRITE    port, byte, imm
000000r 2               .ifnblank       imm
000000r 2                               lda     #imm
000000r 2               .endif
000000r 2               .ifblank        byte
000000r 2                               sta     port
000000r 2               .else
000000r 2                               sta     IO_PORT_BYTE port, byte
000000r 2               .endif
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; read a byte into A from the IO PORT/Byte (PORT_N | BYTE_M)
000000r 2               .macro IO_PORT_READ     port, byte
000000r 2               .ifblank        byte
000000r 2                               lda     port
000000r 2               .else
000000r 2                               lda     IO_PORT_BYTE port, byte
000000r 2               .endif
000000r 2               .endmacro

000000r 2               ; SPI
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               .macro SPI_SEND_CMD b0, b1, b2, b3, b4, crc
000000r 2                               lda             #b0 | $40
000000r 2                               jsr             SPI_SEND
000000r 2                               lda             #b1
000000r 2                               jsr             SPI_TRANSCEIVE
000000r 2                               sta             ZP_TEMP_2
000000r 2                               lda             #b2
000000r 2                               jsr             SPI_TRANSCEIVE
000000r 2                               lda             #b3
000000r 2                               jsr             SPI_TRANSCEIVE
000000r 2                               lda             #b4
000000r 2                               jsr             SPI_TRANSCEIVE
000000r 2               .ifnblank       crc
000000r 2                               lda             #(crc << 1)+1
000000r 2               .else
000000r 2                               lda             #$FF
000000r 2               .endif
000000r 2                               jsr             SPI_TRANSCEIVE
000000r 2                               jsr             SPI_RECV
000000r 2                               sta             ZP_TEMP
000000r 2               .endmacro

000000r 2               .define LOADA(arg)      lda arg
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro  LDA_CORA    CharOrAddr
000000r 2               .ifnblank   CharOrAddr
000000r 2                               LOADA           CharOrAddr
000000r 2               .endif
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro META_CALL    call, C1, C2, C3, C4, C5, C6, C7, C8, C9
000000r 2                   .ifblank C2
000000r 2                               LDA_CORA        {C1}
000000r 2                               jsr             call
000000r 2                               .exitmacro
000000r 2                   .else
000000r 2                               lda             C1
000000r 2                               jsr             call
000000r 2                   .endif
000000r 2                               META_CALL       {call}, {C2}, {C3}, {C4}, {C5}, {C6}, {C7}, {C8}, {C9}
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro META_JMP     call, C1, C2, C3, C4, C5, C6, C7, C8, C9
000000r 2                   .ifblank C2
000000r 2                               LDA_CORA        {C1}
000000r 2                               jmp             call
000000r 2                               .exitmacro
000000r 2                   .else
000000r 2                               lda             C1
000000r 2                               jsr             call
000000r 2                   .endif
000000r 2                               META_JMP        {call}, {C2}, {C3}, {C4}, {C5}, {C6}, {C7}, {C8}, {C9}
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro  PRINT_CHAR      C1, C2, C3, C4, C5, C6, C7, C8, C9
000000r 2                               META_CALL       WRITE_CHAR, {C1}, {C2}, {C3}, {C4}, {C5}, {C6}, {C7}, {C8}, {C9}
000000r 2               .endmacro

000000r 2               INPUT_BUFFER:                                ;    $200, for serial input
000000r 2  xx xx xx xx                  .res            $100
000004r 2  xx xx xx xx  
000008r 2  xx xx xx xx  
000100r 2               WOZ_IN:
000100r 2  xx xx xx xx                  .res            $100         ;    $300 for WOZMON
000104r 2  xx xx xx xx  
000108r 2  xx xx xx xx  
000200r 2               
000200r 2               .segment "BIOS"
000013r 2               
000013r 2               SER_SEND_STATUS_READY = 0
000013r 2               SER_SEND_STATUS_BUSY  = 1
000013r 2               SER_SEND_STATUS_ERROR = $FF
000013r 2               ;
000013r 2               ; usually ends up at $F840  (see os_rom_C02.cfg)
000013r 2               ;
000013r 2  30 31 32 33  HEX_MAP: .byte "0123456789ABCDEF"
000017r 2  34 35 36 37  
00001Br 2  38 39 41 42  
000023r 2               
000023r 2               SERIAL_INIT:
000023r 2  78                           sei
000024r 2  A9 1F                        lda             #$10 | SR_SELECT    ; 8-N-1
000026r 2  8D rr rr                     sta             ACIA_R_CTRL
000029r 2               .if ROCKWELL_ACIA = 1
000029r 2  A9 05                        lda             #ACIA_CMD_BIT_DTRL | ACIA_CMD_BIT_TLIE  ; No parity, no echo, tx & rx interrupts.
00002Br 2               .else
00002Br 2                               lda             #ACIA_CMD_BIT_DTRL | ACIA_CMD_BIT_TLID  ; No parity, no echo, rx interrupts.
00002Br 2               .endif
00002Br 2  8D rr rr                     sta             ACIA_R_CMD
00002Er 2               .if ROCKWELL_ACIA = 1 .OR ACIA_USE_VIA_TIMER = 1
00002Er 2  A9 00                        lda             #SER_SEND_STATUS_READY
000030r 2  85 rr                        sta             ZP_SER_SEND_STATUS
000032r 2               .endif
000032r 2  A2 06                        ldx             #IRQ_NUMBER_ONBOARD_SERIAL
000034r 2  A9 rr                        lda             #<SERIAL_IRQ_HANDLER
000036r 2  A0 rr                        ldy             #>SERIAL_IRQ_HANDLER
000038r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             IRQ_SET_VECTOR
00003Br 2  58                           cli
00003Cr 2  60                           rts
00003Dr 2               
00003Dr 2               ; Input a character from the serial interface.
00003Dr 2               ; On return, carry flag indicates whether a key was pressed
00003Dr 2               ; If a key was pressed, the key value will be in the A register
00003Dr 2               ;
00003Dr 2               ; Modifies: flags, A
00003Dr 2               ; TODO: select the appropriate read stream for the current task
00003Dr 2               READ_CHAR_NE:                                         ; no echo entry
00003Dr 2  A9 01                        lda   #1
00003Fr 2  85 rr                        sta   ZP_TEMP
000041r 2  80 02                        bra   SERIAL_READ
000043r 2               READ_CHAR:
000043r 2  64 rr                        stz   ZP_TEMP
000045r 2               SERIAL_READ:
000045r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             BUFFER_SIZE
000048r 2  D0 02                        bne   :+
00004Ar 2  18                           clc
00004Br 2  60                           rts
00004Cr 2               :
00004Cr 2  DA                           phx
00004Dr 2  A6 rr                        ldx             ZP_READ_PTR
00004Fr 2  BD rr rr                     lda             INPUT_BUFFER,X
000052r 2  E6 rr                        inc             ZP_READ_PTR
000054r 2  A6 rr                        ldx             ZP_TEMP
000056r 2  D0 03                        bne    :+                               ; no echo
000058r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr WRITE_CHAR                          ; echo
00005Br 2  FA           :               plx
00005Cr 2  38                           sec
00005Dr 2  60                           rts
00005Er 2               
00005Er 2               ; Write decimal value of .A to output
00005Er 2               WRITE_DEC:
00005Er 2  C9 00                        cmp             #0
000060r 2  10 18                        bpl             WRITE_DEC_U
000062r 2  48                           pha
000063r 2  A9 2D 20 rr                  PRINT_CHAR      #'-'
000067r 2  rr           
000068r 2  68                           pla
000069r 2  C9 80                        cmp             #$80                        ; special case for -128
00006Br 2  D0 0A                        bne             :+
00006Dr 2  A9 31 20 rr                  PRINT_CHAR      #'1'
000071r 2  rr           
000072r 2  A9 28 4C rr                  PRINT_BYTE_JMP  #$28
000076r 2  rr           
000077r 2               :
000077r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             NEGATE
00007Ar 2               
00007Ar 2               WRITE_DEC_U:
00007Ar 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             MOD_10
00007Dr 2  E0 00                        cpx             #0
00007Fr 2  F0 1B                        beq             :++++
000081r 2  5A                           phy
000082r 2  A8                           tay
000083r 2  8A                           txa
000084r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             MOD_10
000087r 2  E0 00                        cpx             #0
000089r 2  D0 06                        bne             :+
00008Br 2  C9 00                        cmp             #0
00008Dr 2  D0 08                        bne             :++
00008Fr 2  80 09                        bra             :+++
000091r 2               :
000091r 2  48                           pha
000092r 2  8A                           txa
000093r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             WRITE_HEX
000096r 2  68                           pla
000097r 2               :
000097r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             WRITE_HEX
00009Ar 2               :
00009Ar 2  98                           tya
00009Br 2  7A                           ply
00009Cr 2               :
00009Cr 2  4C rr rr                     jmp             WRITE_HEX
00009Fr 2               
00009Fr 2               WRITE_BYTE_MIN:
00009Fr 2  C9 10                        cmp             #$10
0000A1r 2  90 0B                        bcc             WRITE_HEX
0000A3r 2               
0000A3r 2               WRITE_BYTE:
0000A3r 2  48                           pha                                         ; Save A for LSD.
0000A4r 2  4A                           lsr
0000A5r 2  4A                           lsr
0000A6r 2  4A                           lsr
0000A7r 2  4A                           lsr                                         ; MSD to LSD position.
0000A8r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             WRITE_HEX                   ; Output hex digit.
0000ABr 2  68                           pla                                         ; Restore A.
0000ACr 2               
0000ACr 2               WRITE_HEX_MASK:
0000ACr 2  29 0F                        and             #$0F                        ; Mask LSD for hex print.
0000AEr 2               
0000AEr 2               WRITE_HEX:
0000AEr 2  DA                           phx
0000AFr 2  AA                           tax
0000B0r 2  BD rr rr                     lda             HEX_MAP,x
0000B3r 2  24                           .byte           $24                        ; skip a byte (over phx below)
0000B4r 2                                                                          ; changed from $22 (PGS 11/8/26)
0000B4r 2               ; Output a character (from the A register) to the serial interface.
0000B4r 2               ;
0000B4r 2               ; Modifies: flags
0000B4r 2               ; TODO: select appropriate output stream for the given task
0000B4r 2               WRITE_CHAR:
0000B4r 2               SERIAL_WRITE:
0000B4r 2  DA                           phx
0000B5r 2               
0000B5r 2               .if ROCKWELL_ACIA = 0
0000B5r 2                               phy
0000B5r 2               .endif
0000B5r 2               WRITE_DELAY:
0000B5r 2               .if ROCKWELL_ACIA = 1 .OR ACIA_USE_VIA_TIMER = 1
0000B5r 2  A6 rr                        ldx             ZP_SER_SEND_STATUS
0000B7r 2  E0 00                        cpx             #SER_SEND_STATUS_READY
0000B9r 2  F0 03                        beq             :+
0000BBr 2  CB                           wai                                         ; Leave this in, even if RDY has a pull-up
0000BCr 2  80 F7                        bra             WRITE_DELAY
0000BEr 2               :
0000BEr 2               .endif
0000BEr 2  8D rr rr                     IO_PORT_WRITE   ACIA_R_DATA
0000C1r 2               
0000C1r 2               .if ROCKWELL_ACIA = 1 .OR ACIA_USE_VIA_TIMER = 1
0000C1r 2  A2 01                        ldx             #SER_SEND_STATUS_BUSY
0000C3r 2  86 rr                        stx             ZP_SER_SEND_STATUS
0000C5r 2               .endif
0000C5r 2               
0000C5r 2               .if ROCKWELL_ACIA = 0
0000C5r 2                   .if ACIA_USE_VIA_TIMER = 1
0000C5r 2                               pha
0000C5r 2                               lda             #SWT_SELECT_L
0000C5r 2                               ldy             #SWT_SELECT_H
0000C5r 2                               jsr             VIA_START_T2
0000C5r 2                               pla
0000C5r 2                   .else
0000C5r 2                               ldx             #SWT_SELECT_L + 1
0000C5r 2                               ldy             #SWT_SELECT_H + 1
0000C5r 2               :
0000C5r 2                               dex
0000C5r 2                               bne             :-
0000C5r 2                               dey
0000C5r 2                               bne             :-
0000C5r 2                   .endif
0000C5r 2                               ply
0000C5r 2               .endif
0000C5r 2               
0000C5r 2  FA                           plx
0000C6r 2  60                           rts
0000C7r 2               
0000C7r 2               ; Convenience method to write CR/LF to output stream
0000C7r 2               WRITE_CRLF:
0000C7r 2  A9 0D 20 rr                  PRINT_CHAR_JMP  #$0D, #$0A
0000CBr 2  rr A9 0A 4C  
0000CFr 2  rr rr        
0000D1r 2               
0000D1r 2               WRITE_P2:
0000D1r 2  A9 0D                        lda #$0D
0000D3r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr WRITE_CHAR
0000D6r 2  A9 0A                        lda #$0A
0000D8r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr WRITE_CHAR
0000DBr 2  A9 3E                        lda #'>'
0000DDr 2  20 rr rr                     jsr WRITE_CHAR
0000E0r 2  60                           rts
0000E1r 2               
0000E1r 2               WRITE_PROMPT:
0000E1r 2  20 rr rr                     PRINT_CRLF
0000E4r 2  A9 54 20 rr                  PRINT_CHAR      #'T'
0000E8r 2  rr           
0000E9r 2  AD F0 FF 20                  PRINT_HEX_MASK  $FFF0
0000EDr 2  rr rr        
0000EFr 2  A9 20 20 rr                  PRINT_SPACE
0000F3r 2  rr           
0000F4r 2  A5 rr 20 rr                  PRINT_BYTE      RAM_BANK_REG
0000F8r 2  rr           
0000F9r 2  C9 F0                        cmp             #$F0
0000FBr 2  90 10                        bcc             @not_shared
0000FDr 2  A9 28 20 rr                  PRINT_CHAR      #'('
000101r 2  rr           
000102r 2  AD F1 FF 20                  PRINT_HEX_MASK  $FFF1                       ; Shared RAM sub-bank
000106r 2  rr rr        
000108r 2  A9 29 20 rr                  PRINT_CHAR      #')'
00010Cr 2  rr           
00010Dr 2               
00010Dr 2               @not_shared:
00010Dr 2  A9 3A 20 rr                  PRINT_CHAR      #':'
000111r 2  rr           
000112r 2  A5 rr 20 rr                  PRINT_BYTE      ROM_BANK_REG
000116r 2  rr           
000117r 2  A9 3E 4C rr                  PRINT_CHAR_JMP  #'>'
00011Br 2  rr           
00011Cr 2               
00011Cr 2               ; .A, .Y hold the addr of HString to write
00011Cr 2               ; Clobbers .A, .Y; Preserves .X
00011Cr 2               WRITE_HSTRING:
00011Cr 2  DA                           phx
00011Dr 2  85 rr                        sta             ZP_HS_TEMP
00011Fr 2  84 rr                        sty             ZP_HS_TEMP + 1
000121r 2  B2 rr                        lda             (ZP_HS_TEMP)                ; Length of HString
000123r 2  AA                           tax
000124r 2  A0 00                        ldy             #0
000126r 2               @write_loop:
000126r 2  C8                           iny
000127r 2  B1 rr 20 rr                  PRINT_CHAR      {(ZP_HS_TEMP),Y}
00012Br 2  rr           
00012Cr 2  CA                           dex
00012Dr 2  D0 F7                        bne             @write_loop
00012Fr 2  FA                           plx
000130r 2  60                           rts
000131r 2               
000131r 2               ; Initialize the circular input buffer
000131r 2               ; Modifies: flags, A
000131r 2               INIT_BUFFER:
000131r 2  A5 rr                        lda           ZP_READ_PTR
000133r 2  85 rr                        sta           ZP_WRITE_PTR
000135r 2  60                           rts
000136r 2               
000136r 2               ; Set up the SPI interface registers on the VIA
000136r 2               SPI_INIT:
000136r 2  48                           pha
000137r 2  A9 00 8D rr                  IO_PORT_WRITE   VIA_R_AUX_CTRL, , 0
00013Br 2  rr           
00013Cr 2  8D rr rr                     IO_PORT_WRITE   VIA_R_INT_ENABLE
00013Fr 2  A9 FF 8D rr                  IO_PORT_WRITE   VIA_R_PER_CTRL, , $FF
000143r 2  rr           
000144r 2  A9 7F 8D rr                  IO_PORT_WRITE   IOR_SPI_DDR,  , SPI_DDR_BITS
000148r 2  rr           
000149r 2  A9 02 8D rr                  IO_PORT_WRITE   IOR_SPI_DATA, , SPI_BIT_CSB   ; de-select all SPI devices
00014Dr 2  rr           
00014Er 2  68                           pla
00014Fr 2  60                           rts
000150r 2               
000150r 2               ; Macro to remove essentially duplicate code
000150r 2               .macro          SPI_SEND_SETUP  mode
000150r 2                               sta             ZP_SPI_DATA_OUT
000150r 2                               phy
000150r 2                               txa
000150r 2                               ora             #SPI_BIT_MOSI
000150r 2                               tay
000150r 2                               lda             ZP_SPI_DATA_OUT
000150r 2                               sei
000150r 2               .ifblank        mode
000150r 2                               asl             ZP_SPI_DATA_IN
000150r 2               .endif
000150r 2                               sec
000150r 2                               rol
000150r 2               .endmacro
000150r 2               
000150r 2               ; Write and Read SPI data
000150r 2               ; Uses two ZP registers for data_in and data_out
000150r 2               ; A: data to send
000150r 2               ; X: device ID to send to/receive from
000150r 2               ; Returns input data in A
000150r 2               ; Modifies A, ZP_SPI_DATA_IN, ZP_SPI_DATA_OUT
000150r 2               SPI_TRANSCEIVE:
000150r 2  85 rr 5A 8A                  SPI_SEND_SETUP
000154r 2  09 04 A8 A5  
000158r 2  rr 78 06 rr  
00015Er 2  B0 05                        bcs             @spi_send_1
000160r 2               @spi_send_0:
000160r 2  8E rr rr                     stx             IOR_SPI_DATA
000163r 2  80 03                        bra             @spi_send
000165r 2               @spi_send_1:
000165r 2  8C rr rr                     sty             IOR_SPI_DATA
000168r 2               @spi_send:
000168r 2  EE rr rr                     inc             IOR_SPI_DATA        ; SPI_CLK = 1
00016Br 2  2C rr rr                     bit             IOR_SPI_DATA        ; MISO (bit 7) => N flag
00016Er 2  10 02                        bpl             @spi_recv
000170r 2  E6 rr                        inc             ZP_SPI_DATA_IN      ; incoming bit was a 1 (set LSb = 1)
000172r 2               @spi_recv:
000172r 2  0A                           asl
000173r 2  F0 0A                        beq             SPI_OPERATION_DONE
000175r 2  B0 04                        bcs             @had_1
000177r 2  06 rr                        asl             ZP_SPI_DATA_IN
000179r 2  80 E5                        bra             @spi_send_0
00017Br 2               @had_1:
00017Br 2  06 rr                        asl             ZP_SPI_DATA_IN
00017Dr 2  80 E6                        bra             @spi_send_1
00017Fr 2               
00017Fr 2               SPI_OPERATION_DONE:
00017Fr 2  A9 02                        lda             #SPI_BIT_CSB        ; de-select all SPI devices
000181r 2  0C rr rr                     tsb             IOR_SPI_DATA
000184r 2  7A                           ply
000185r 2  A5 rr                        lda             ZP_SPI_DATA_IN      ; load the input for return in A
000187r 2  58                           cli
000188r 2  60                           rts
000189r 2               
000189r 2               ; Write SPI data
000189r 2               ; Uses two ZP registers for data_in and data_out
000189r 2               ; A: data to send
000189r 2               ; X: device ID to send to
000189r 2               ; Modifies A, ZP_SPI_DATA_OUT
000189r 2               SPI_SEND:
000189r 2  85 rr 5A 8A                  SPI_SEND_SETUP  1
00018Dr 2  09 04 A8 A5  
000191r 2  rr 78 38 2A  
000195r 2               @send_loop:
000195r 2  B0 05                        bcs             @spi_send_1
000197r 2  8E rr rr                     stx             IOR_SPI_DATA
00019Ar 2  80 03                        bra             @spi_send
00019Cr 2               @spi_send_1:
00019Cr 2  8C rr rr                     sty             IOR_SPI_DATA
00019Fr 2               @spi_send:
00019Fr 2  EE rr rr                     inc             IOR_SPI_DATA        ; SPI_CLK = 1
0001A2r 2  0A                           asl
0001A3r 2  D0 F0                        bne             @send_loop
0001A5r 2  4C rr rr                     jmp             SPI_OPERATION_DONE
0001A8r 2               
0001A8r 2               ; Read from the SPI device
0001A8r 2               ; X: device to read from
0001A8r 2               ; Result returned in A
0001A8r 2               SPI_RECV:
0001A8r 2  5A                           phy
0001A9r 2  A0 08                        ldy             #8
0001ABr 2  8A                           txa
0001ACr 2  09 06                        ora             #SPI_BIT_MOSI | SPI_BIT_CSB
0001AEr 2  8D rr rr                     sta             IOR_SPI_DATA        ; Select the device to receive from
0001B1r 2  78                           sei
0001B2r 2               @recv_loop:
0001B2r 2  0A                           asl                                 ; Shift in 0 to LSb of result
0001B3r 2  EE rr rr                     inc             IOR_SPI_DATA
0001B6r 2  2C rr rr                     bit             IOR_SPI_DATA        ; MISO (bit 7) => N flag
0001B9r 2  10 01                        bpl             @spi_recv_2
0001BBr 2                                                                   ; Set LSb = 1
0001BBr 2  1A                           inc
0001BCr 2               @spi_recv_2:
0001BCr 2  88                           dey
0001BDr 2  D0 F3                        bne             @recv_loop
0001BFr 2  85 rr                        sta             ZP_SPI_DATA_IN
0001C1r 2  4C rr rr                     jmp             SPI_OPERATION_DONE
0001C4r 2               
0001C4r 2               ; Delay for some number of cycles to ensure SPI device is ready to start working
0001C4r 2               SPI_INIT_DELAY:
0001C4r 2  48                           pha
0001C5r 2  DA                           phx
0001C6r 2  5A                           phy
0001C7r 2  8A                           txa                                         ; set SPI device
0001C8r 2  09 06                        ora             #SPI_BIT_CSB | SPI_BIT_MOSI ; de-select all devices
0001CAr 2  AA                           tax
0001CBr 2  09 01                        ora             #SPI_BIT_CLK
0001CDr 2  A0 50                        ldy             #SPI_INIT_DELAY_CYCLES
0001CFr 2               @loop:
0001CFr 2  8D rr rr                     sta             IOR_SPI_DATA
0001D2r 2  8E rr rr                     stx             IOR_SPI_DATA
0001D5r 2  88                           dey
0001D6r 2  D0 F7                        bne             @loop
0001D8r 2  7A                           ply
0001D9r 2  FA                           plx
0001DAr 2  68                           pla
0001DBr 2  60                           rts
0001DCr 2               
0001DCr 2               ; Return (in A) the number of unread bytes in the circular input buffer as an unsigned byte
0001DCr 2               ; Modifies: flags, A
0001DCr 2               BUFFER_SIZE:
0001DCr 2  A5 rr                        lda             ZP_WRITE_PTR
0001DEr 2  38                           sec
0001DFr 2  E5 rr                        sbc             ZP_READ_PTR
0001E1r 2  60                           rts
0001E2r 2               
0001E2r 2               ; Maskable interrupt request handler; by default, do nothing
0001E2r 2               IRQ_HANDLER:
0001E2r 2  40                           rti
0001E3r 2               
0001E3r 2               
0001E3r 2               SERIAL_IRQ_HANDLER:
0001E3r 2  48                           pha
0001E4r 2               
0001E4r 2               .if ROCKWELL_ACIA = 1
0001E4r 2  A9 10                        lda             #ACIA_STATUS_BIT_TDRE
0001E6r 2               .endif
0001E6r 2               
0001E6r 2  2C rr rr                     bit             ACIA_R_STATUS
0001E9r 2  10 19                        bpl             @int_done 	            ; bit 7 not set, so not ACIA IRQ
0001EBr 2               
0001EBr 2               .if ROCKWELL_ACIA = 1
0001EBr 2  F0 0B                        beq             @do_recv                ; if not Tx, then must be Rx
0001EDr 2  A9 00                        lda             #SER_SEND_STATUS_READY
0001EFr 2  85 rr                        sta             ZP_SER_SEND_STATUS
0001F1r 2               
0001F1r 2               @check_recv:
0001F1r 2  A9 08                        lda             #ACIA_STATUS_BIT_RDRF   ; is read register full?
0001F3r 2  2C rr rr                     bit             ACIA_R_STATUS
0001F6r 2  F0 0C                        beq             @int_done
0001F8r 2               .endif
0001F8r 2               
0001F8r 2               @do_recv:
0001F8r 2  AD rr rr                     IO_PORT_READ    ACIA_R_DATA
0001FBr 2  DA                           phx
0001FCr 2  A6 rr                        ldx             ZP_WRITE_PTR
0001FEr 2  9D rr rr                     sta             INPUT_BUFFER,X
000201r 2  E6 rr                        inc             ZP_WRITE_PTR
000203r 2  FA                           plx
000204r 2               
000204r 2               @int_done:
000204r 2  68                           pla
000205r 2  40                           rti
000206r 2               
000206r 2               ;; *****************************************************************
000206r 2               .if 0
000206r 2               ; I2C
000206r 2               
000206r 2               I2C_SCL = $01
000206r 2               I2C_SDA = $02
000206r 2               I2C_CTRL_PORT = VIA_R_PORTA
000206r 2               I2C_DATA_PORT = VIA_R_DDRA
000206r 2               
000206r 2               .macro I2C_ON       val
000206r 2                           tay
000206r 2                           lda     #val
000206r 2                           ora     I2C_DATA_PORT
000206r 2                           sta     I2C_DATA_PORT
000206r 2                           tya
000206r 2               .endmacro
000206r 2               
000206r 2               .macro I2C_OFF      val
000206r 2                           tay
000206r 2                           lda     #~val
000206r 2                           and     I2C_DATA_PORT
000206r 2                           sta     I2C_DATA_PORT
000206r 2                           tya
000206r 2               .endmacro
000206r 2               
000206r 2               .macro SDA_LOW
000206r 2                           I2C_OFF I2C_SDA
000206r 2               .endmacro
000206r 2               
000206r 2               .macro SCL_LOW
000206r 2                           I2C_OFF I2C_SCL
000206r 2               .endmacro
000206r 2               
000206r 2               .macro SDA_HIGH
000206r 2                           I2C_ON  I2C_SDA
000206r 2               .endmacro
000206r 2               
000206r 2               .macro SCL_HIGH
000206r 2                           I2C_ON  I2C_SDA
000206r 2               .endmacro
000206r 2               
000206r 2               .macro SCL_PULSE
000206r 2                           inc     I2C_DATA_PORT
000206r 2                           dec     I2C_DATA_PORT
000206r 2               .endmacro
000206r 2               
000206r 2               ; A: Byte to send
000206r 2               ; Return (in A): 1 = SUCCESS, 0 = FAILURE
000206r 2               I2C_SEND:
000206r 2                           ldx     #$00
000206r 2                           stx     I2C_CTRL_PORT
000206r 2                           ldx     #$09
000206r 2               @loop:
000206r 2                           dex
000206r 2                           beq     @ack
000206r 2                           rol
000206r 2                           jsr     I2C_SEND_BIT
000206r 2                           bra    @loop
000206r 2               @ack:
000206r 2                           jsr     I2C_RECV_BIT    ; ack in A, 0 = success
000206r 2                           eor     #$01            ; return 1 on success, 0 on fail
000206r 2               @end:
000206r 2                           rts
000206r 2               
000206r 2               
000206r 2               I2C_RECV:   lda     #$00
000206r 2                           sta     I2C_CTRL_PORT
000206r 2                           pha
000206r 2                           ldx     #$09
000206r 2               @loop:      dex
000206r 2                           beq     @end
000206r 2                           jsr     rec_bit
000206r 2                           ror
000206r 2                           pla
000206r 2                           rol
000206r 2                           pha
000206r 2                           jmp     @loop
000206r 2               @end:
000206r 2                           pla
000206r 2                           rts
000206r 2               
000206r 2               ; A: Bit to send
000206r 2               I2C_SEND_BIT:
000206r 2                           bcc     @send_one
000206r 2                           SDA_LOW
000206r 2                           bra    @clock_out
000206r 2               @send_one:
000206r 2                           SDA_HIGH
000206r 2               
000206r 2               @clock_out:
000206r 2                           SCL_PULSE
000206r 2                           SDA_LOW
000206r 2                           rts
000206r 2               
000206r 2               I2C_RECV_BIT:
000206r 2                           SDA_HIGH
000206r 2                           SCL_HIGH
000206r 2                           lda     I2C_CTRL_PORT
000206r 2                           and     #I2C_SDA
000206r 2                           bne     @is_one
000206r 2                           lda     #$00
000206r 2                           jmp     @end
000206r 2               @is_one:
000206r 2                           lda     #$01
000206r 2               @end:
000206r 2                           SCL_LOW
000206r 2                           SDA_LOW
000206r 2                           rts
000206r 2               
000206r 2               
000206r 2               I2C_START:
000206r 2                           SDA_LOW
000206r 2                           SCL_LOW
000206r 2                           rts
000206r 2               
000206r 2               
000206r 2               I2C_STOP:
000206r 2                           SCL_HIGH
000206r 2                           SDA_HIGH
000206r 2                           rts
000206r 2               
000206r 2               
000206r 2               I2C_ACK:
000206r 2                           pha
000206r 2                           lda     #$00
000206r 2                           jsr     I2C_SEND_BIT
000206r 2                           pla
000206r 2                           rts
000206r 2               
000206r 2               I2C_NACK:
000206r 2                           pha
000206r 2                           lda     #$01
000206r 2                           jsr     I2C_SEND_BIT
000206r 2                           pla
000206r 2                           rts
000206r 2               .endif
000206r 2               
000206r 2               VIA_T2_INT_BIT = $20
000206r 2               VIA_T1_INT_BIT = $40
000206r 2               VIA_INT_ENABLE = $80
000206r 2               
000206r 2               
000206r 2               VIA_INIT:
000206r 2               .if ROCKWELL_ACIA <> 1 .AND ACIA_USE_VIA_TIMER = 1
000206r 2                           pha
000206r 2                           lda     #0
000206r 2                           sta     VIA_R_AUX_CTRL
000206r 2                           pla
000206r 2               .endif
000206r 2  60                       rts
000207r 2               
000207r 2               VIA_ENABLE_T1_INT:
000207r 2  A9 40                    lda     #VIA_T1_INT_BIT
000209r 2  09 80                    ora     #VIA_INT_ENABLE
00020Br 2  8D rr rr                 sta     VIA_R_INT_ENABLE
00020Er 2  60                       rts
00020Fr 2               
00020Fr 2               VIA_ENABLE_T2_INT:
00020Fr 2  A9 20                    lda     #VIA_T2_INT_BIT
000211r 2  09 80                    ora     #VIA_INT_ENABLE
000213r 2  8D rr rr                 sta     VIA_R_INT_ENABLE
000216r 2  60                       rts
000217r 2               
000217r 2               VIA_DISABLE_T1_INT:
000217r 2  48                       pha
000218r 2  A9 40                    lda     #VIA_T1_INT_BIT
00021Ar 2  8D rr rr                 sta     VIA_R_INT_ENABLE
00021Dr 2  68                       pla
00021Er 2  60                       rts
00021Fr 2               
00021Fr 2               VIA_DISABLE_T2_INT:
00021Fr 2  48                       pha
000220r 2  A9 20                    lda     #VIA_T2_INT_BIT
000222r 2  8D rr rr                 sta     VIA_R_INT_ENABLE
000225r 2  68                       pla
000226r 2  60                       rts
000227r 2               
000227r 2               ; .A.Y = Timer value
000227r 2               VIA_START_T2:
000227r 2  8D rr rr                 sta     VIA_R_T2C_L
00022Ar 2  8C rr rr                 sty     VIA_R_T2C_H
00022Dr 2  4C rr rr                 jmp     VIA_ENABLE_T2_INT
000230r 2               
000230r 2               VIA_STOP_T2:
000230r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp     VIA_DISABLE_T2_INT
000233r 2               
000233r 2               ; .A = Sub-component interrupt flag to test
000233r 2               VIA_IS_INT:
000233r 2  18                       clc
000234r 2  2D rr rr                 and     VIA_R_INT_FLAGS
000237r 2  F0 01                    beq     :+
000239r 2  38                       sec
00023Ar 2               :
00023Ar 2  60                       rts
00023Br 2               
00023Br 2               ; OUT: C = 1 if T2 timer set the IRQ, 0 if not
00023Br 2               VIA_IS_T1_INT:
00023Br 2  A9 40                    lda     #VIA_T1_INT_BIT
00023Dr 2  80 F4                    bra     VIA_IS_INT
00023Fr 2               
00023Fr 2               VIA_IS_T2_INT:
00023Fr 2  A9 20                    lda     #VIA_T2_INT_BIT
000241r 2  80 F0                    bra     VIA_IS_INT
000243r 2               
000243r 2               VIA_CLEAR_T1_INT:       ; READ LOB OF T1 Counter
000243r 2  AD rr rr                 lda     VIA_R_T1C_L
000246r 2  60                       rts
000247r 2               
000247r 2               VIA_CLEAR_T2_INT:       ; READ LOB OF T2 Counter
000247r 2  AD rr rr                 lda     VIA_R_T2C_L
00024Ar 2  60                       rts
00024Br 2               
00024Br 2               ; ****************************************************************************

00024Br 2               IRQ_VECTOR_INIT:
00024Br 2  78                       sei
00024Cr 2  38                       sec
00024Dr 2  48                       pha
00024Er 2  DA                       phx
00024Fr 2  5A                       phy
000250r 2  A2 0F                    ldx     #$F
000252r 2  A9 rr                    lda     #<SERIAL_IRQ_HANDLER
000254r 2  A0 rr                    ldy     #>SERIAL_IRQ_HANDLER
000256r 2               
000256r 2               @loop:
000256r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr     IRQ_SET_VEC1
000259r 2  CA                       dex
00025Ar 2  10 FA                    bpl     @loop
00025Cr 2               
00025Cr 2  A2 08                    ldx     #IRQ_NUMBER_SW
00025Er 2  A9 rr                    lda     #<SW_IRQ_HANDLER
000260r 2  A0 rr                    ldy     #>SW_IRQ_HANDLER
000262r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr     IRQ_SET_VEC1
000265r 2

00026Br 2  20 rr rr                 jsr     IRQ_SET_VEC1
00026Er 2               
00026Er 2  7A                       ply
00026Fr 2  FA                       plx
000270r 2  68                       pla
000271r 2  18                       clc
000272r 2  58                       cli
000273r 2  60                       rts
000274r 2               
000274r 2               ; X: IRQ#, .A.Y: Vector Addr
000274r 2               IRQ_SET_VECTOR:
000274r 2  78                       sei
000275r 2  48                       pha
000276r 2  AD F2 FF                 lda     V_REGISTER
000279r 2  85 rr                    sta     ZP_V_SAVE
00027Br 2  68                       pla
00027Cr 2               
00027Cr 2               IRQ_SET_VEC1:
00027Cr 2  8E F2 FF                 stx     V_REGISTER
00027Fr 2  8D FE FF                 sta     $FFFE
000282r 2  8C FF FF                 sty     $FFFF
000285r 2  90 01                    bcc     :+
000287r 2  60                       rts
000288r 2               :
000288r 2  A5 rr                    lda     ZP_V_SAVE
00028Ar 2  8D F2 FF                 sta     V_REGISTER
00028Dr 2  58                       cli
00028Er 2  60                       rts
00028Fr 2               
00028Fr 2               SW_IRQ_HANDLER:
00028Fr 2  48                       pha
000290r 2  4A                       lsr
000291r 2  4A                       lsr
000292r 2  4A                       lsr
000293r 2  4A                       lsr
000294r 2  68                       pla
000295r 2  40                       rti
000296r 2               
000296r 2               VIA_IRQ_HANDLER:
000296r 2               ; check which sub-device is triggering the IRQ
000296r 2  48                       pha
000297r 2  A9 20                    lda     #VIA_T2_INT_BIT
000299r 2  2D rr rr                 and     VIA_R_INT_FLAGS
00029Cr 2  F0 0E                    beq     :+
00029Er 2               .if ROCKWELL_ACIA = 1 .OR ACIA_USE_VIA_TIMER = 1
00029Er 2  A9 00                    lda     #SER_SEND_STATUS_READY
0002A0r 2  85 rr                    sta     ZP_SER_SEND_STATUS     ; signals serial send buffer is ready for another byte
0002A2r 2               .endif
0002A2r 2  AD rr rr                 lda     VIA_R_T2C_L             ; clear the interrupt
0002A5r 2  A9 20                    lda     #VIA_T2_INT_BIT         ; disable the T2 interrupt
0002A7r 2  8D rr rr                 sta     VIA_R_INT_ENABLE
0002AAr 2  80 0A                    bra     :++
0002ACr 2               
0002ACr 2               :
0002ACr 2  A9 20                    lda     #VIA_T2_INT_BIT
0002AEr 2  2D rr rr                 and     VIA_R_INT_FLAGS
0002B1r 2  F0 03                    beq     :+
0002B3r 2                                                           ; Do whatever T1 timer would do
0002B3r 2  AD rr rr                 lda     VIA_R_T1C_L             ; clear the interrupt
0002B6r 2               
0002B6r 2               :
0002B6r 2  68                       pla
0002B7r 2  40                       rti

0002BCr 2  4C rr rr                     jmp  MON_START

0000FAr 2               
0000FAr 2               .macro VECTORS
0000FAr 2                               .word   NMI_HANDLER     ; NMI vector
0000FAr 2                               .word   RESET_ENTRY     ; RESET vector
0000FAr 2                               .word   IRQ_HANDLER     ; IRQ vector
0000FAr 2                               ;       will actually be pulled from the Vector RAM, depending on lowest priority IRQ currently triggered,
0000FAr 2                               ;       or vector for IRQ# set in V[0..3] if none are triggered.  Can trigger S/W IRQs by setting V and then
0000FAr 2                               ;       calling BRK.  S/W IRQ# is $F, so setting V[0..3] to $F and v[4..7] to a different number, you can
0000FAr 2                               ;       have up to 16 unique S/W IRQs.  You can invoke a hardware device IRQ handler in the same way
0000FAr 2               .endmacro
0000FAr 2

0000E8r 2  54 37 5C 5C  
0000F0r 2  84 37 37 5C      .byte MN_beq, MN_sbc, MN_sbc, MN_nop, MN_nop, MN_sbc, MN_inc, MN_smb, MN_sed, MN_sbc, MN_plx, MN_nop, MN_nop, MN_sbc, MN_inc, MN_bbs
0000F4r 2  5C 37 17 8E  
0000F8r 2  02 37 46 5C  
000100r 2               
000100r 2               ; 0..3 = even indexes, 4..7 = odd
000100r 2               ; x000: one byte
000100r 2               ; xxx1: two byte
000100r 2               ; xxx0 (!x000): three byte
000100r 2               
000100r 2               ;x1xx = Indexed
000100r 2               ;   x10x = Indexed by X
000100r 2               ;   x11x (!1110) = Indexed by Y
000100r 2               ;1xxx = Indirect (!100x && !1110)
000100r 2               
000100r 2               ; INDEXED SUFFIXES
000100r 2               ; 010x == ,X
000100r 2               ; 110x == ,X)
000100r 2               ; 011x == ,Y
000100r 2               ; 1111 == ),Y
000100r 2               
000100r 2               AM_ACC   := 0     ;                     %0000
000100r 2               AM_REL   := 1     ; $rr => $aaaa        %0001
000100r 2               AM_ZPREL := 2     ; $zz,$rr => $aaaa    %0010
000100r 2               AM_ZP    := 3     ; $zz                 %0011
000100r 2               AM_ABSX  := 4     ; $aaaa,X             %0100
000100r 2               AM_ZPX   := 5     ; $zz,X               %0101
000100r 2               AM_ABSY  := 6     ; $aaaa,Y             %0110
000100r 2               AM_ZPY   := 7     ; $zz,Y               %0111
000100r 2               AM_IMP   := 8     ;                     %1000
000100r 2               AM_IMM   := 9     ; #$ii                %1001
000100r 2               AM_IND   := $A    ; ($aaaa)             %1010
000100r 2               AM_ZPIND := $B    ; ($zz)               %1011
000100r 2               AM_ABSIX := $C    ; ($aaaa,X)           %1100
000100r 2               AM_ZPIX  := $D    ; ($zz,X)             %1101
000100r 2               AM_ABS   := $E    ; $aaaa               %1110
000100r 2               AM_ZPIY  := $F    ; ($zz),Y             %1111
000100r 2               
000100r 2               ; ADDRESSING MODES
000100r 2               ; Even Opcode Modes (%xxxxyyz0), xxxx = row, yy = column, z = 0 (low nibble), z = 1 (hi nibble)
000100r 2               MN_AMODE_EVEN:
000100r 2  88 33 08 EE      .byte $88,$33,$08,$EE	; %0000yyz0
000104r 2  B1 53 08 4E      .byte $B1,$53,$08,$4E	; %0001yyz0
000108r 2  8E 33 08 EE      .byte $8E,$33,$08,$EE	; %0010yyz0
00010Cr 2  B1 55 08 44      .byte $B1,$55,$08,$44	; %0011yyz0
000110r 2  88 38 08 EE      .byte $88,$38,$08,$EE	; %0100yyz0
000114r 2  B1 58 88 48      .byte $B1,$58,$88,$48	; %0101yyz0
000118r 2  88 33 08 EA      .byte $88,$33,$08,$EA	; %0110yyz0
00011Cr 2  B1 55 88 4C      .byte $B1,$55,$88,$4C	; %0111yyz0
000120r 2  81 33 88 EE      .byte $81,$33,$88,$EE	; %1000yyz0
000124r 2  B1 75 88 4E      .byte $B1,$75,$88,$4E	; %1001yyz0
000128r 2  99 33 88 EE      .byte $99,$33,$88,$EE	; %1010yyz0
00012Cr 2  B1 75 88 64      .byte $B1,$75,$88,$64	; %1011yyz0
000130r 2  89 33 88 EE      .byte $89,$33,$88,$EE	; %1100yyz0
000134r 2  B1 58 88 48      .byte $B1,$58,$88,$48	; %1101yyz0
000138r 2  89 33 88 EE      .byte $89,$33,$88,$EE	; %1110yyz0
00013Cr 2  B1 58 88 48      .byte $B1,$58,$88,$48	; %1111yyz0
000140r 2               
000140r 2               ; ODD Opcode Modes (%xxxayyz1), a = row, yy = column, z = 0 (low nibble), z = 1 (hi nibble)
000140r 2  8D 33 89 2E      .byte $8D,$33,$89,$2E	; %xxx0yyz1
000144r 2  8F 35 86 24      .byte $8F,$35,$86,$24	; %xxx1yyz1
000148r 2               
000148r 2               MNEMONIC_STR:
000148r 2  54 58 53 45      .byte "TXSEDEXTRBVSEIBRASLDYWAINCMPLYLSROLDANDEYPLADCLCLDXRORTSBCSTYATSXSTXABPLXSTAXJSRTIBMINXBITAYNOPHXBVCPYBBSTZBNEORABCCPXDECLVSTPHPHARMBEQSECLINYSMBBRKJMPLPHY"
00014Cr 2  44 45 58 54  
000150r 2  52 42 56 53  
0001E3r 2               DA_DATAEND:
0001E3r 2  05 20 3D 3E  NamedHString HS_RelPrefix, " => $"
0001E7r 2  20 24        
0001E9r 2               
0001E9r 2               .segment "DISASM_CODE"
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; ZP_D_XAM, ZP_D_XAM+1: Address to Disassemble
000000r 2               ; set ZP_D_STATE=1 to print disassembly, ZP_D_STATE=0 to print bytes
000000r 2               
000000r 2               DISASM_WM:
000000r 2  A5 rr            lda         ZP_WM_XAM + 1
000002r 2  85 rr            sta         ZP_D_XAM + 1
000004r 2  A5 rr            lda         ZP_WM_XAM
000006r 2  85 rr            sta         ZP_D_XAM
000008r 2               
000008r 2               DISASM:
000008r 2  20 rr rr         jsr         _disasm_load_inst
00000Br 2  20 rr rr         jsr         _disasm_print_inst_bytes
00000Er 2  A5 rr            lda         ZP_D_STATE
000010r 2  D0 03            bne         :+
000012r 2  4C rr rr         jmp         @end_of_print
000015r 2               :
000015r 2  A0 00            ldy         #0
000017r 2  20 rr rr         jsr         _disasm_print_padding
00001Ar 2  A9 20            lda   #$20                       ;
00001Cr 2  20 rr rr         jsr   WRITE_CHAR                 ; extra space and separator
00001Fr 2  A9 7C            lda   #'|'                       ; added for readability
000021r 2  20 rr rr         jsr   WRITE_CHAR                 ;
000024r 2  A9 20            lda   #$20
000026r 2  20 rr rr         jsr   WRITE_CHAR
000029r 2  A6 rr            ldx         ZP_D_INST            ; look up opcode in table.
00002Br 2  BD rr rr         lda         MN_OFFSETS, x        ; cool.  Can do in reverse for assembler
00002Er 2  AA               tax
00002Fr 2  BD rr rr         lda   MNEMONIC_STR, x            ; print mnemonic
000032r 2  20 rr rr         jsr   WRITE_CHAR
000035r 2  BD rr rr         lda   MNEMONIC_STR+1, x
000038r 2  20 rr rr         jsr   WRITE_CHAR
00003Br 2  BD rr rr         lda   MNEMONIC_STR+2, x
00003Er 2  20 rr rr         jsr   WRITE_CHAR
000041r 2  A5 rr            lda         ZP_D_EXBYTES
000043r 2  D0 03            bne         :+
000045r 2  4C rr rr         jmp         @end_of_print               ; one-byte inst prints only mnemonic
000048r 2               :
000048r 2  A5 rr            lda         ZP_D_INST
00004Ar 2  AA               tax
00004Br 2  29 07            and         #7
00004Dr 2  C9 07            cmp         #7                          ; BBR, BBS, RMB, SMB
00004Fr 2  D0 0C            bne         @ex_space
000051r 2  8A               txa
000052r 2  4A               lsr
000053r 2  4A               lsr
000054r 2  4A               lsr
000055r 2  4A               lsr
000056r 2  29 07            and         #7
000058r 2  20 rr rr         jsr   WRITE_HEX
00005Br 2  80 05            bra         @skip_ex_space              ; skip one space
00005Dr 2               
00005Dr 2               @ex_space:
00005Dr 2  A9 20            lda   #$20
00005Fr 2  20 rr rr         jsr   WRITE_CHAR
000062r 2               
000062r 2               @skip_ex_space:
000062r 2  A9 20            lda   #$20
000064r 2  20 rr rr         jsr   WRITE_CHAR
000067r 2  A6 rr            ldx         ZP_D_MODE
000069r 2  E0 0A            cpx         #AM_IND                     ; indirect
00006Br 2  90 09            bcc         @not_indirect
00006Dr 2  E0 0E            cpx         #AM_ABS                     ; but not absolute
00006Fr 2  F0 05            beq         @not_indirect
000071r 2  A9 28            lda   #'('
000073r 2  20 rr rr         jsr   WRITE_CHAR
000076r 2               
000076r 2               @not_indirect:
000076r 2  E0 09            cpx         #AM_IMM
000078r 2  D0 06            bne         @not_immediate
00007Ar 2  20 rr rr         jsr         _disasm_immediate
00007Dr 2  4C rr rr         jmp         @end_of_print
000080r 2               
000080r 2               @not_immediate:
000080r 2  E0 03            cpx         #AM_ZP                      ; relative?
000082r 2  B0 37            bcs         @not_relative
000084r 2  20 rr rr         jsr         _disasm_zeropage
000087r 2  8A               txa
000088r 2  4A               lsr                                     ; shift LOb to C
000089r 2  B0 0D            bcs         @not_zprel                  ; AM_REL = 1, AM_ZPREL = 2, so skip ZP part if LOb is 1
00008Br 2  A9 2C            lda   #','
00008Dr 2  20 rr rr         jsr   WRITE_CHAR
000090r 2  A9 20            lda   #$20
000092r 2  20 rr rr         jsr   WRITE_CHAR
000095r 2  20 rr rr         jsr         _disasm_zeropage
000098r 2               
000098r 2               @not_zprel:
000098r 2  A9 rr            lda             #<HS_RelPrefix
00009Ar 2  A0 rr            ldy             #>HS_RelPrefix
00009Cr 2  20 rr rr         jsr   WRITE_HSTRING
00009Fr 2  18               clc
0000A0r 2  A4 rr            ldy         ZP_D_EXBYTES
0000A2r 2  64 rr            stz         ZP_TEMP
0000A4r 2  B9 rr rr         lda         ZP_D_INST, y
0000A7r 2  10 02            bpl         @skip_ff_set
0000A9r 2  C6 rr            dec         ZP_TEMP                     ; $FF => ZP_TEMP
0000ABr 2               
0000ABr 2               @skip_ff_set:
0000ABr 2  65 rr            adc         ZP_D_XAM
0000ADr 2  AA               tax
0000AEr 2  A5 rr            lda         ZP_D_XAM + 1
0000B0r 2  65 rr            adc         ZP_TEMP
0000B2r 2  20 rr rr         jsr   WRITE_BYTE
0000B5r 2  8A               txa
0000B6r 2  20 rr rr         jsr   WRITE_BYTE
0000B9r 2  80 31            bra         @end_of_print
0000BBr 2               
0000BBr 2               @not_relative:
0000BBr 2  A5 rr            lda         ZP_D_EXBYTES
0000BDr 2  C9 01            cmp         #1
0000BFr 2  F0 05            beq         @two_byte_inst              ; inst is two bytes (vs three)?
0000C1r 2  20 rr rr         jsr         _disasm_absolute
0000C4r 2  80 03            bra         @continue_multibyte
0000C6r 2               
0000C6r 2               @two_byte_inst:
0000C6r 2  20 rr rr         jsr         _disasm_zeropage
0000C9r 2               
0000C9r 2               @continue_multibyte:
0000C9r 2  E0 0E            cpx         #AM_ABS                     ; absolute?
0000CBr 2  F0 1F            beq         @end_of_print
0000CDr 2  E0 0F            cpx         #AM_ZPIY
0000CFr 2  D0 05            bne         @not_zpiy
0000D1r 2  A9 29            lda   #')'
0000D3r 2  20 rr rr         jsr   WRITE_CHAR
0000D6r 2               
0000D6r 2               @not_zpiy:
0000D6r 2  2F rr 06         bbr2        ZP_D_MODE, @not_indexed     ; indexed?
0000D9r 2  8A               txa
0000DAr 2  4A               lsr
0000DBr 2  4A               lsr                                     ; shift LOb to C
0000DCr 2  20 rr rr         jsr         _disasm_commaXY
0000DFr 2               
0000DFr 2               @not_indexed:
0000DFr 2  E0 0A            cpx         #AM_IND
0000E1r 2  30 09            bmi         @end_of_print
0000E3r 2  E0 0E            cpx         #AM_ABS
0000E5r 2  B0 05            bcs         @end_of_print               ; AM_ABS or AM_ZPIY?  Skip trailing RPAREN
0000E7r 2  A9 29            lda   #')'
0000E9r 2  20 rr rr         jsr   WRITE_CHAR
0000ECr 2               
0000ECr 2               @end_of_print:
0000ECr 2  60               rts
0000EDr 2               
0000EDr 2               ;---------------------------------------
0000EDr 2               ; Helper procedures
0000EDr 2               
0000EDr 2               _disasm_load_inst:
0000EDr 2  A0 00            ldy         #0
0000EFr 2  A6 rr            ldx         ZP_D_STATE
0000F1r 2  F0 22            beq         @done                       ; if not in DISASM mode, just print one byte
0000F3r 2  B2 rr            lda         (ZP_D_XAM)
0000F5r 2  4A               lsr                                     ; /2 and shift LOb to C
0000F6r 2  90 04            bcc         @shift_mode                 ; is even bytecode?
0000F8r 2  29 0F            and         #$0F
0000FAr 2  09 80            ora         #$80
0000FCr 2               
0000FCr 2               @shift_mode:
0000FCr 2  4A               lsr
0000FDr 2  AA               tax
0000FEr 2  BD rr rr         lda         MN_AMODE_EVEN, x
000101r 2  90 04            bcc         @no_shift
000103r 2  4A               lsr
000104r 2  4A               lsr
000105r 2  4A               lsr
000106r 2  4A               lsr
000107r 2               
000107r 2               @no_shift:
000107r 2  29 0F            and         #$0F
000109r 2  85 rr            sta         ZP_D_MODE
00010Br 2  29 07            and         #7                          ; test if bits 0..2 are zeros (one-byte inst)
00010Dr 2  F0 06            beq         @done
00010Fr 2  C8               iny
000110r 2  29 01            and         #1
000112r 2  D0 01            bne         @done                       ; odd modes are 2-byte inst
000114r 2  C8               iny
000115r 2               
000115r 2               @done:
000115r 2  84 rr            sty         ZP_D_EXBYTES
000117r 2  A0 00            ldy         #0
000119r 2               
000119r 2               :
000119r 2  B2 rr            lda         (ZP_D_XAM)
00011Br 2  99 rr rr         sta         ZP_D_INST, y
00011Er 2  E6 rr            inc         ZP_D_XAM
000120r 2  D0 02            bne         :+
000122r 2  E6 rr            inc         ZP_D_XAM + 1
000124r 2               
000124r 2               :
000124r 2  C4 rr            cpy         ZP_D_EXBYTES
000126r 2  F0 03            beq         :+
000128r 2  C8               iny
000129r 2  80 EE            bra         :--
00012Br 2               
00012Br 2               :
00012Br 2  60               rts
00012Cr 2               
00012Cr 2               ;AM_ACC   := 0     ;                     %0000
00012Cr 2               ;AM_REL   := 1     ; $rr => $aaaa        %0001
00012Cr 2               ;AM_ZPREL := 2     ; $zz,$rr => $aaaa    %0010
00012Cr 2               ;AM_ZP    := 3     ; $zz                 %0011
00012Cr 2               ;AM_ABSX  := 4     ; $aaaa,X             %0100
00012Cr 2               ;AM_ZPX   := 5     ; $zz,X               %0101
00012Cr 2               ;AM_ABSY  := 6     ; $aaaa,Y             %0110
00012Cr 2               ;AM_ZPY   := 7     ; $zz,Y               %0111
00012Cr 2               ;AM_IMP   := 8     ;                     %1000
00012Cr 2               ;AM_IMM   := 9     ; #$ii                %1001
00012Cr 2               ;AM_IND   := $A    ; ($aaaa)             %1010
00012Cr 2               ;AM_ZPIND := $B    ; ($zz)               %1011
00012Cr 2               ;AM_ABSIX := $C    ; ($aaaa,X)           %1100
00012Cr 2               ;AM_ZPIX  := $D    ; ($zz,X)             %1101
00012Cr 2               ;AM_ABS   := $E    ; $aaaa               %1110
00012Cr 2               ;AM_ZPIY  := $F    ; ($zz),Y             %1111
00012Cr 2               
00012Cr 2               _disasm_print_inst_bytes:
00012Cr 2  A0 00            ldy         #0
00012Er 2  80 01            bra         :++
000130r 2               
000130r 2               :
000130r 2  C8               iny
000131r 2               
000131r 2               :
000131r 2  A9 20 20 rr      PRINT_SPACE
000135r 2  rr           
000136r 2  B9 rr rr 20      PRINT_BYTE  {ZP_D_INST, y}
00013Ar 2  rr rr        
00013Cr 2  C4 rr            cpy         ZP_D_EXBYTES
00013Er 2  90 F0            bcc         :--
000140r 2  60               rts
000141r 2               
000141r 2               _disasm_print_padding:
000141r 2  A5 rr            lda         ZP_D_EXBYTES
000143r 2  C9 01            cmp         #1
000145r 2  F0 04            beq         :+
000147r 2  49 02            eor         #2
000149r 2  F0 0F            beq         :+++
00014Br 2               
00014Br 2               :
00014Br 2  AA               tax
00014Cr 2               
00014Cr 2               :
00014Cr 2  A9 20 20 rr      PRINT_SPACE
000150r 2  rr           
000151r 2  20 rr rr         PRINT_CHAR
000154r 2  20 rr rr         PRINT_CHAR
000157r 2  CA               dex
000158r 2  D0 F2            bne         :-
00015Ar 2               
00015Ar 2               :
00015Ar 2  60               rts
00015Br 2               
00015Br 2               ; Carry: 1 = Y, Carry: 0 = X
00015Br 2               _disasm_commaXY:
00015Br 2  A9 2C 20 rr      PRINT_CHAR  #','
00015Fr 2  rr           
000160r 2  A9 58            lda         #'X'
000162r 2  90 01            bcc         :+
000164r 2  1A               inc
000165r 2               
000165r 2               :
000165r 2  4C rr rr         PRINT_CHAR_JMP
000168r 2               
000168r 2               _disasm_absolute:
000168r 2  C8               iny
000169r 2  20 rr rr         jsr         _disasm_zeropage
00016Cr 2  88               dey
00016Dr 2  80 0B            bra         _disasm_zp_byte
00016Fr 2               
00016Fr 2               _disasm_immediate:
00016Fr 2  A9 23 20 rr      PRINT_CHAR  #'#'
000173r 2  rr           
000174r 2                   ; fall through
000174r 2               
000174r 2               _disasm_zeropage:
000174r 2  C8               iny
000175r 2  A9 24 20 rr      PRINT_CHAR  #'$'
000179r 2  rr           
00017Ar 2               
00017Ar 2               _disasm_zp_byte:
00017Ar 2  B9 rr rr 4C      PRINT_BYTE_JMP  {ZP_D_INST, y}
00017Er 2  rr rr        
000180r 2               ;

000180r 1               .include "hyforth/hyforth.s"
000180r 2               ;----------------------------------------------------------------------
000180r 2               ;
000180r 2               ;  Patrick Struthers - March 2026
000180r 2               ;      - THANKS to AGSB for the starting point here....
000180r 2               ;
000180r 2               ;  The HyForth project starts here with AGSB's core Forth engine;
000180r 2               ;  the engine now resides in ROM (as of 5/1/26) and the heap is copied to RAM
000180r 2               ;  on cold start.
000180r 2               ;
000180r 2               ;  The ulitmate purpose of working this port through is to develop a
000180r 2               ;  flexible and powerful operating system for the Hydra-16 of reasonable
000180r 2               ;  efficiency and minimal memory footprint.  Eventually, HyForth will be
000180r 2               ;  able to grow and shrink in RAM footprint according to context.
000180r 2               ;
000180r 2               ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------
000180r 2               ;
000180r 2               .debuginfo
000180r 2               .setcpu "65C02"
000180r 2               
000180r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000180r 2               ; macros for dictionary, creates code as follows:
000180r 2               ;
000180r 2               ;   h_name:
000180r 2               ;   .word  link_to_previous_entry
000180r 2               ;   .byte  strlen(name) + flags
000180r 2               ;   .byte  name
000180r 2               ;   name:
000180r 2               ;
000180r 2               ; label for primitives
000180r 2               ;
000180r 2               .macro makelabel arg1, arg2
000180r 2               .ident (.concat (arg1, arg2)):
000180r 2               .endmacro
000180r 2               ;
000180r 2               ; header for primitives
000180r 2               ; the entry point for dictionary is h_~name~
000180r 2               ; the entry point for code is ~name~
000180r 2               ;
000180r 2               .macro def_word name, label, flag
000180r 2               makelabel "h_", label
000180r 2               .ident(.sprintf("H%04X", hcount + 1)):
000180r 2                 .word .ident (.sprintf ("H%04X", hcount))
000180r 2               hcount .set hcount + 1
000180r 2                 .byte .strlen(name) + flag + 0 ; nice trick !
000180r 2                 .byte name
000180r 2               makelabel "", label
000180r 2               .endmacro
000180r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000180r 2               ;  macros for PGS stuff
000180r 2               ;
000180r 2               .macro WCRLF_np                ; no push of A
000180r 2                      lda #$0D
000180r 2                      jsr WRITE_CHAR
000180r 2                      lda #$0A
000180r 2                      jsr WRITE_CHAR
000180r 2               .endmacro
000180r 2               
000180r 2               .macro WCRLF
000180r 2                      pha
000180r 2                      lda #$0D
000180r 2                      jsr WRITE_CHAR
000180r 2                      lda #$0A
000180r 2                      jsr WRITE_CHAR
000180r 2                      pla
000180r 2               .endmacro
000180r 2               
000180r 2               .macro  WSEQ_np  strlbl
000180r 2                    phy
000180r 2                    ldy #0
000180r 2               :
000180r 2                    lda strlbl, y
000180r 2                    beq :+
000180r 2                    iny
000180r 2                    jsr WRITE_CHAR
000180r 2                    bra :-
000180r 2               :
000180r 2                    WCRLF_np
000180r 2                    ply
000180r 2               .endmacro
000180r 2               
000180r 2               
000180r 2               .macro  WSEQ_raw  strlbl
000180r 2                    phy
000180r 2                    ldy #0
000180r 2               :
000180r 2                    lda strlbl, y
000180r 2                    beq :+
000180r 2                    iny
000180r 2                    jsr WRITE_CHAR
000180r 2                    bra :-
000180r 2               :
000180r 2                    ply
000180r 2               .endmacro
000180r 2               
000180r 2               .macro WSEQ  strlbl
000180r 2                     pha
000180r 2                     WSEQ_np  strlbl
000180r 2                     pla
000180r 2               .endmacro
