000180r 2               ;  for error messages
000180r 2               ;
000180r 2               .macro ERR_entry errmsg
000180r 2                   .byte <errmsg
000180r 2                   .byte >errmsg
000180r 2                   emcount .set emcount + 1
000180r 2               .endmacro
000180r 2               
000180r 2               .macro WERR errptr
000180r 2               ;.macro WERR
000180r 2                   .local werrloop, werrend
000180r 2                   WCRLF_np
000180r 2                   ldy #0
000180r 2                   lda (errptr),y
000180r 2                   sta TEMP0
000180r 2                   iny
000180r 2                   lda (errptr),y
000180r 2                   sta TEMP0+1
000180r 2                   ldy #0
000180r 2               werrloop:
000180r 2                   lda (TEMP0),y
000180r 2                   beq werrend
000180r 2                   iny
000180r 2                   jsr WRITE_CHAR
000180r 2                   bra werrloop
000180r 2               werrend:
000180r 2                   WCRLF_np
000180r 2                   rts
000180r 2               .endmacro
000180r 2               ;
000180r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000180r 2               ;              library list  - handle like an error/token jumptable
000180r 2               ;
000180r 2               ;                --- this is also how MS-BASIC knows where to send
000180r 2               ;   the code when a tokenized command is invoked.  Inherent limitation
000180r 2               ;   with one-byte tokens (for errors or libraries) is max of 255 entries.
000180r 2               ;
000180r 2               .macro LIBENTRY libname
000180r 2                   .byte <libname
000180r 2                   .byte >libname
000180r 2                   libcount .set libcount + 1
000180r 2               .endmacro
000180r 2               ;
000180r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000180r 2               ; variables for macros
000180r 2               
000180r 2               hcount .set 0
000180r 2               emcount .set 0             ; # of error messages set up
000180r 2               libcount .set 0
000180r 2               
000180r 2               H0000 = 0
000180r 2               
000180r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000180r 2               ;               CONFIGURATION OPTIONS
000180r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000180r 2               
000180r 2               ; number conversion
000180r 2               numbers := 1      ; include DEC/BIN/HEX conversion
000180r 2               
000180r 2               SINGLE := 1     ; single digits hard coded?
000180r 2               
000180r 2               DEBUG := 1        ; enable inclusion of debug code
000180r 2               
000180r 2               HYWORDS := 1      ; add in additional hardcoded words / logic
000180r 2               
000180r 2               ANSIOK := 1         ; add ANSI screen stuff
000180r 2               
000180r 2               YSOUND := 1        ; add sound support
000180r 2               
000180r 2               ;TXT2STACK := 1     ; use old TXTGET instead of new
000180r 2               
000180r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000180r 2               ;              for PGS hyforth stuff
000180r 2               ;
000180r 2               ; error codes
000180r 2               ;
000180r 2               ERR_RPTR := $01    ; return stack pointer error
000180r 2               ERR_SPTR := $02    ; data stack pointer error
000180r 2               ERR_DIV0 := $03    ; divide by zero
000180r 2               ERR_MEM := $04     ; memory not-avail
000180r 2               ERR_UKW := $05     ; unknown word
000180r 2               ERR_SEC := $06     ; security error, ie dangerous address write
000180r 2               ERR_SYS := $07     ; return from system call error
000180r 2               ERR_PAD := $08
000180r 2                 ; warnings
000180r 2               WRN_SEC := $86     ; security
000180r 2               WRN_MEM := $84     ; memory alloc
000180r 2               ;
000180r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000180r 2               ;                 CORE ENGINE CONSTANTS
000180r 2               ;
000180r 2               CELL = 2         ; cell size, two bytes, 16-bit
000180r 2               FLAG_IMM = 1<<7  ; immediate flag
000180r 2               FLAG_COM = 1<<6  ; compiled flag
000180r 2               MAXSTR = 100
000180r 2               
000180r 2               ; terminal input buffer, forward
000180r 2               ; getline, token, skip, scan, depends on page boundary
000180r 2               ; INBUF = $0400  (see segment STACKS below)
000180r 2               ; moves forwards
000180r 2               INBUF_end = $FD
000180r 2               
000180r 2               ; data stacks
000180r 2               ; moves backwards, push decreases before copy
000180r 2               DSEND = $7E
000180r 2               
000180r 2               ; return stack
000180r 2               ; moves backwards, push decreases before copy
000180r 2               RTEND = $FE
000180r 2               
000180r 2               ; malloc stack and managed memory area
000180r 2               ; both move downwards like other stacks
000180r 2               ;
000180r 2               MEMEND = $01FE            ; pointers DOWN from MEMEND+MEMSTK (store-and-decrement)
000180r 2               MEMTOP = $7BF0            ; malloc allocates DOWN from MEMTOP
000180r 2               SCRBUF = $7C00            ; screen buffer 1K going up
000180r 2               MSBUF = $600             ; note, allocated at assembly, may change
000180r 2               PADBUF = $800            ; note, allocated at assembly, may change
000180r 2               
000180r 2               PADEND = $01FE            ; PAD+PADEND, up with PADPTR - increment and store
000180r 2               ;
000180r 2               ;   types:  <type> <len0 len1> <data data data...>
000180r 2               ;
000180r 2               MEM_SCR = $01  ; screen  (1024 bytes, with 63 char rows terminated either $09 or $00
000180r 2               MEM_SZ = $04   ; zero-termed string
000180r 2               MEM_FRAC = $06 ; 'fractional binary' <type> <03 00> <offset> <xxxx>
000180r 2               MEM_TAB = $07  ; table
000180r 2               ;
000180r 2               ; spi
000180r 2               ;F9E8 SPI_INIT_DELAY
000180r 2               ;F9CC SPI_RECV
000180r 2               ;F9AD SPI_SEND
000180r 2               ;F9A3 SPI_OPERATION_DONE
000180r 2               ;F974 SPI_TRANSCEIVE
000180r 2               
000180r 2               INROM = $A000
000180r 2               
000180r 2               ;----------------------------------------------------------------------
000180r 2               ;       Look closely at hyforth.cfg and the output of ca65/ld65 after
000180r 2               ;  a build; the RAM and ROM code is carefully arranged when the binary
000180r 2               ;  is created, to make initialization easier and optimize RAM use.
000180r 2               ;  The main program engine starts at $EA00 in OS ROM and is only about 500 bytes
000180r 2               ;  long.  Additional functions, then initialization and support code follow.
000180r 2               ;  The heap or 'dictionary' primitives are stored on a banked ROM, mapped
000180r 2               ;  to $A000, along with some meta-compiled words following the primitives.
000180r 2               ;
000180r 2               ;       Currently, the primitives are loaded with 'COPYTORAM' (at $A000)
000180r 2               ;  using parameters for the size and location of the heap stored at $A003,4,5.
000180r 2               ;
000180r 2               ;       The dictionary has a particular structure, with 'bye' and 'abort'
000180r 2               ;  at the beginning; there are some core words, then contents of 'primitives.s',
000180r 2               ; then 'hywords.s', followed by the end of the dictionary with critical items
000180r 2               ; such as 'fetch', 'store', 'immediate', 'compile, 'semis', 'exit, and ancillary
000180r 2               ; stuff.  Any sub-routines that are called by multiple words (and therefore need set
000180r 2               ; addresses) appear in the 'utes.s' or 'upper.s' files.  These are considered
000180r 2               ; 'support code' and reside in OS ROM following the basic HyForth engine.
000180r 2               ;
000180r 2               ;       HyForth is started by running 'A00R' or 'EC00R' from Wozmon;
000180r 2               ;  the basic functions can be supplemented with the meta-compiled words, or
000180r 2               ;  'libraries' as well as runtime-loaded binaries and other items.  Libraries
000180r 2               ;  are loaded with 'autoload' or 'bload' (compiled or binary).
000180r 2               ;
000180r 2               ;----------------------------------------------------------------------
000180r 2               ;                   ZERO PAGE USAGE
000180r 2               ;----------------------------------------------------------------------
000180r 2               .segment "ZEROPAGE"
00002Dr 2               ;
00002Dr 2               .org $A0
0000A0  2               ZPSTART:
0000A0  2                                ;   16 bytes for PAD and clipboard
0000A0  2                                ;
0000A0  2  xx xx        PADPTR:     .res 2             ; pointer in stack               $A0
0000A2  2  xx xx xx xx  .res 14                        ; reserved fer later
0000A6  2  xx xx xx xx  
0000AA  2  xx xx xx xx  
0000B0  2               ;
0000B0  2               ;                 Utilities, some BIOS crossover
0000B0  2               ;
0000B0  2  xx xx        Z_DEBUG:    .res 2             ; dedicated ZP temp for debugger  $B0
0000B2  2               ;
0000B2  2  xx xx        Z_MATH1:    .res 2             ; ZP temp for math routines   $B2  (*, /, */, felin)
0000B4  2  xx xx        Z_MATH2:    .res 2             ; ZP temp for math routines   $B4  (*, /, */, felin)
0000B6  2  xx xx        Z_MATH3:    .res 2             ; ZP temp for math routines   $B6  (*, /, */, felin)
0000B8  2  xx xx        Z_MATH4:    .res 2             ; ZP temp for math/ANSI       $B8  (HEX2DIG, */)
0000BA  2               ;
0000BA  2  xx xx        Z_ANSI1:    .res 2             ; ZP temp for ANSI            $BA
0000BC  2  xx xx        Z_ANSI2:    .res 2             ; ZP temp for ANSI            $BC
0000BE  2  xx xx        Z_ANSI3:    .res 2             ; ZP temp for ANSI            $BE
0000C0  2               ;
0000C0  2               ;                  HyFedit specific
0000C0  2               ;
0000C0  2  xx xx        HFE_BPTR:   .res 2             ; pointer into 1K buffer      $C0
0000C2  2  xx           HFE_CH:     .res 1             ; cursor horizontal (columns) $C2
0000C3  2  xx           HFE_CV:     .res 1             ; cursor vertical (rows)      $C3
0000C4  2  xx           HFE_T0:     .res 1             ; reserved                    $C4
0000C5  2  xx           HFE_T0H:    .res 1             ; reserved                    $C5
0000C6  2  xx           HFE_T1:     .res 1             ; reserved                    $C6
0000C7  2  xx           HFE_T1H:    .res 1             ; reserved                    $C7
0000C8  2               ;HFE_T2 is at $FE/$FF  (TEMP7)
0000C8  2               ;
0000C8  2               ;                   HyForth setup stuff
0000C8  2               ;
0000C8  2               TEMP0:                    ; DUMPREG, lib/libs    $C8
0000C8  2  xx xx           .res 2
0000CA  2               TEMP8:                        ;  cons and pick   $CA
0000CA  2  xx xx           .res 2
0000CC  2               TEMP9:                        ;   Imm            $CC
0000CC  2  xx xx           .res 2
0000CE  2               MEMPTR:                       ;   malloc         $CE
0000CE  2  xx xx           .res 2
0000D0  2               MEMLAST:
0000D0  2  xx xx           .res 2                      ;  malloc         $D0
0000D2  2               TIB:
0000D2  2  xx xx           .res 2          ; pointer to input buffer     $D2
0000D4  2               TIBEND:
0000D4  2  xx xx           .res 2          ; pointer to end of TIB       $D4
0000D6  2               DFLAG:
0000D6  2  xx              .res 1          ; debug flag                  $D6
0000D7  2               ERRFLAG:
0000D7  2  xx              .res 1          ; error type, 0 = none        $D7
0000D8  2               ERRPTR:
0000D8  2  xx xx           .res 2          ; ptr to mitigation/message   $D8
0000DA  2               DIGBASE:
0000DA  2  xx              .res 1          ; base for number conversion  $DA
0000DB  2               RSEED:
0000DB  2  xx xx xx xx     .res 4          ; random # seed               $DB
0000DF  2               ALFLAG:            ; autoload flag               $DF
0000DF  2  xx              .res 1
0000E0  2               ;
0000E0  2               ;                   internal Forth
0000E0  2               ;   NOTE:  do NOT change the order of the first eight words;
0000E0  2               ;     AGSB's 'training' words assume that STATUS -> WORKREG
0000E0  2               ;     are consecutive and in below sequence.
0000E0  2               ;
0000E0  2  00 00        STATUS:     .word $0   ; state at lsb, last size+flag at msb   $E0
0000E2  2  00 00        CURBUF:     .word $0   ; CURBUF next free byte in TIB          $E2
0000E4  2  00 00        LASTHEAP:   .word $0   ; last link cell                        $E4
0000E6  2  00 00        NEXTHEAP:   .word $0   ; next free cell in heap dictionary     $E6
0000E8  2               ;
0000E8  2               ;                   pointer registers
0000E8  2               ;
0000E8  2  00 00        DSPTR:      .word $0   ; data stack pointer                    $E8
0000EA  2  00 00        RTPTR:      .word $0   ; return stack pointer                  $EA
0000EC  2  00 00        INSTPTR:    .word $0   ; instruction pointer                   $EC
0000EE  2  00 00        WORKREG:    .word $0   ; working register                      $EE
0000F0  2               ;
0000F0  2               ;                    TEMP1 - 4
0000F0  2               ;
0000F0  2  00 00        TEMP1:    .word $0     ; first                                 $F0
0000F2  2  00 00        TEMP2:    .word $0     ; second                                $F2
0000F4  2  00 00        TEMP3:    .word $0     ; third  (two bytes)                    $F4
0000F6  2  00 00        TEMP4:    .word $0     ; fourth  (two bytes)                   $F6
0000F8  2               ;
0000F8  2               ;          NXTTOK, BACKHEAP, TEMP5 - 7
0000F8  2               ;
0000F8  2  00 00        NXTTOK:     .word $0   ; next token in tib (INBUF)             $F8
0000FA  2  00 00        BACKHEAP:   .word $0   ; hold 'here while compile              $FA
0000FC  2               supprint:              ; suppress printing in MEMCPY
0000FC  2  xx           TEMP5:   .res 1        ;    WORDS, supprint for MEMCPY         $FC
0000FD  2  xx           TEMP6:   .res 1        ;    WORDS, DIGCOUNT, TEXTGET           $FD
0000FE  2               HFE_T2:                ;   third temp variable for HyFedit
0000FE  2  xx xx        TEMP7:   .res 2        ;    AUTOLOAD, HyFedit                  $FE
000100  2               ;
000100  2               ; *** $B0-$FF total usage in ZP, including TEMP vars ***
000100  2               ;
000100  2               ;   HYDRA-16 serial/read buffers at $200 and $300 so skip those
000100  2               ;
000100  2               ;----------------------------------------------------------------------
000100  2               ;                   FORTH STACKS
000100  2               ;----------------------------------------------------------------------
000100  2               .segment "BUFFERS"
000200r 2               INBUF:                                      ;    $400
000200r 2  xx xx xx xx        .res 256
000204r 2  xx xx xx xx  
000208r 2  xx xx xx xx  
000300r 2               DS:                          ; data stack (S)    $500
000300r 2  xx xx xx xx        .res 126
000304r 2  xx xx xx xx  
000308r 2  xx xx xx xx  
00037Er 2  xx xx              .res 2
000380r 2               RT:                          ; return stack (R)  $580
000380r 2  xx xx xx xx        .res 126
000384r 2  xx xx xx xx  
000388r 2  xx xx xx xx  
0003FEr 2  xx xx              .res 2
000400r 2               MEMSTK:                      ; memory manager    $600
000400r 2  xx xx xx xx        .res 510
000404r 2  xx xx xx xx  
000408r 2  xx xx xx xx  
0005FEr 2  xx xx              .res 2
000600r 2               PAD:                         ; pad memory         $800
000600r 2               ;     .res 256
000600r 2  xx xx xx xx        .res 512               ; clipboard / command history (together with PAD for now)
000604r 2  xx xx xx xx  
000608r 2  xx xx xx xx  
000800r 2               ;CLIP:
000800r 2               ;      .res 256
000800r 2               ;
000800r 2               ;
000800r 2               .segment "FORTH_ROM"              ; regular core $EC00 and RAM segment at $0A00 now
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               ; ************ the real deal...
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               
000000r 2               forth_main:
000000r 2  4C rr rr         jmp F_cold
000003r 2  4C rr rr         jmp COPYTORAM
000006r 2               HYPROMPT:
000006r 2  0D 0A            .byte $0D, $0A
000008r 2  48 46 3E         .byte "HF>"
00000Br 2  00               .byte 0
00000Cr 2               ;
00000Cr 2               HYLIBMSG:
00000Cr 2  0D 0A            .byte $0D, $0A
00000Er 2  4C 69 62 61      .byte "Libaries:"
000012r 2  72 69 65 73  
000016r 2  3A           
000017r 2  00               .byte 0
000018r 2               .ifdef DEBUG
000018r 2               WDISP:
000018r 2  0D 0A            .byte $0D, $0A
00001Ar 2  57 3D            .byte "W="
00001Cr 2  00               .byte 0
00001Dr 2               WATDISP:
00001Dr 2  20 20 5B 57      .byte "  [W]="
000021r 2  5D 3D        
000023r 2  00               .byte 0
000024r 2               .endif
000024r 2               ;
000024r 2               ;
000024r 2               ;
000024r 2               F_cold:                         ; cold start - clears EVERYTHING
000024r 2  D8               cld
000025r 2  20 rr rr         jsr CLEAR                   ; zero out zero page, INBUF, DS, RT, SCRBUF
000028r 2                                               ;
000028r 2                                               ; initialize HEAP pointers
000028r 2  AD 03 A0         lda INROM+3                 ; standard 'heap' has been copied to RAM
00002Br 2  85 E5            sta LASTHEAP + 1            ; and 'switcher' ('exit') code handles
00002Dr 2                                               ; the rest, with 'nest', 'unnest', 'keeps' 'next', 'jump'
00002Dr 2                                               ; 'spull', 'spush', 'comma' etc. all in ROM
00002Dr 2  AD 04 A0         lda INROM+4                 ; wherever THAT ends up.
000030r 2  85 E4            sta LASTHEAP
000032r 2                                               ; next heap free cell
000032r 2  AD 05 A0         lda INROM+5                 ; along with 'ends'
000035r 2  1A               inc a
000036r 2  85 E7            sta NEXTHEAP + 1
000038r 2  64 E6            stz NEXTHEAP
00003Ar 2  64 D7            stz ERRFLAG                ; clear ERROR flag
00003Cr 2               
00003Cr 2  A0 rr            ldy #>(MEMSTK+MEMEND)       ; initialize memory manager area
00003Er 2  84 CF            sty MEMPTR + 1
000040r 2  A0 rr            ldy #<(MEMSTK+MEMEND)
000042r 2  84 CE            sty MEMPTR
000044r 2  A0 F0            ldy #<MEMTOP
000046r 2  84 D0            sty MEMLAST
000048r 2  A0 7B            ldy #>MEMTOP
00004Ar 2  84 D1            sty MEMLAST+1
00004Cr 2               
00004Cr 2  A0 00            ldy #<SCRBUF                ; initialize screen pointer
00004Er 2  8C rr rr         sty HFEBUF                  ; HFE_BPTR is set up when
000051r 2  A0 7C            ldy #>SCRBUF                ; HFE_COLD is called
000053r 2  8C rr rr         sty HFEBUF+1                ; done.  SCRBUF already cleared
000056r 2               
000056r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000056r 2               ; various reinitialization points
000056r 2               ;
000056r 2               F_warm:                          ; warm start.  Clears TIB, DS, RT, and PAD
000056r 2               reset:                           ; leaves heap, memory area, and screen buffer alone
000056r 2  A0 FD            ldy #INBUF_end
000058r 2  84 D4            sty TIBEND
00005Ar 2  A0 rr            ldy #<INBUF
00005Cr 2  84 D2            sty TIB
00005Er 2  A0 rr            ldy #>INBUF
000060r 2  84 D3            sty TIB+1
000062r 2  84 D5            sty TIBEND+1
000064r 2  84 E3            sty CURBUF+1
000066r 2  84 F9            sty NXTTOK+1
000068r 2               
000068r 2  A0 rr            ldy #>DS                     ; DS and RT are now half page each
00006Ar 2  84 E9            sty DSPTR + 1
00006Cr 2  A0 rr            ldy #>RT
00006Er 2  84 EB            sty RTPTR + 1
000070r 2               
000070r 2  20 rr rr         jsr PADINIT                  ; clear PAD
000073r 2               
000073r 2  A9 FF            lda #$FF                     ; DEBUG OFF by default
000075r 2  85 D6            sta DFLAG
000077r 2               ;
000077r 2  A9 rr            lda #<STARTAUTO                ; check to see if anything to autoload
000079r 2  85 F0            sta TEMP1
00007Br 2  A9 rr            lda #>STARTAUTO
00007Dr 2  85 F1            sta TEMP1+1
00007Fr 2  A0 00            ldy #0
000081r 2  B1 F0            lda (TEMP1),y
000083r 2  F0 0B            beq warm_next
000085r 2               
000085r 2  64 E0            stz STATUS                   ; status is 'interpret' == \0
000087r 2  64 DF            stz ALFLAG
000089r 2  A0 7E            ldy #<DSEND
00008Br 2  84 E8            sty DSPTR
00008Dr 2                   ;
00008Dr 2                   ;  stz DFLAG           ; start debug
00008Dr 2                   ;
00008Dr 2  4C rr rr         jmp CLOAD_ENTER
000090r 2               ;
000090r 2               warm_next:
000090r 2  64 DF            stz ALFLAG                   ; autoload flag OFF
000092r 2               
000092r 2               abort:                            ; clear DS (clear data stack)
000092r 2  A0 7E            ldy #<DSEND
000094r 2  84 E8            sty DSPTR
000096r 2               
000096r 2               errrtn:                          ; return from error w/o clearing DS
000096r 2               quit:
000096r 2  A0 FE            ldy #<RTEND                  ; clear RT
000098r 2  84 EA            sty RTPTR
00009Ar 2               
00009Ar 2  20 rr rr         jsr WERROR                   ; print any error messages, clear error flags
00009Dr 2  A0 00            ldy #0                       ; reset INBUF
00009Fr 2  A9 00            lda #0
0000A1r 2  91 D2            sta (TIB),y                  ; clear INBUF stuff
0000A3r 2  64 E2            stz CURBUF                   ; clear cursor  (pointer into INBUF)
0000A5r 2  64 E0            stz STATUS                   ; status is 'interpret' == \0
0000A7r 2               
0000A7r 2  2C               .byte $2c       ; mask next two bytes, jump to resolve/okey
0000A8r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
0000A8r 2               ; the outer loop
0000A8r 2               
0000A8r 2               resolvept:
0000A8r 2  rr rr            .word okey
0000AAr 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
0000AAr 2               okey:               ; well shit, I hope this is easy....
0000AAr 2               resolve:           ; get a token
0000AAr 2  20 rr rr         jsr token      ; then just process the regular way
0000ADr 2               .ifdef DEBUG
0000ADr 2  A5 D6            lda DFLAG                 ; DEBUG
0000AFr 2  D0 0F            bne RVPSKIP
0000B1r 2  A9 0D 20 rr      WCRLF_np
0000B5r 2  rr A9 0A 20  
0000B9r 2  rr rr        
0000BBr 2  A9 50            lda #'P'
0000BDr 2  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR            ; DEBUG
0000C0r 2               .endif
0000C0r 2               
0000C0r 2               RVPSKIP:
0000C0r 2               
0000C0r 2               RESFIND:                ; load last or 'latest' word on heap
0000C0r 2  A5 E5            lda LASTHEAP + 1
0000C2r 2  85 F3            sta TEMP2 + 1
0000C4r 2  A5 E4            lda LASTHEAP
0000C6r 2  85 F2            sta TEMP2
0000C8r 2               
0000C8r 2               RESLOOP:              ; lsb linked list
0000C8r 2  A5 F2            lda TEMP2
0000CAr 2  85 EE            sta WORKREG             ; so 'last' -> W
0000CCr 2  05 F3            ora TEMP2+1             ; only zero if both are zero
0000CEr 2  D0 07            bne RESEACH              ; PGS - did he forget this?  Sure did...
0000D0r 2               
0000D0r 2               WORDNOTFOUND:
0000D0r 2  A9 05            lda #ERR_UKW           ; UNKNOWN WORD error
0000D2r 2  85 D7            sta ERRFLAG
0000D4r 2  4C rr rr         jmp errrtn ; end of dictionary, no more words to search, abort
0000D7r 2               
0000D7r 2               RESEACH:                        ; msb linked list
0000D7r 2  A5 F3            lda TEMP2 + 1
0000D9r 2  85 EF            sta WORKREG + 1           ; update next link
0000DBr 2               
0000DBr 2  A2 EE            ldx #WORKREG
0000DDr 2  A0 F2            ldy #TEMP2
0000DFr 2  20 rr rr         jsr copyfrom                  ; W += 2, now pointing at size/flag byte from 'here'
0000E2r 2  A0 00            ldy #0              ; compare words
0000E4r 2  B1 EE            lda (WORKREG), y    ; save the flag, first byte is (size and flag)
0000E6r 2  85 E1            sta STATUS + 1
0000E8r 2                           ;; *** start of mod for bit check
0000E8r 2  29 3F            and #$3F            ; mask off flags
0000EAr 2  38               sec
0000EBr 2  F1 F8            sbc (NXTTOK), y    ; compare lengths
0000EDr 2  D0 D9            bne RESLOOP
0000EFr 2  C8               iny
0000F0r 2               ; compare chars
0000F0r 2               RESEQUAL:
0000F0r 2  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK), y
0000F2r 2  C9 20            cmp #$20            ; space ends
0000F4r 2  F0 09            beq RESDONE
0000F6r 2  38               sec                 ; verify
0000F7r 2  F1 EE            sbc (WORKREG), y
0000F9r 2  0A               asl                 ; clean 7-bit ascii
0000FAr 2  D0 CC            bne RESLOOP
0000FCr 2  C8               iny                 ; get next char
0000FDr 2  D0 F1            bne RESEQUAL
0000FFr 2               
0000FFr 2               RESDONE:
0000FFr 2  98               tya                ; increment W by y, W will point at CFA?
000100r 2  20 rr rr         jsr addwx
000103r 2               
000103r 2               eval:
000103r 2               ; executing ? if status = 0
000103r 2  A5 E0            lda STATUS
000105r 2  F0 1F            beq execute
000107r 2               ;
000107r 2               ; falls thru on compile, but...
000107r 2               ; immediate ? if status+1 < 0 (bit seven set)
000107r 2  A5 E1            lda STATUS + 1
000109r 2  30 1B            bmi immediate
00010Br 2               
00010Br 2               compile:          ; otherwise compile
00010Br 2               .ifdef DEBUG
00010Br 2  A5 D6            lda DFLAG          ; DEBUG, print C if here
00010Dr 2  D0 0F            bne CMPSKIP
00010Fr 2  A9 0D 20 rr      WCRLF_np
000113r 2  rr A9 0A 20  
000117r 2  rr rr        
000119r 2  A9 43            lda #'C'
00011Br 2  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
00011Er 2               CMPSKIP:
00011Er 2               .endif
00011Er 2  20 rr rr         jsr wcomma          ; copy W into NEXTHEAP ('here'), increment NEXTHEAP
000121r 2  B0 03            bcs immediate
000123r 2  4C rr rr         jmp resolve         ; if not 'immediate' go on to next token
000126r 2               ;
000126r 2               immediate:
000126r 2               execute:
000126r 2               
000126r 2               .ifdef DEBUG
000126r 2  A5 D6            lda DFLAG         ; DEBUG, print E if here
000128r 2  D0 0F            bne EXESKIP
00012Ar 2  A9 0D 20 rr      WCRLF_np
00012Er 2  rr A9 0A 20  
000132r 2  rr rr        
000134r 2  A9 45            lda #'E'
000136r 2  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
000139r 2               EXESKIP:
000139r 2               .endif
000139r 2  A9 rr            lda #>resolvept     ; set up INSTPTR to run,
00013Br 2  85 ED            sta INSTPTR + 1     ; or return to interpreter.
00013Dr 2  A9 rr            lda #<resolvept
00013Fr 2  85 EC            sta INSTPTR
000141r 2  4C rr rr         jmp pick             ; almost done, 'next' and either ENTER or EXEC
000144r 2               
000144r 2               ;-----------------------START PROCESSING INPUT-----------------------
000144r 2               try:
000144r 2  B1 D2            lda (TIB), y                   ; index is in y
000146r 2  F0 04            beq getline    ; if \0  - get a line if pointing at 0
000148r 2  C8               iny
000149r 2  49 20            eor #$20    ; return 0 in  A if a space
00014Br 2  60               rts
00014Cr 2               
00014Cr 2               ;--------------------GET AN INPUT LINE ENDING WITH CR/LF ------------
00014Cr 2               getline:   ; drop rts of try, fall through to 'token'
00014Cr 2  68               pla
00014Dr 2  68               pla
00014Er 2               ;
00014Er 2               ;   DO AUTOLOAD HERE
00014Er 2               ;      load a space, then copy next line to buffer
00014Er 2               ;      calc y (length + 1) jump to GETLNEND
00014Er 2               ;
00014Er 2  A5 DF            lda ALFLAG
000150r 2  F0 06            beq GLNORMAL
000152r 2  20 rr rr         jsr ALOADTIB
000155r 2  4C rr rr         jmp GETLNSKIPCRLF
000158r 2               
000158r 2               GLNORMAL:
000158r 2  5A A0 00 B9      WSEQ_raw HYPROMPT    ; print prompt
00015Cr 2  rr rr F0 06  
000160r 2  C8 20 rr rr  
000167r 2               ;
000167r 2  A0 00            ldy #0   ; leave the first
000169r 2               GETLOOP:
000169r 2  91 D2            sta (TIB), y  ; dummy store on first pass, overwritten
00016Br 2  C8               iny
00016Cr 2  C4 D4            cpy TIBEND
00016Er 2  F0 44            beq GETLNEND
000170r 2  C0 FF            cpy #$FF
000172r 2  D0 02            bne GETREADLOOP
000174r 2  A0 01            ldy #1
000176r 2               GETREADLOOP:            ; character read loop
000176r 2  20 rr rr         jsr READ_CHAR_NE    ; read with no echo, so we can write back what we want
000179r 2  90 FB            bcc GETREADLOOP
00017Br 2                   ;
00017Br 2                   ;  will check here for ctrl-a,c,v,x
00017Br 2  20 rr rr         jsr CTRLCHECK
00017Er 2  B0 F6            bcs GETREADLOOP
000180r 2                   ;
000180r 2  C9 09            cmp #$09         ; handle tab
000182r 2  D0 1F            bne GETLNNOTAB
000184r 2  48 A9 0D 20      WCRLF             ; yup, was a tab
000188r 2  rr rr A9 0A  
00018Cr 2  20 rr rr 68  
000190r 2  A9 20            lda #$20          ; CRLF and indent
000192r 2  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
000195r 2  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
000198r 2  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
00019Br 2  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
00019Er 2  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
0001A1r 2  80 C6            bra GETLOOP      ; will store a space rather than tab
0001A3r 2               GETLNNOTAB:
0001A3r 2  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR    ; just write back what we got
0001A6r 2  C9 0D            cmp #$0D
0001A8r 2  F0 0A            beq GETLNEND
0001AAr 2  C9 08            cmp #$08         ; handle backspace
0001ACr 2  D0 BB            bne GETLOOP
0001AEr 2  88               dey              ; yup, backing up
0001AFr 2  88               dey
0001B0r 2  B1 D2            lda (TIB), y      ; make sure prev char not overwritten
0001B2r 2  80 B5            bra GETLOOP
0001B4r 2               GETLNEND:                ; clear all if y eq \0
0001B4r 2  48 A9 0D 20      WCRLF                 ; macro, preserves A
0001B8r 2  rr rr A9 0A  
0001BCr 2  20 rr rr 68  
0001C0r 2               GETLNSKIPCRLF:          ; SKIP to here if don't want CRLF
0001C0r 2  A9 20            lda #$20
0001C2r 2  5A               phy
0001C3r 2  A0 00            ldy #0
0001C5r 2  91 D2            sta (TIB), y       ; start with space
0001C7r 2  7A               ply
0001C8r 2  91 D2            sta (TIB), y        ; ends with space
0001CAr 2  A9 00            lda #0            ; mark eol with 0
0001CCr 2  C8               iny
0001CDr 2  91 D2            sta (TIB), y
0001CFr 2  88               dey
0001D0r 2               ; start it
0001D0r 2  85 E2            sta CURBUF
0001D2r 2               
0001D2r 2               ;------------------AWKWARD Brazilian english comment here...-----------
0001D2r 2               ;
0001D2r 2               ;    **** docs for 'token', the linchpin of the parser ***
0001D2r 2               ;
0001D2r 2               ; in place every token,
0001D2r 2               ; the counter is placed at last space before word
0001D2r 2               ; no rewinds
0001D2r 2               ;
0001D2r 2               ;  (i.e 'the length byte goes immediately before name of word')
0001D2r 2               ;
0001D2r 2               ;    to access the buffer after a line is entered, to something like...
0001D2r 2               ;
0001D2r 2               ;      jsr token
0001D2r 2               ;      ldy #0
0001D2r 2               ;      lda (NXTTOK), y       ; length will be in A
0001D2r 2               ;      .... do some shit ...
0001D2r 2               ;loop:
0001D2r 2               ;      iny
0001D2r 2               ;      lda (NXTTOK), y       ; first char of token
0001D2r 2               ;      cmp #$20
0001D2r 2               ;      beq done             ; if is space, token done
0001D2r 2               ;      ... do some shit ...
0001D2r 2               ;      bra loop             ; go get next char
0001D2r 2               ;      ... etc ....
0001D2r 2               ;done:
0001D2r 2               ;
0001D2r 2               token:
0001D2r 2  A4 E2            ldy CURBUF   ; last position on INBUF
0001D4r 2               
0001D4r 2               TOKENSKIP:   ; skip spaces
0001D4r 2  20 rr rr         jsr try
0001D7r 2  F0 FB            beq TOKENSKIP
0001D9r 2  88               dey   ; keep y == <start of input word> + 1
0001DAr 2  84 F8            sty NXTTOK
0001DCr 2               
0001DCr 2               TOKENSCAN:  ; scan spaces
0001DCr 2  20 rr rr         jsr try
0001DFr 2  D0 FB            bne TOKENSCAN
0001E1r 2  88               dey   ; keep y == <end of input word> + 1
0001E2r 2  84 E2            sty CURBUF
0001E4r 2               
0001E4r 2               TOKENDONE:  ; find size and store it;
0001E4r 2  98               tya
0001E5r 2  38               sec
0001E6r 2  E5 F8            sbc NXTTOK
0001E8r 2  A4 F8            ldy NXTTOK    ; keep it
0001EAr 2  88               dey
0001EBr 2  91 D2            sta (TIB), y  ; store size for counted string
0001EDr 2  84 F8            sty NXTTOK
0001EFr 2                   ;
0001EFr 2                   ;  During interpretive mode at least...do number and string capture here, before
0001EFr 2                   ;     looking at word list; will be pushed on stack.
0001EFr 2                   ;  This SHOULD have a general digit converter (any base up to 16); return C = 1 if no conversion
0001EFr 2                   ;   if SINGLE is defined, this will skip single digit numbers and use hardcoded ones.
0001EFr 2                   ;
0001EFr 2                   ;  Following check for #'s, check for quoted inline txt a la 'q^....^'
0001EFr 2                   ;
0001EFr 2               .ifdef numbers
0001EFr 2  A0 00            ldy #0
0001F1r 2  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK),y
0001F3r 2  AA               tax                ; store size in X, pass to conversion
0001F4r 2  20 rr rr         jsr DIGCONVT
0001F7r 2  B0 02            bcs CHKFERTXT       ; if some error in conversion, skip and continue processing
0001F9r 2  80 0C            bra TOKCLR0
0001FBr 2               .endif  ; 'numbers'
0001FBr 2               CHKFERTXT:
0001FBr 2  20 rr rr         jsr TXG3            ; TEXTGET - new version, WORKS!
0001FEr 2  B0 18            bcs TOKENEND
000200r 2  A0 00            ldy #0
000202r 2  A9 20            lda #$20
000204r 2  4C rr rr         jmp TOKCLR
000207r 2               TOKCLR0:
000207r 2  A0 00            ldy #0
000209r 2  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK),y     ; load length again
00020Br 2  AA               tax
00020Cr 2  E8               inx
00020Dr 2  A9 20            lda #$20         ; copy spaces over entire converted string
00020Fr 2               TOKCLR:
00020Fr 2  91 F8            sta (NXTTOK),y
000211r 2  C8               iny
000212r 2  CA               dex
000213r 2  D0 FA            bne TOKCLR
000215r 2               TOKNEXT:
000215r 2  4C rr rr         jmp token               ; and use 'token' to rebuild input buffer w/o converted #
000218r 2               
000218r 2               
000218r 2               TOKENEND:
000218r 2  18               clc     ; clean - setup token
000219r 2  60               rts
00021Ar 2               ;
00021Ar 2               ;--------------------UTILITIES----------------------------------------
00021Ar 2               ;    A whole bunch of helper functions;
00021Ar 2               ;  wcomma / comma increment WORKREG (or another reg) and NEXTHEAP ('here')
00021Ar 2               ;  while copying into NEXTHEAP, uses incwx.
00021Ar 2               ;
00021Ar 2               ;  copyinto / copyfrom copy AND increment/decrement
00021Ar 2               ;  spush/rpush - pushes something onto stacks indexed on ZP by Y (increment using addwx/incwx)
00021Ar 2               ;  spull/rpull - pulls from stacks, copies into ZP indexed by Y (decrement builtin)
00021Ar 2               ;
00021Ar 2               ;  addwx/incwx/decwx do some of the inc/dec-rementing duties
00021Ar 2               ;
00021Ar 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
00021Ar 2               ;
00021Ar 2               ;   COMMA allocates memory at top of heap for 'other stuff'
00021Ar 2               ;   and makes sure heap and working reg pointers are updated.
00021Ar 2               ;
00021Ar 2               ; heap linked list (moves forward)
00021Ar 2               ;
00021Ar 2               wcomma:
00021Ar 2  A0 EE            ldy #WORKREG                  ; copy addr at WORKREG, change addr fld NEXTHEAP points to
00021Cr 2               comma:
00021Cr 2  A2 E6            ldx #NEXTHEAP                 ; Y has source of address, change addr fld NEXTHEAP points to
00021Er 2                   ; FALL THROUGH - copyinto, then rts after second incwx
00021Er 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
00021Er 2               ; from a page zero address indexed by Y
00021Er 2               ; into a page zero indirect address indexed by X
00021Er 2               ;
00021Er 2               copyinto:
00021Er 2  B9 00 00         lda 0, y
000221r 2  81 00            sta (0, x)
000223r 2  20 rr rr         jsr incwx
000226r 2  B9 01 00         lda 1, y
000229r 2  81 00            sta (0, x)
00022Br 2  4C rr rr         jmp incwx                        ; incwx ends with rts!
00022Er 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
00022Er 2               ;
00022Er 2               ; generics - PUSH, PULL, incwx/addwx, copyfrom
00022Er 2               ;
00022Er 2               ;------------------------PUSH a cell--------------------------------
00022Er 2               spush_2:
00022Er 2  A0 F4            ldy #TEMP3       ; push TEMP3 on top
000230r 2  4C rr rr         jmp spush
000233r 2               spush_1:
000233r 2  A0 F2            ldy #TEMP2       ; push TEMP2 on top
000235r 2  4C rr rr         jmp spush
000238r 2               spush_0:             ; push TEMP1 to stack, probably top of stack
000238r 2  A0 F0            ldy #TEMP1
00023Ar 2                    ; FALL THROUGH
00023Ar 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
00023Ar 2               ; PUSH a cell
00023Ar 2               ; from a page zero address indexed by Y
00023Ar 2               ; into a page zero indirect address indexed by X
00023Ar 2               spush:
00023Ar 2  A2 E8            ldx #DSPTR
00023Cr 2  A5 E8            lda DSPTR
00023Er 2  C9 rr            cmp #<DS
000240r 2  F0 20            beq ptrerr_s            ; DS boundary check
000242r 2  4C rr rr         jmp push
000245r 2               rpush:
000245r 2  A2 EA            ldx #RTPTR
000247r 2  A5 EA            lda RTPTR
000249r 2  C9 rr            cmp #<RT                ; RT boundary check
00024Br 2  F0 11            beq ptrerr_r
00024Dr 2               
00024Dr 2                   ; FALL THROUGH
00024Dr 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
00024Dr 2               ; classic stack backwards
00024Dr 2               push:
00024Dr 2  20 rr rr         jsr decwx
000250r 2  B9 01 00         lda 1, y
000253r 2  81 00            sta (0, x)
000255r 2  20 rr rr         jsr decwx
000258r 2  B9 00 00         lda 0, y
00025Br 2  81 00            sta (0, x)
00025Dr 2  60               rts
00025Er 2               ;
00025Er 2               ;                      pointer error (DS or RT)
00025Er 2               ptrerr_r:                      ; pop jsr off stack, throw error
00025Er 2  A9 01            lda #ERR_RPTR
000260r 2  80 02            bra ptrerr_cont
000262r 2               ptrerr_s:
000262r 2  A9 02            lda #ERR_SPTR
000264r 2               ptrerr_cont:
000264r 2  85 D7            sta ERRFLAG
000266r 2  68               pla
000267r 2  68               pla
000268r 2  4C rr rr         jmp errrtn
00026Br 2               ;
00026Br 2               ;---------------- PULL a cell, with convenience for TEMP 1/2/3 -----
00026Br 2               ;
00026Br 2               spull_2:
00026Br 2  A0 F4            ldy #TEMP3              ; pull TEMP3 from top
00026Dr 2  4C rr rr         jmp spull
000270r 2               
000270r 2               spull_1:                    ; pull TEMP2 from top
000270r 2  A0 F2            ldy #TEMP2
000272r 2  4C rr rr         jmp spull
000275r 2               
000275r 2               spull_0:
000275r 2  A0 F0            ldy #TEMP1             ; pull TEMP1 from top of DS
000277r 2               ;
000277r 2               ;  FALL THROUGH
000277r 2               ;
000277r 2               ; PULL a cell
000277r 2               ; from a page zero indirect address indexed by X
000277r 2               ; into a page zero address indexed by y
000277r 2               ;
000277r 2               spull:
000277r 2  A2 E8            ldx #DSPTR
000279r 2  A5 E8            lda DSPTR         ; pointer bounds checking
00027Br 2  C9 7E            cmp #DSEND        ; ditto
00027Dr 2  F0 E3            beq ptrerr_s      ; ditto
00027Fr 2  4C rr rr         jmp pull          ; pull includes rts from incwx so....
000282r 2               rpull:                ;
000282r 2  A2 EA            ldx #RTPTR
000284r 2  A5 EA            lda RTPTR        ; pointer bounds checking
000286r 2  C9 FE            cmp #RTEND        ; ditto
000288r 2  F0 D4            beq ptrerr_r        ; ditto
00028Ar 2               ;
00028Ar 2               ;  FALL THROUGH
00028Ar 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
00028Ar 2               ;
00028Ar 2               ; from a page zero indirect address indexed by X
00028Ar 2               ; into a page zero address indexed by y
00028Ar 2               pull:
00028Ar 2               copyfrom:
00028Ar 2  A1 00            lda (0, x)
00028Cr 2  99 00 00         sta 0, y
00028Fr 2  20 rr rr         jsr incwx      ; NOTE:  not a jmp, returns here.
000292r 2  A1 00            lda (0, x)
000294r 2  99 01 00         sta 1, y
000297r 2                ;
000297r 2                ;  FALL THROUGH
000297r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000297r 2               ; increment a word in page zero, identified by X and offset to it from $00.
000297r 2               ;
000297r 2               ;   Usage: ldx #<pointer name> and then jsr incwx/addwx/decwx
000297r 2               ;
000297r 2               ;   THESE functions are SOLELY intended to increment/decrement
000297r 2               ;   zeropage pointers.  Offsets indexed by
000297r 2               ;   X are INTO the zeropage space, not relative to a specific
000297r 2               ;   pointer location; X IDENTIFIES the 16-bit pointer in
000297r 2               ;   question, period.
000297r 2               ;
000297r 2               incwx:
000297r 2  A9 01            lda #01
000299r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000299r 2               ; add a byte in A to a word in page zero. X identifies the pointer
000299r 2               ; being incremented, and is not itself dec'd or inc'd.
000299r 2               ;
000299r 2               addwx:
000299r 2  18               clc
00029Ar 2  75 00            adc 0, x
00029Cr 2  95 00            sta 0, x
00029Er 2  90 03            bcc addwx_end
0002A0r 2  F6 01            inc 1, x
0002A2r 2  18               clc      ; keep carry clean.
0002A3r 2                            ; our convention is that functions SET the carry for positive results,
0002A3r 2                            ; clear carry for negative or neutral ones.
0002A3r 2               addwx_end:
0002A3r 2  60               rts
0002A4r 2               
0002A4r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
0002A4r 2               ;          decwx, heap moves
0002A4r 2               ; decrement a word in page zero, identified by X and offset to it from $00.
0002A4r 2               ;
0002A4r 2               ;   Usage: ldx #<pointer name> and then jsr decwx
0002A4r 2               ;
0002A4r 2               ;   THESE functions are SOLELY intended to increment/decrement
0002A4r 2               ;   zeropage pointers.  Offsets indexed by
0002A4r 2               ;   X are INTO the zeropage space, not relative to a specific
0002A4r 2               ;   pointer location; X IDENTIFIES the 16-bit pointer in
0002A4r 2               ;   question, period.
0002A4r 2               ;
0002A4r 2               decwx:
0002A4r 2  B5 00            lda 0, x
0002A6r 2  D0 02            bne decwx_end
0002A8r 2  D6 01            dec 1, x
0002AAr 2               decwx_end:
0002AAr 2  D6 00            dec 0, x
0002ACr 2  60               rts
0002ADr 2               ;
0002ADr 2               ;
0002ADr 2               ;
0002ADr 2               ENGINEEND:
0002ADr 2               ;                           END OF CORE ENGINE
0002ADr 2               ;----------------------------------------------------------------------
0002ADr 2               ;
0002ADr 2               ;                        DICTIONARY and ADDITIONS
0002ADr 2               ;
0002ADr 2               ;    upper.s -- error messaging, CLEAR on start, debug, utilities
0002ADr 2               ;------------------------------------------------------------------------
0002ADr 2               ;
0002ADr 2               upper_ram:
0002ADr 2                  .include "upper.s"
0002ADr 3               ;
0002ADr 3               ;   upper.s  - utility functions for HyForth - eventually ROM resident
0002ADr 3               ;
0002ADr 3               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
0002ADr 3               ;  error / system messaging
0002ADr 3               ;           set ERRFLAG to appropriate error code (hyforth.s preamble),
0002ADr 3               ;      print error/system message, then clear ERRFLAG and ERRPTR again.
0002ADr 3               ;
0002ADr 3               WERROR:
0002ADr 3  A9 rr            lda #>err_jumptable
0002AFr 3  85 D9            sta ERRPTR+1
0002B1r 3  A5 D7            lda ERRFLAG
0002B3r 3  F0 36            beq ERREND
0002B5r 3  0A               asl a
0002B6r 3  18               clc
0002B7r 3  69 rr            adc #<err_jumptable
0002B9r 3  85 D8            sta ERRPTR
0002BBr 3  90 02            bcc ERRSKIP
0002BDr 3  E6 D9            inc ERRPTR+1
0002BFr 3               ERRSKIP:
0002BFr 3  A9 0D 20 rr      WERR ERRPTR
0002C3r 3  rr A9 0A 20  
0002C7r 3  rr rr A0 00  
0002EBr 3               ERREND:
0002EBr 3  A9 00            lda #0
0002EDr 3  85 D7            sta ERRFLAG
0002EFr 3  85 D8            sta ERRPTR
0002F1r 3  85 D9            sta ERRPTR+1
0002F3r 3  60               rts
0002F4r 3               ;
0002F4r 3               ;err_jumptable:    moved to RAM space (hyforth.s) to facilitate custom
0002F4r 3               ;   system messaging
0002F4r 3               ;
0002F4r 3               ;  error messages

000303r 3  00               .byte 0

000359r 3               
000359r 3               ;-------------------------------------------------------------
000359r 3               ;
000359r 3               ;           CODE called by engine AND heap
000359r 3               ;
000359r 3               ;------- used by 'exit'
000359r 3               ;------------------------NOTE:  this is ALL of 'exit' but the screaming.
000359r 3               ;----------------- For modularity purposes this is the only sensible place for it.
000359r 3               ;
000359r 3               EXIT:
000359r 3               unnest:                             ; EXIT - done with previous word, on to next whether compiled or primitive
000359r 3               
000359r 3  A0 EC            ldy #INSTPTR
00035Br 3  20 rr rr         jsr rpull                       ; IP = [RTPTR], RTPTR += 2
00035Er 3               
00035Er 3               next:                               ;    go on to next word; IP is pointing at next entry in CFA of word
00035Er 3                                                   ; or first entry in CFA of next word.
00035Er 3                                                   ;
00035Er 3                                                   ; WORKREG = (INSTPTR) ; INSTPTR += 2
00035Er 3  A2 EC            ldx #INSTPTR
000360r 3  A0 EE            ldy #WORKREG
000362r 3  20 rr rr         jsr copyfrom                    ; W = [IP], IP += 2  ( and either ENTER or EXECUTE )
000365r 3               
000365r 3               pick:                               ;        'THE SWITCHER' - COMPILED OR PRIMITIVE?
000365r 3                                                   ;

000398r 3               ;  ( otherwise known as THE SWITCH )
000398r 3               ;
000398r 3  A5 EF            lda WORKREG + 1                 ; compare pages (MSBs)
00039Ar 3  C9 rr            cmp #>ends + 1                  ;  !! Compiled must be higher in memory than hardcoded !! (see below)
00039Cr 3  30 16            bmi jump                        ; jump over nest if native code
00039Er 3  A0 01            ldy #1                          ; ldy #0 if check extra byte ($89 or $24 for various 'bit' variants)
0003A0r 3  B1 EE            lda (WORKREG),y
0003A2r 3  F0 10            beq jump                        ; yep, jump to native execution
0003A4r 3               
0003A4r 3               nest:                               ; ENTER in classic Forth lingo   ( COMPILED )
0003A4r 3  A0 EC            ldy #INSTPTR
0003A6r 3  20 rr rr         jsr rpush                       ; RTPTR -=2, [RTPTR] = [IP]
0003A9r 3  A5 EE            lda WORKREG                     ; W = IP
0003ABr 3  85 EC            sta INSTPTR
0003ADr 3  A5 EF            lda WORKREG + 1
0003AFr 3  85 ED            sta INSTPTR + 1
0003B1r 3  4C rr rr         jmp next                        ; next
0003B4r 3               
0003B4r 3               jump:                               ; EXECUTE                      ( PRIMITIVE )
0003B4r 3  6C EE 00         jmp (WORKREG)                   ; start running code at next word
0003B7r 3                                                   ;  JUMP [W]

0003FFr 3  84 A0            sty PADPTR                   ; is $0000 (no previous item)

000408r 3  9C rr rr         stz PAD+1

00040Cr 3               CPYPAD:
00040Cr 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_1
00040Fr 3               NEWPAD:                            ; new PAD item setup
00040Fr 3                                                  ; address of temp string in TEMP2, zero term'd
00040Fr 3  A5 A0            lda PADPTR
000411r 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
000413r 3  A5 A1            lda PADPTR+1                   ; backup PADPTR to TEMP1
000415r 3  85 F1            sta TEMP1+1
000417r 3  A2 A0            ldx #PADPTR
000419r 3  A9 03            lda #3
00041Br 3  20 rr rr         jsr addwx
00041Er 3               ;    inc PADPTR
00041Er 3               ;    inc PADPTR                     ; skip over previous address and length
00041Er 3               ;    inc PADPTR
00041Er 3  A0 FF            ldy #$FF
000420r 3               NEWPADLP0:

000446r 3               MALLOC:
000446r 3                   ;  TEMP1 and TEMP2 should have bytes / record type if 'jsr MALLOC'
000446r 3                   ;  uses TEMP3, y, x, a
000446r 3  A5 D0            lda MEMLAST
000448r 3  38               sec

00044Br 3  85 D0            sta MEMLAST
00044Dr 3  B0 02            bcs MALSK00
00044Fr 3  C6 D1            dec MEMLAST+1

000451r 3  38               sec
000452r 3  E5 F0            sbc TEMP1

000456r 3  A5 D1            lda MEMLAST+1
000458r 3  E5 F1            sbc TEMP1+1
00045Ar 3  85 D1            sta MEMLAST+1
00045Cr 3                                       ;MEMLAST updated to start of new record
00045Cr 3  A5 D0            lda MEMLAST
00045Er 3  85 F4            sta TEMP3
000460r 3  A5 D1            lda MEMLAST+1
000462r 3  85 F5            sta TEMP3+1         ; use TEMP3 to walk through clearing of memory
000464r 3  A0 00            ldy #0
000466r 3  A5 F2            lda TEMP2            ; write type first
000468r 3  91 F4            sta (TEMP3),y
00046Ar 3  C8               iny
00046Br 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1            ; LSB length

00046Fr 3  C8               iny
000470r 3  A5 F1            lda TEMP1+1          ; MSB length
000472r 3  91 F4            sta (TEMP3),y
000474r 3  A2 F4            ldx #TEMP3
000476r 3  A9 03            lda #3
000478r 3  20 rr rr         jsr addwx            ; increment TEMP3 by 3
00047Br 3               MALLOOP:

000481r 3  C6 F0            dec TEMP1

000485r 3  A5 F1            lda TEMP1+1

00048Fr 3  C6 F1            dec TEMP1+1
000491r 3               MALSK02:

000499r 3  A0 00            ldy #0
00049Br 3  A5 D0            lda MEMLAST
00049Dr 3  91 CE            sta (MEMPTR),y
00049Fr 3  C8               iny
0004A0r 3  A5 D1            lda MEMLAST+1

0004CDr 3               ;-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
0004CDr 3               ;                                TEXTGET / TXG3
0004CDr 3               ;   get escaped, delimited text from INBUF, store in temp or permanent memory
0004CDr 3               ;    'token' has set NXTTOK to starting address in INBUF.
0004CDr 3               ;
0004CDr 3               ;  uses TEMP1, TEMP2, TEMP3 (MALLOC only?), TEMP4, TEMP5, TEMP6, x, y
0004CDr 3               ;    TEMP1 starts as length, then as dest address (pushed on stack to end)
0004CDr 3               ;    TEMP2 as record type, then as modified dest address during copy
0004CDr 3               ;    TEMP3 stomped by MALLOC ('Q'), not affect by 'q' option
0004CDr 3               ;    TEMP4 counts esc chars, 4+1 flag for PAD vs memory dest
0004CDr 3               ;    TEMP5 and TEMP6 are bookends of NXTTOK offset
0004CDr 3               ;    y used as index throughout in various read/cpy operations
0004CDr 3               ;    x at end to copy back so parser knows how many chars to blank out if all goes well
0004CDr 3               ;
0004CDr 3               ;   Original TEXTGET code (TXG2) still stored in 11/12/26, 9:41AM version
0004CDr 3               ;
0004CDr 3               ;          copy/escape - translate escaped chars
0004CDr 3               ;
0004CDr 3               ;      will modify this sometime to require and handle escaped
0004CDr 3               ;    two-digit hex values, ie \EE and such.
0004CDr 3               ;
0004CDr 3               ;  NOTE:  if a \0 or \00 is in middle of string, TEXTGET WILL
0004CDr 3               ;    copy it correctly, but...it may not print or copy right
0004CDr 3               ;    with other functions that expect proper z-termination.
0004CDr 3               ;
0004CDr 3               ;  table includes all chars from $20 to $46 inclusive
0004CDr 3               ;    $30-$39 -> trunc'd to $0x, $41-$46 to $0A-$0F
0004CDr 3               ;    $22 is dbl-quote, $1B (\@) is escape char
0004CDr 3               ;
0004CDr 3               TXHEXTBL:
0004CDr 3  20 21            .byte " !"
0004CFr 3  22               .byte $22
0004D0r 3  23 24 25 26      .byte "#$%&'()*+,-./"
0004D4r 3  27 28 29 2A  
0004D8r 3  2B 2C 2D 2E  
0004DDr 3  00 01 02 03      .byte $00, $01, $02, $03, $04, $05, $06, $07, $08, $09
0004E1r 3  04 05 06 07  
0004E5r 3  08 09        
0004E7r 3  3A 3B 3C 3D      .byte ":;<=>?"
0004EBr 3  3E 3F        
0004EDr 3  1B 0A 0B 0C      .byte $1B, $0A, $0B, $0C, $0D, $0E, $0F
0004F1r 3  0D 0E 0F     
0004F4r 3               
0004F4r 3               TXCPYESC:
0004F4r 3  C8               iny
0004F5r 3  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK),y
0004F7r 3  C9 20            cmp #$20                    ; is this a single hex digit
0004F9r 3  90 0D            bcc TXCPYSK                 ; or the escape char '\@'?
0004FBr 3  C9 47            cmp #$47
0004FDr 3  F0 09            beq TXCPYSK
0004FFr 3  B0 07            bcs TXCPYSK
000501r 3  38               sec
000502r 3  E9 20            sbc #$20                    ; if so, lookup substitute
000504r 3  AA               tax
000505r 3  BD rr rr         lda TXHEXTBL,x
000508r 3               TXCPYSK:
000508r 3  C6 F2            dec TEMP2                    ; adjust TEMP2 so we are
00050Ar 3  B0 02            bcs TXDECT2SK                ; not printing the extra '\'
00050Cr 3  C6 F3            dec TEMP2+1
00050Er 3               TXDECT2SK:
00050Er 3  60               rts
00050Fr 3               ;
00050Fr 3               ;  end of TXG2  (new text capture)
00050Fr 3               ;
00050Fr 3               ;-------------- TXG3 -  new version of TEXTGET
00050Fr 3               ;
00050Fr 3               TXG3:
00050Fr 3  4C rr rr         jmp TX3GETTIB                 ; TEMP1 will have length, TEMP2 dest addr of string
000512r 3                                                 ; TEMP6 has starting index in buffer, TEMP5 end index
000512r 3               ;
000512r 3               TX3TIBDONE:                       ;
000512r 3                   ;                   now we switch between q (PAD) and Q (MALLOC)
000512r 3                   ;
000512r 3  A5 F7            lda TEMP4+1
000514r 3  C9 51            cmp #'Q'
000516r 3  F0 14            beq TX3mCONT                ; 'q' or 'Q' ?
000518r 3                   ;
000518r 3                   ;  if 'q':
000518r 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1
00051Ar 3  A0 02            ldy #2
00051Cr 3  91 A0            sta (PADPTR),y              ; copy length to PAD
00051Er 3  A5 A0            lda PADPTR                  ; and then copy ptr location
000520r 3  85 F2            sta TEMP2
000522r 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1                    ; copy for pushing later
000524r 3  A5 A1            lda PADPTR+1
000526r 3  85 F3            sta TEMP2+1
000528r 3  85 F1            sta TEMP1+1                  ; copy for pushing later
00052Ar 3                   ;
00052Ar 3  80 0E            bra TX3tCONT
00052Cr 3                   ;
00052Cr 3                   ;    if 'Q':
00052Cr 3               TX3mCONT:
00052Cr 3  20 rr rr         jsr MALLOC                      ; TEMP1 now has address on mem stack, length already written
00052Fr 3  A0 00            ldy #0
000531r 3  B1 F0            lda (TEMP1),y
000533r 3  85 F2            sta TEMP2
000535r 3  C8               iny
000536r 3  B1 F0            lda (TEMP1),y                   ; and make a copy of address for copy
000538r 3  85 F3            sta TEMP2+1                     ; operation from input buffer below.
00053Ar 3                   ; continue
00053Ar 3               TX3tCONT:
00053Ar 3  4C rr rr         jmp TX3STRCPY
00053Dr 3               TX3STRCOPYDONE:                   ; copy string and translate escaped chars
00053Dr 3  A5 F7            lda TEMP4+1
00053Fr 3  C9 51            cmp #'Q'
000541r 3  F0 1B            beq TX3mCONT2                ; 'q' or 'Q' ?
000543r 3  A0 02            ldy #2                       ; 'q', so finish updating and incrementing PADPTR
000545r 3  B1 A0            lda (PADPTR),y               ; get length
000547r 3  1A               inc a
000548r 3  1A               inc a
000549r 3  1A               inc a
00054Ar 3  18               clc
00054Br 3  65 A0            adc PADPTR
00054Dr 3  85 A0            sta PADPTR
00054Fr 3  90 02            bcc TX3tUPD
000551r 3  E6 A1            inc PADPTR+1
000553r 3               TX3tUPD:
000553r 3  A0 00            ldy #0
000555r 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1
000557r 3  91 A0            sta (PADPTR),y
000559r 3  C8               iny
00055Ar 3  A5 F1            lda TEMP1+1
00055Cr 3  91 A0            sta (PADPTR),y
00055Er 3               ;
00055Er 3               TX3mCONT2:
00055Er 3  4C rr rr         jmp TX3FIXTIB
000561r 3               TX3NOGOOD:
000561r 3  38               sec                             ; C=1, no text, so process as token
000562r 3               TX3END:
000562r 3  60               rts
000563r 3               ;
000563r 3               ;--
000563r 3               ;
000563r 3               TX3GETTIB:
000563r 3  64 F6            stz TEMP4                       ; to count escape '\' chars
000565r 3  64 F7            stz TEMP4+1                     ; flag for PAD vs memory storage
000567r 3  64 F1            stz TEMP1+1                     ; will store length here
000569r 3  A9 04            lda #$04
00056Br 3  85 F2            sta TEMP2                       ; record type is 'sz'
00056Dr 3  64 F3            stz TEMP2+1
00056Fr 3  64 FD            stz TEMP6                       ; to save y for later copy
000571r 3  A0 01            ldy #1                          ; skip len of first token
000573r 3               TX3SKSPC:
000573r 3  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK),y
000575r 3  C9 20            cmp #$20                        ; skip leading spaces
000577r 3  D0 03            bne TX3SK00
000579r 3  C8               iny
00057Ar 3  80 F7            bra TX3SKSPC
00057Cr 3               TX3SK00:
00057Cr 3  C9 71            cmp #'q'                        ; text will go to PAD
00057Er 3  D0 02            bne TX3SK_Q
000580r 3  80 04            bra TX3SK_q
000582r 3               TX3SK_Q:
000582r 3  C9 51            cmp #'Q'                        ; text will got to memory
000584r 3  D0 DB            bne TX3NOGOOD
000586r 3               TX3SK_q:
000586r 3               ;    and #$40                        ; mask off q vs Q bits
000586r 3  85 F7            sta TEMP4+1                     ; store q or Q
000588r 3  C8               iny
000589r 3  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK),y
00058Br 3  C9 5E            cmp #'^'
00058Dr 3  D0 D2            bne TX3NOGOOD
00058Fr 3  C8               iny
000590r 3  84 FD            sty TEMP6                       ; TEMP6 stores pos of first char
000592r 3               TX3SCAN:
000592r 3  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK),y                  ; find delimiting '^'
000594r 3  C9 5C            cmp #'\'
000596r 3  D0 02            bne TX3SK04
000598r 3  E6 F6            inc TEMP4
00059Ar 3               TX3SK04:
00059Ar 3  C9 5E            cmp #'^'
00059Cr 3  D0 08            bne TX3SK03                    ; handle escaped ^ only
00059Er 3  88               dey                            ; other escaped chars handled when copied to
00059Fr 3  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK),y                 ; MALLOC'd address (see TX2COPYLOOP below)
0005A1r 3  C8               iny
0005A2r 3  C9 5C            cmp #'\'
0005A4r 3  D0 0B            bne TX3FOUND
0005A6r 3               TX3SK03:
0005A6r 3  C8               iny                             ; get index back after checking for \^
0005A7r 3  98               tya
0005A8r 3  18               clc
0005A9r 3  65 F8            adc NXTTOK
0005ABr 3  C9 64            cmp #MAXSTR
0005ADr 3  B0 B2            bcs TX3NOGOOD
0005AFr 3  80 E1            bra TX3SCAN
0005B1r 3               TX3FOUND:                         ; quoted text found.  Now do some math.
0005B1r 3  84 FC            sty TEMP5                       ; TEMP5 is pos+1 last letter
0005B3r 3  98               tya
0005B4r 3  38               sec
0005B5r 3  E5 FD            sbc TEMP6                       ; and TEMP6 is where we started, so...
0005B7r 3  38               sec
0005B8r 3  E5 F6            sbc TEMP4                       ; subtract escape (\) chars
0005BAr 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1                       ; and this should be the length
0005BCr 3  E6 F0            inc TEMP1
0005BEr 3  4C rr rr         jmp TX3TIBDONE
0005C1r 3               ;
0005C1r 3               ;--
0005C1r 3               ;
0005C1r 3               ;   now for math.  NXTTOK ptr needs to be ref'd with same y
0005C1r 3               ;    as TEMP2, so we need to match them up...and we are hitting
0005C1r 3               ;    the record at +3, so....TEMP6 is start
0005C1r 3               TX3STRCPY:
0005C1r 3  A5 F2            lda TEMP2
0005C3r 3  38               sec
0005C4r 3  E5 FD            sbc TEMP6
0005C6r 3  B0 02            bcs TX3SK01
0005C8r 3  C6 F3            dec TEMP2+1
0005CAr 3               TX3SK01:
0005CAr 3  18               clc
0005CBr 3  69 03            adc #3
0005CDr 3  90 02            bcc TX3SK02
0005CFr 3  E6 F3            inc TEMP2+1
0005D1r 3               TX3SK02:
0005D1r 3  A4 FD            ldy TEMP6
0005D3r 3               TX3CPYLOOP:
0005D3r 3  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK),y
0005D5r 3  C9 5C            cmp #'\'
0005D7r 3  D0 03            bne  TX3CPY0
0005D9r 3  20 rr rr         jsr TXCPYESC                   ; handle escaped chars (translate using table below)
0005DCr 3               TX3CPY0:
0005DCr 3  91 F2            sta (TEMP2),y
0005DEr 3  C8               iny
0005DFr 3  C4 FC            cpy TEMP5
0005E1r 3  D0 F0            bne TX3CPYLOOP
0005E3r 3               ;
0005E3r 3  A9 00            lda #0
0005E5r 3  91 F2            sta (TEMP2),y                   ; put zero on end
0005E7r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spush_0                     ; push address from mem stack on DS
0005EAr 3  4C rr rr         jmp TX3STRCOPYDONE
0005EDr 3               ;
0005EDr 3               ;
0005EDr 3               TX3FIXTIB:
0005EDr 3  A5 FC            lda TEMP5                       ; now get the length of statement in TIB back
0005EFr 3  38               sec                             ; so can return in X
0005F0r 3  E5 FD            sbc TEMP6
0005F2r 3  18               clc
0005F3r 3  69 04            adc #4
0005F5r 3  AA               tax                             ; calc and bring back length to X
0005F6r 3  18               clc                             ; C=0 means all good, so tell parser to cleanup buffer
0005F7r 3  4C rr rr         jmp TX3END
0005FAr 3               ;
0005FAr 3               ;
0005FAr 3               ;
0005FAr 3               .ifdef numbers
0005FAr 3               ;------------------------
0005FAr 3               ;      CONVERT DIGITS, PUSH on DS
0005FAr 3               
0005FAr 3               DIGCONVT:    ;  Y is index into NXTPTR, X is length
0005FAr 3  64 F0            stz TEMP1
0005FCr 3  64 F1            stz TEMP1+1
0005FEr 3  64 F2            stz TEMP2                       ; no necc for bin or hex, but
000600r 3  64 F3            stz TEMP2+1                     ; since is convenient....
000602r 3  64 FD            stz TEMP6
000604r 3  A0 01            ldy #1                          ; skip over length
000606r 3  A9 0A            lda #10
000608r 3  85 DA            sta DIGBASE
00060Ar 3  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK),y
00060Cr 3  C9 2D            cmp #'-'
00060Er 3  F0 0A            beq DIGCONV_MINUS
000610r 3  C9 24            cmp #'$'
000612r 3  F0 12            beq DIGHEX1
000614r 3  C9 25            cmp #'%'
000616r 3  F0 07            beq DIGBIN1
000618r 3  80 12            bra DIG_SNG
00061Ar 3               DIGCONV_MINUS:
00061Ar 3  E6 FD            inc TEMP6
00061Cr 3  4C rr rr         jmp DIG_XY
00061Fr 3               DIGBIN1:
00061Fr 3  A9 02            lda #2
000621r 3  85 DA            sta DIGBASE
000623r 3  4C rr rr         jmp DIG_XY
000626r 3               DIGHEX1:
000626r 3  A9 10            lda #16
000628r 3  85 DA            sta DIGBASE
00062Ar 3               DIG_XY:
00062Ar 3  C8               iny
00062Br 3  CA               dex
00062Cr 3               DIG_SNG:
00062Cr 3                 .ifdef SINGLE
00062Cr 3  E0 01            cpx #1                          ; skip single digit, handle hard-wired or...
00062Er 3  D0 03            bne DIGCONV_LOOP                ; only one digit left, we can handle that elsewhere
000630r 3  4C rr rr         jmp DIGCONV_ERR                 ; not really an error, just return and let 'find' to it
000633r 3                 .endif
000633r 3               DIGCONV_LOOP:
000633r 3  20 rr rr         jsr GETDIG
000636r 3  90 03            bcc DIGCONT0
000638r 3  4C rr rr         jmp DIGCONV_ERR
00063Br 3               DIGCONT0:
00063Br 3  48               pha                             ; else save it to add in a bit
00063Cr 3  06 F0            asl TEMP1                       ; do first shift
00063Er 3  26 F1            rol TEMP1+1
000640r 3  A5 DA            lda DIGBASE                     ; check base that was set above
000642r 3  C9 0A            cmp #10
000644r 3  F0 06            beq DIGDEC                      ; it's DEC, go there
000646r 3  C9 02            cmp #2
000648r 3  F0 2F            beq DIGBIN                      ; it's BIN, go there
00064Ar 3  80 1D            bra DIGHEX                      ; Go to hex if others not true
00064Cr 3               DIGDEC:
00064Cr 3  06 F0            asl TEMP1                       ; 2nd shift; upper nybble doesn't end up right
00064Er 3  26 F1            rol TEMP1+1
000650r 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1                       ; load results of two shifts
000652r 3  18               clc
000653r 3  65 F2            adc TEMP2                       ; add in previous total from last loop
000655r 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
000657r 3  A5 F1            lda TEMP1+1                     ; and same with second digit, and the carry
000659r 3  65 F3            adc TEMP2+1
00065Br 3  85 F1            sta TEMP1+1
00065Dr 3               DIGDEC2:
00065Dr 3  06 F0            asl TEMP1                       ; final shift
00065Fr 3  26 F1            rol TEMP1+1
000661r 3  68               pla                             ; bring back read digit
000662r 3  C9 0A            cmp #10                         ; make sure it's not hex, mostly
000664r 3  90 18            bcc DIGCONT                     ; jump to continue
000666r 3  4C rr rr         jmp DIGCONV_ERR                 ; else throw error
000669r 3               DIGHEX:
000669r 3  06 F0            asl TEMP1
00066Br 3  26 F1            rol TEMP1+1
00066Dr 3  06 F0            asl TEMP1
00066Fr 3  26 F1            rol TEMP1+1
000671r 3  06 F0            asl TEMP1
000673r 3  26 F1            rol TEMP1+1                     ; shifted three more times
000675r 3  68               pla
000676r 3  4C rr rr         jmp DIGCONT
000679r 3               DIGBIN:                             ; already did the single shift
000679r 3  68               pla
00067Ar 3  C9 02            cmp #2
00067Cr 3  B0 3E            bcs DIGCONV_ERR                 ; fall through to the additon of new digit
00067Er 3               DIGCONT:
00067Er 3  18               clc                             ; add digit finally
00067Fr 3  65 F0            adc TEMP1
000681r 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
000683r 3  85 F2            sta TEMP2                       ; copy to intermediate result in case another dec digit
000685r 3  90 02            bcc DIGCONT2
000687r 3  E6 F1            inc TEMP1+1                     ; and inc 2nd byte if necc.
000689r 3               DIGCONT2:
000689r 3  A5 DA            lda DIGBASE
00068Br 3  C9 0A            cmp #10
00068Dr 3  D0 08            bne DIGCONT3
00068Fr 3  A5 F1            lda TEMP1+1
000691r 3  85 F3            sta TEMP2+1                     ; save it for next round, regardless
000693r 3  C9 80            cmp #$80                        ; check to see if > $8000
000695r 3  B0 25            bcs DIGCONV_ERR
000697r 3               DIGCONT3:
000697r 3  C8               iny
000698r 3  CA               dex
000699r 3  D0 98            bne DIGCONV_LOOP
00069Br 3  A5 DA            lda DIGBASE
00069Dr 3  C9 0A            cmp #10
00069Fr 3  D0 16            bne DIGCONT4
0006A1r 3               DECFINISH:
0006A1r 3  A5 FD            lda TEMP6                       ; check for minus
0006A3r 3  F0 12            beq DIGCONT4
0006A5r 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1
0006A7r 3  49 FF            eor #$FF
0006A9r 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
0006ABr 3  A5 F1            lda TEMP1+1
0006ADr 3  49 FF            eor #$FF
0006AFr 3  85 F1            sta TEMP1+1
0006B1r 3  E6 F0            inc TEMP1
0006B3r 3  D0 02            bne DIGCONT4
0006B5r 3  E6 F1            inc TEMP1+1
0006B7r 3               DIGCONT4:
0006B7r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spush_0                     ; push TEMP1 on to stack
0006BAr 3  18               clc
0006BBr 3  60               rts
0006BCr 3               DIGCONV_ERR:
0006BCr 3  38               sec
0006BDr 3  60               rts
0006BEr 3               
0006BEr 3               ;--------------------------
0006BEr 3               GETDIG:                             ; y is index to next char in NXTTOK
0006BEr 3  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK),y
0006C0r 3  38               sec
0006C1r 3  E9 30            sbc #'0'
0006C3r 3  90 10            bcc GETDIG_ERR
0006C5r 3  C9 0A            cmp #10
0006C7r 3  90 0A            bcc GETDIG_RTN
0006C9r 3  E9 07            sbc #7
0006CBr 3  C9 0A            cmp #10
0006CDr 3  90 06            bcc GETDIG_ERR
0006CFr 3  C9 10            cmp #16
0006D1r 3  B0 02            bcs GETDIG_ERR
0006D3r 3               GETDIG_RTN:                         ; pass carry clear for good digit
0006D3r 3  18               clc
0006D4r 3  60               rts
0006D5r 3               GETDIG_ERR:                         ; pass carry set for no digit
0006D5r 3  38               sec
0006D6r 3  60               rts
0006D7r 3               ;  end of new number conv
0006D7r 3               ;
0006D7r 3               H2NUM:
0006D7r 3  27 10         .byte $27,$10                ; 10000
0006D9r 3  03 E8         .byte $03,$E8                ; 1000
0006DBr 3  00 64         .byte $00,$64                ; 100
0006DDr 3  00 0A         .byte $00,$0A                ; 10
0006DFr 3               HEX2DEC:                            ; uses X, TEMP1, Z_MATH1,2,3,4
0006DFr 3  A5 A0            lda PADPTR
0006E1r 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
0006E3r 3  A5 A1            lda PADPTR+1
0006E5r 3  85 F1            sta TEMP1+1
0006E7r 3  A9 00            lda #0
0006E9r 3  85 B2            sta Z_MATH1                   ; flag for first non-zero digit
0006EBr 3  A2 A0            ldx #PADPTR
0006EDr 3  A9 03            lda #3
0006EFr 3  20 rr rr         jsr addwx
0006F2r 3               ;    inc PADPTR
0006F2r 3               ;    inc PADPTR
0006F2r 3               ;    inc PADPTR                     ; we'll come back to write length
0006F2r 3  A0 00            ldy #0
0006F4r 3  A2 00            ldx #0
0006F6r 3               H2DDIV10:
0006F6r 3  BD rr rr         lda H2NUM,x
0006F9r 3  85 B7            sta Z_MATH3+1
0006FBr 3  E8               inx
0006FCr 3  BD rr rr         lda H2NUM,x
0006FFr 3  85 B6            sta Z_MATH3
000701r 3  E8               inx
000702r 3  64 B8            stz Z_MATH4
000704r 3               H2DLOOP:
000704r 3  A5 B5            lda Z_MATH2+1
000706r 3  C5 B7            cmp Z_MATH3+1
000708r 3  90 19            bcc  H2DSK1
00070Ar 3  D0 06            bne  H2DSK0
00070Cr 3  A5 B4            lda Z_MATH2
00070Er 3  C5 B6            cmp Z_MATH3
000710r 3  90 11            bcc  H2DSK1
000712r 3               H2DSK0:
000712r 3  A5 B4            lda Z_MATH2
000714r 3  38               sec
000715r 3  E5 B6            sbc Z_MATH3
000717r 3  85 B4            sta Z_MATH2
000719r 3  A5 B5            lda Z_MATH2+1
00071Br 3  E5 B7            sbc Z_MATH3+1
00071Dr 3  85 B5            sta Z_MATH2+1
00071Fr 3  E6 B8            inc Z_MATH4
000721r 3  80 E1            bra H2DLOOP
000723r 3               H2DSK1:
000723r 3  A5 B2            lda Z_MATH1
000725r 3  D0 06            bne  H2DSK2
000727r 3  A5 B8            lda Z_MATH4
000729r 3  F0 0A            beq  H2DSK3
00072Br 3  E6 B2            inc Z_MATH1
00072Dr 3               H2DSK2:

00072Fr 3  18               clc
000730r 3  69 30            adc #'0'
000732r 3  91 A0            sta (PADPTR),y
000734r 3  C8               iny
000735r 3               H2DSK3:
000735r 3  E0 08            cpx #8
000737r 3  F0 03            beq H2DFIN
000739r 3  4C rr rr         jmp H2DDIV10                      ; back to top of loop
00073Cr 3               H2DFIN:
