00073Er 3  18               clc
00073Fr 3  69 30            adc #'0'
000741r 3  91 A0            sta (PADPTR),y
000743r 3  C8               iny
000744r 3  A9 00            lda #0                           ; write 0 on end of string, oops!
000746r 3  91 A0            sta (PADPTR),y
000748r 3  C8               iny
000749r 3  5A               phy                               ; will pull back to A here in a bit
00074Ar 3  98               tya
00074Br 3  18               clc
00074Cr 3  65 A0            adc PADPTR
00074Er 3  85 A0            sta PADPTR
000750r 3  90 02            bcc H2DSK4
000752r 3  E6 A1            inc PADPTR+1
000754r 3               H2DSK4:
000754r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spush_0                       ; push address of string
000757r 3               ;   update length and PADPTR
000757r 3  68               pla                               ; pull back pushed Y
000758r 3  A0 02            ldy #2
00075Ar 3  91 F0            sta (TEMP1),y                     ; copy length
00075Cr 3  A0 00            ldy #0
00075Er 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1
000760r 3  91 A0            sta (PADPTR),y                    ; and copy address of prev
000762r 3  C8               iny                               ; entry here before leaving
000763r 3  A5 F1            lda TEMP1+1
000765r 3  91 A0            sta (PADPTR),y
000767r 3  60               rts
000768r 3               ;
000768r 3               ;
000768r 3               .endif    ; numbers
000768r 3               ;
000768r 3               ;--------------------------------------------------------------
000768r 3               ;                     ctrl check  - s, c, x, v
000768r 3               ;
000768r 3               ;         ctrl-s - copy TIB 0 to y to current screen buffer line
000768r 3               ;         ctrl-c - copy TIB 0 to y to PAD
000768r 3               ;         ctrl-v - pull last PAD entry and overwrite TIB starting at y (bounds check)
000768r 3               ;         ctrl-x - copy TIB 0 to y to PAD, clear TIB
000768r 3               ;
000768r 3               CTRLCHECK:                   ; A has last key entered, Y is current position in TIB
000768r 3  48               pha
000769r 3  C9 13            cmp #$13      ; ctrl-s
00076Br 3  F0 0F            beq DOCTRLS
00076Dr 3  C9 03            cmp #$03      ; ctrl-c
00076Fr 3  F0 33            beq JMPCTRLC
000771r 3  C9 16            cmp #$16      ; ctrl-v   - TIBEND should be check when adding into TIB
000773r 3  F0 2C            beq JMPCTRLV
000775r 3  C9 18            cmp #$18      ; ctrl-x
000777r 3  F0 2E            beq JMPCTRLX
000779r 3  18               clc             ; fell through, return
00077Ar 3               CTRLEND:
00077Ar 3  68               pla
00077Br 3  60               rts
00077Cr 3               
00077Cr 3               DOCTRLS:
00077Cr 3  20 rr rr         jsr TIB2PAD                   ; backs up Y to TEMP5, too.
00077Fr 3  A0 00            ldy #0
000781r 3  B1 A0            lda (PADPTR),y          ; we want PREVIOUS ITEM addr, not current
000783r 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1               ; pointer addr
000785r 3  C8               iny
000786r 3  B1 A0            lda (PADPTR),y
000788r 3  85 F1            sta TEMP1+1
00078Ar 3  A2 F0            ldx #TEMP1
00078Cr 3  A9 03            lda #3
00078Er 3  20 rr rr         jsr addwx
000791r 3  A5 C3            lda HFE_CV              ; paste at current row
000793r 3  85 F2            sta TEMP2
000795r 3  20 rr rr         jsr FELIN               ; get row address from TEMP2, returns addr to TEMP2
000798r 3  20 rr rr         jsr FECPY_TOC           ; and at current column
00079Br 3  A4 FC            ldy TEMP5                     ; restore y
00079Dr 3  38               sec                           ; C=1 means eat keystroke w/o save to buffer or echo
00079Er 3  4C rr rr         jmp CTRLEND
0007A1r 3               
0007A1r 3               JMPCTRLV:
0007A1r 3  4C rr rr         jmp DOCTRLV
0007A4r 3               JMPCTRLC:
0007A4r 3  4C rr rr         jmp DOCTRLC
0007A7r 3               JMPCTRLX:
0007A7r 3  4C rr rr         jmp DOCTRLX
0007AAr 3

0007AFr 3  38               sec                           ; C=1 means eat keystroke w/o save to buffer or echo
0007B0r 3  4C rr rr         jmp CTRLEND
0007B3r 3

0007B6r 3  A4 FC            ldy TEMP5

0007C0r 3               DOCXBSPC:

0007DCr 3  85 F1            sta TEMP1+1

0007E0r 3  A5 FC            lda TEMP5                      ; write the length while we're here
0007E2r 3  91 A0            sta (PADPTR),y
0007E4r 3  A9 03            lda #3       ; was 2
0007E6r 3  18               clc
0007E7r 3  65 A0            adc PADPTR

0007F5r 3  C8               iny             ; added

0008C5r 3  CA               dex
0008C6r 3  D0 FA            bne clrscrbuf1               ; and 64th col is zero, so...
0008C8r 3  A9 00            lda #0                       ; all rows zero term'd but
0008CAr 3  91 C8            sta (TEMP0),y                ; don't know if really need
0008CCr 3  A5 C8            lda TEMP0                    ; to clear rows this way or not, yet.
0008CEr 3  18               clc
0008CFr 3  69 40            adc #$40
0008D1r 3  90 02            bcc clrscrbuf2
0008D3r 3  E6 C9            inc TEMP0+1
0008D5r 3               clrscrbuf2:
0008D5r 3  85 C8            sta TEMP0
0008D7r 3  C6 FC            dec TEMP5
0008D9r 3  D0 DF            bne clrscrbuf0
0008DBr 3  60               rts
0008DCr 3               ;
0008DCr 3               ;-------------------------------------------------------------------
0008DCr 3               ;
0008DCr 3               ;  ANSI CLS, cursor reposition, and color change
0008DCr 3               ;
0008DCr 3               ANSIHDR:
0008DCr 3  A9 1B            lda #27
0008DEr 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
0008E1r 3  A9 5B            lda #91
0008E3r 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
0008E6r 3  60               rts
0008E7r 3               
0008E7r 3               ANSIONEDIG:   ;two ASCII digits in Z_ANSI1
0008E7r 3  A5 BB            lda Z_ANSI1 + 1
0008E9r 3  C9 30            cmp #$30
0008EBr 3  F0 03            beq ansi_scr_00
0008EDr 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
0008F0r 3               ansi_scr_00:
0008F0r 3  A5 BA            lda Z_ANSI1
0008F2r 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
0008F5r 3  60               rts
0008F6r 3               ;
0008F6r 3               CLEAR_SCR:
0008F6r 3               ANSICLS:
0008F6r 3  20 rr rr         jsr ANSIHDR
0008F9r 3  A9 32            lda #'2'
0008FBr 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
0008FEr 3  A9 4A            lda #'J'
000900r 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
000903r 3  60               rts
000904r 3               
000904r 3               ANSISCR:      ; assumes Z_ANSI1 AND Z_ANSI2 have digits
000904r 3                             ; one or two digit ASCII #'s already
000904r 3  20 rr rr         jsr ANSIHDR
000907r 3  20 rr rr         jsr ANSIONEDIG
00090Ar 3  A9 3B            lda #';'
00090Cr 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
00090Fr 3  A5 BC            lda Z_ANSI2
000911r 3  85 BA            sta Z_ANSI1
000913r 3  A5 BD            lda Z_ANSI2+1
000915r 3  85 BB            sta Z_ANSI1+1
000917r 3  20 rr rr         jsr ANSIONEDIG
00091Ar 3  A9 66            lda #'f'
00091Cr 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
00091Fr 3  60               rts
000920r 3               
000920r 3               ANSICOL:                 ; assumes Z_ANSI1 has digits
000920r 3  20 rr rr         jsr ANSIHDR
000923r 3  20 rr rr         jsr ANSIONEDIG
000926r 3  A9 6D            lda #'m'
000928r 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
00092Br 3  60               rts
00092Cr 3               ;
00092Cr 3               ; ---------------- end of upper.s
00092Cr 3               ;
00092Cr 3               ;   hyf_edit.s is loaded in hyforth.s directly after this
00092Cr 3               ;
00092Cr 3               
00092Cr 2               UPPER_END:

00092Cr 2               ;   hyf_edit.s -- the HyFedit screen editor stuff

00092Cr 3               ;----------------------------------------------------------------------
00092Cr 3               ;
00092Cr 3               ;  Patrick Struthers - October/November 2026
00092Cr 3               ;
00092Cr 3               ; *** hyf_edit.s
00092Cr 3               ;
00092Cr 3               ; HyFedit - screen editor for Hydra-16 / HyForth
00092Cr 3               ;
00092Cr 3               ; NOTES:  for now this is included at the tail of 'upper.s' in
00092Cr 3               ;   the main hyforth build, but has a seperate ROM space
00092Cr 3               ;   at $F500 per the os_rom.cfg file.  May just add it to
00092Cr 3               ;   tail of core Forth OS ROM code after all...
00092Cr 3               ;
00092Cr 3               ;
00092Cr 3               .debuginfo
00092Cr 3               .setcpu "65C02"
00092Cr 3               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
00092Cr 3               ;  macros in hyforth.s
00092Cr 3               ;.macro WCRLF_np                 -- print CRLF, no push/pull of A
00092Cr 3               ;.macro WCRLF                    -- print CRLF, p/p A
00092Cr 3               ;.macro WSEQ_np  strlbl          -- print (strlbl) + \0, p/p Y only
00092Cr 3               ;.macro WSEQ  strlbl             -- print (strlbl) + \0, p/p Y and A, w/CRLF
00092Cr 3               ;.macro ANSI b1,b2,b3,b4,b5,b6   -- print up to six ANSI codes, p/p A
00092Cr 3               ;
00092Cr 3               ;.macro ERR_entry errmsg         -- macro to create errmsg entry
00092Cr 3               ;.macro WERR errptr              -- write error using
00092Cr 3               ;
00092Cr 3               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
00092Cr 3               ;  definitions in hyforth.s
00092Cr 3               ;
00092Cr 3               ; DEBUG := 1        ; enable inclusion of debug code
00092Cr 3               ;
00092Cr 3               ; INROM = $A000
00092Cr 3               ;
00092Cr 3               ; malloc stack and managed memory area
00092Cr 3               ; both move downwards like other stacks
00092Cr 3               ;
00092Cr 3               ;MEMEND = $01FE            ; pointers DOWN from MEMEND+MEMSTK (store-and-decrement)
00092Cr 3               ;MEMTOP = $8000            ; malloc allocates DOWN from MEMTOP
00092Cr 3               ;
00092Cr 3               ;INBUF = $0400             ; see stacks segment
00092Cr 3               ;
00092Cr 3               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
00092Cr 3               ;  HyFedit definitions
00092Cr 3               ;
00092Cr 3               HFE_EOL = $00              ; end of line
00092Cr 3               HFE_EOR = $09              ; tab means end of row
00092Cr 3               HFE_HZC = $2D              ; for horizontal frame '-'
00092Cr 3               HFE_VTC = $7C              ; for vertical frame   '|'
00092Cr 3               ;
00092Cr 3               ;
00092Cr 3               ;.segment "ZEROPAGE"
00092Cr 3               ; **** hyforth.s ZP
00092Cr 3               ;
00092Cr 3               ;.org $C0
00092Cr 3               ;HFE_BPTR:   .res 2             ; pointer into 1K buffer      $C0
00092Cr 3               ;HFE_CH:     .res 1             ; cursor horizontal           $C2
00092Cr 3               ;HFE_CV:     .res 1             ; cursor vertical             $C3
00092Cr 3               ;HFE_T0:     .res 1             ; reserved                    $C4
00092Cr 3               ;HFE_T0H:    .res 1             ; reserved                    $C5
00092Cr 3               ;HFE_T1:     .res 1             ; reserved                    $C6
00092Cr 3               ;HFE_T1H:    .res 1             ; reserved                    $C7
00092Cr 3               ;
00092Cr 3               ;TEMP0:     .res 2             ;  DUMPREG                    $C8
00092Cr 3               ;TEMP8:     .res 2             ;  Hstring macro              $CA
00092Cr 3               ;TEMP9:     .res 2             ;  Imm                        $CC
00092Cr 3               ;MEMPTR:    .res 2             ;  malloc                     $CE
00092Cr 3               ;MEMLAST:   .res 2             ;  malloc                     $D0
00092Cr 3               ;TIB:       .res 2             ; pointer to input buffer     $D2
00092Cr 3               ;TIBEND:    .res 2             ; pointer to end of TIB       $D4
00092Cr 3               ;DFLAG:     .res 1             ; debug flag                  $D6
00092Cr 3               ;ERRFLAG:   .res 1             ; error type, 0 = none        $D7
00092Cr 3               ;ERRPTR:    .res 2             ; ptr to mitigation/message   $D8
00092Cr 3               ;TEMP1:     .res 2     ; first, COPYTORAM, MEMCPY            $F0
00092Cr 3               ;TEMP2:     .res 2     ; second, COPYTORAM, MEMCPY           $F2
00092Cr 3               ;TEMP3:     .res 2     ; third, COPYTORAM, MEMCPY            $F4
00092Cr 3               ;TEMP4:     .res 2     ; fourth,                             $F6
00092Cr 3               ;NXTTOK:    .res 2     ; next token in tib (INBUF)           $F8
00092Cr 3               ;TEMP5:     .res 1     ;    WORDS, WFIND, MEMCPY             $FC
00092Cr 3               ;TEMP6:     .res 1     ;    WORDS, DIGCONT, TEXTGET          $FD
00092Cr 3               ;TEMP7:     .res 2     ;    AUTOLOAD                         $FE
00092Cr 3               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
00092Cr 3               ;
00092Cr 3               ;     hyforth.s buffers
00092Cr 3               ;.segment "BUFFERS"
00092Cr 3               ;
00092Cr 3               ;   *** at $0400
00092Cr 3               ;INBUF:
00092Cr 3               ;      .res 256
00092Cr 3               ;
00092Cr 3               ;   *** at $0600
00092Cr 3               ;MEMSTK:                      ; memory manager
00092Cr 3               ;      .res 510
00092Cr 3               ;      .res 2
00092Cr 3               ;
00092Cr 3               ;
00092Cr 3               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
00092Cr 3               ;  hyforth.s starts at $0800
00092Cr 3               ;
00092Cr 3               ;  space reserved for *stuff* at $0806 to $0825
00092Cr 3               ;
00092Cr 3               ;HFE_RSV:           ; at $0806
00092Cr 3               ;HFECOLS:        ; offsets to data in RAM
00092Cr 3               ;    .byte $3F    ; 63 columns w/ $00 or $09 in rightmost
00092Cr 3               ;HFEROWS:
00092Cr 3               ;    .byte $10    ; 16 rows
00092Cr 3               ;HFEBUF:
00092Cr 3               ;    .res 2       ; addr to buffer
00092Cr 3               ;             ideally, we would allocate 1K with 'malloc', then copy
00092Cr 3               ;             the address to HFEBUF in HyForth, then call HFE_COLD
00092Cr 3               ;             ...or if buffer already got stuff, call HFE_WARM
00092Cr 3               ;
00092Cr 3               ;   .res 6
00092Cr 3               ;HFE_TITLE:
00092Cr 3               ;    .byte "Generic ScreenX"
00092Cr 3               ;    .byte 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0    ; title of screen - zero terminated string, up to 12 chars incl \0
00092Cr 3               ;
00092Cr 3               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
00092Cr 3               ;.segment "FORTH_EDIT"
00092Cr 3               ;                             ; this will be at $F500 until further notice...
00092Cr 3               HFE_WARM:
00092Cr 3  A9 01          lda #1                      ; set 'warm start' flag, HFE_T0H
00092Er 3  85 C5          sta HFE_T0H
000930r 3  80 06          bra hfe_warmover                    ; skip over next inst
000932r 3               HFE_COLD:
000932r 3  64 C5          stz HFE_T0H
000934r 3  64 C2          stz HFE_CH                  ; set cursor position
000936r 3  64 C3          stz HFE_CV
000938r 3               hfe_warmover:
000938r 3  AD rr rr       lda HFEBUF+1
00093Br 3  85 C1          sta HFE_BPTR+1
00093Dr 3  AD rr rr       lda HFEBUF                   ; find out where screen buffer is
000940r 3  85 C0          sta HFE_BPTR
000942r 3  20 rr rr       jsr HFE_SCRINIT
000945r 3               
000945r 3  4C rr rr       jmp HFE_START
000948r 3               
000948r 3               HFE_START:
000948r 3  20 rr rr       jsr  HFE_SCRINIT
00094Br 3  20 rr rr       jsr  HFE_REDRAW
00094Er 3               
00094Er 3               HFE_PARSE:
00094Er 3               
00094Er 3               hfe_getline:
00094Er 3               
00094Er 3               HFE_EXIT:
00094Er 3  60             rts
00094Fr 3               ;
00094Fr 3               ; end of main HyFedit code
00094Fr 3               ;
00094Fr 3               ;-------------------------------------------
00094Fr 3               ;   Screen Init
00094Fr 3               HFE_SCRINIT:
00094Fr 3                 ;jsr ANSICLS
00094Fr 3  A9 40          lda #$40         ; 64 columns, including line ending
000951r 3  8D rr rr       sta HFECOLS
000954r 3  A9 10          lda #$10         ; 16 rows
000956r 3  8D rr rr       sta HFEROWS
000959r 3  60             rts
00095Ar 3               
00095Ar 3               ;-------------------------------------------
00095Ar 3               ;   Redraw
00095Ar 3               HFE_REDRAW:
00095Ar 3  20 rr rr       jsr hfe_draw_dline                     ; draw top line
00095Dr 3  20 rr rr       jsr hfe_draw_title                     ; draw title line
000960r 3  20 rr rr       jsr hfe_draw_dline                     ; draw border
000963r 3  AE rr rr       ldx HFEROWS
000966r 3  A9 30          lda #$30
000968r 3  85 C6          sta HFE_T1                              ; for line #
00096Ar 3               HFE_LP0:
00096Ar 3  20 rr rr       jsr hfe_draw_bline
00096Dr 3               ;  iny
00096Dr 3               ;  tya
00096Dr 3  AD rr rr       lda HFECOLS
000970r 3  18             clc
000971r 3  65 C0          adc HFE_BPTR                           ; update pointer to get to
000973r 3  85 C0          sta HFE_BPTR
000975r 3  90 02          bcc HFE_SK0                            ; next line.
000977r 3  E6 C1          inc HFE_BPTR + 1
000979r 3               HFE_SK0:
000979r 3  A5 C6          lda HFE_T1                             ; for printed line count
00097Br 3  1A             inc a                                  ; increment for next
00097Cr 3  C9 3A          cmp #$3A                               ; line, and see if need
00097Er 3  D0 03          bne HFE_SK1                            ; alpha hex yet.
000980r 3  18             clc
000981r 3  69 07          adc #7                                 ; yup.  It's now 'A'
000983r 3               HFE_SK1:
000983r 3  85 C6          sta HFE_T1
000985r 3  CA             dex
000986r 3  D0 E2          bne HFE_LP0
000988r 3  20 rr rr       jsr hfe_draw_dline                     ; draw bottom line
00098Br 3  60             rts
00098Cr 3               ;
00098Cr 3               ;  *** draw horizontal line
00098Cr 3               ;
00098Cr 3               hfe_draw_dline:
00098Cr 3  AD rr rr       lda HFECOLS
00098Fr 3  18             clc
000990r 3  69 04          adc #4
000992r 3  AA             tax
000993r 3               hfe_ddloop:
000993r 3  A9 2D          lda #'-'
000995r 3  20 rr rr       jsr WRITE_CHAR
000998r 3  CA             dex
000999r 3  D0 F8          bne hfe_ddloop
00099Br 3  A9 0D 20 rr    WCRLF_np                      ; CRLF w/o push/pull of A
00099Fr 3  rr A9 0A 20  
0009A3r 3  rr rr        
0009A5r 3  60             rts
0009A6r 3               ;
0009A6r 3               ;  *** draw title line
0009A6r 3               ;
0009A6r 3               hfe_draw_title:
0009A6r 3  A9 7C          lda #'|'
0009A8r 3  20 rr rr       jsr WRITE_CHAR
0009ABr 3  A0 14          ldy #20
0009ADr 3  AE rr rr       ldx HFECOLS
0009B0r 3  E8             inx
0009B1r 3  A9 20          lda #$20
0009B3r 3               hfe_dtloop0:
0009B3r 3  20 rr rr       jsr WRITE_CHAR
0009B6r 3  CA             dex
0009B7r 3  88             dey
0009B8r 3  D0 F9          bne hfe_dtloop0
0009BAr 3                 ;
0009BAr 3  A9 31          lda #$31              ; set color to red
0009BCr 3  85 BA          sta Z_ANSI1
0009BEr 3  A9 33          lda #$33
0009C0r 3  85 BB          sta Z_ANSI1+1
0009C2r 3  20 rr rr       jsr ANSICOL         ; esc[31m
0009C5r 3                 ;
0009C5r 3  A0 00          ldy #0
0009C7r 3  A9 rr          lda #>HFE_TITLE
0009C9r 3  85 C5          sta HFE_T0 + 1
0009CBr 3  A9 rr          lda #<HFE_TITLE
0009CDr 3  85 C4          sta HFE_T0
0009CFr 3               hfe_dtloop1:
0009CFr 3  B1 C4          lda (HFE_T0), y
0009D1r 3  F0 07          beq hfe_dtcon0
0009D3r 3  20 rr rr       jsr WRITE_CHAR
0009D6r 3  C8             iny
0009D7r 3  CA             dex
0009D8r 3  80 F5          bra hfe_dtloop1
0009DAr 3               hfe_dtcon0:
0009DAr 3  64 BA          stz Z_ANSI1         ; set color back to normal
0009DCr 3  64 BB          stz Z_ANSI1+1
0009DEr 3  20 rr rr       jsr ANSICOL         ; esc[0m
0009E1r 3  A9 20          lda #$20
0009E3r 3               hfe_dtloop2:
0009E3r 3  20 rr rr       jsr WRITE_CHAR
0009E6r 3  CA             dex
0009E7r 3  D0 FA          bne hfe_dtloop2
0009E9r 3  20 rr rr       jsr WRITE_CHAR
0009ECr 3  A9 7C          lda #'|'
0009EEr 3  20 rr rr       jsr WRITE_CHAR
0009F1r 3  A9 0D 20 rr    WCRLF_np                      ; CRLF w/o push/pull of A
0009F5r 3  rr A9 0A 20  
0009F9r 3  rr rr        
0009FBr 3  60             rts
0009FCr 3               ;
0009FCr 3               ;  *** draw line from buffer
0009FCr 3               ;
0009FCr 3               hfe_draw_bline:
0009FCr 3  A9 7C          lda #'|'
0009FEr 3  20 rr rr       jsr WRITE_CHAR
000A01r 3                 ;
000A01r 3  A9 32          lda #$32              ; set color to green
000A03r 3  85 BA          sta Z_ANSI1
000A05r 3  A9 33          lda #$33
000A07r 3  85 BB          sta Z_ANSI1+1
000A09r 3  20 rr rr       jsr ANSICOL
000A0Cr 3                 ;
000A0Cr 3  A5 C6          lda HFE_T1                     ; line #
000A0Er 3  20 rr rr       jsr WRITE_CHAR                 ; print it
000A11r 3                 ;
000A11r 3  64 BA          stz Z_ANSI1
000A13r 3  64 BB          stz Z_ANSI1+1        ; and back to normal
000A15r 3  20 rr rr       jsr ANSICOL
000A18r 3                 ;
000A18r 3  A9 7C          lda #'|'
000A1Ar 3  20 rr rr       jsr WRITE_CHAR
000A1Dr 3  A0 00          ldy #0
000A1Fr 3               hfe_db_loop0:
000A1Fr 3  B1 C0          lda (HFE_BPTR),y                ; if \0 or tab,
000A21r 3  F0 1A          beq hfe_db_done                ; line is done
000A23r 3  C9 09          cmp #$09                       ; and we can skip
000A25r 3  F0 16          beq hfe_db_done                ; to next line.
000A27r 3  C9 1F          cmp #$1F
000A29r 3  90 0A          bcc hfe_db_spcsub              ; don't print non-printables
000A2Br 3  C9 7F          cmp #$7F                       ;..there SHOULDN'T be any
000A2Dr 3  B0 06          bcs hfe_db_spcsub              ; but who knows....
000A2Fr 3  20 rr rr       jsr WRITE_CHAR                 ; padding out line.
000A32r 3  C8             iny
000A33r 3  80 EA          bra hfe_db_loop0
000A35r 3               hfe_db_spcsub:
000A35r 3  A9 20          lda #$20
000A37r 3  20 rr rr       jsr WRITE_CHAR
000A3Ar 3  C8             iny
000A3Br 3  80 E2          bra hfe_db_loop0               ; substitute spc for non-printables
000A3Dr 3               hfe_db_done:
000A3Dr 3  84 C7          sty HFE_T1H                    ; swap around to calc extra spaces
000A3Fr 3  AD rr rr       lda HFECOLS
000A42r 3  38             sec
000A43r 3  E5 C7          sbc HFE_T1H
000A45r 3  A8             tay
000A46r 3  A9 20          lda #$20
000A48r 3               hfe_db_loop1:
000A48r 3  20 rr rr       jsr WRITE_CHAR                ; pad out rest of line
000A4Br 3  88             dey
000A4Cr 3  D0 FA          bne  hfe_db_loop1
000A4Er 3  A9 7C          lda #'|'
000A50r 3  20 rr rr       jsr WRITE_CHAR
000A53r 3  A9 0D 20 rr    WCRLF_np                      ; CRLF w/o push/pull of A
000A57r 3  rr A9 0A 20  
000A5Br 3  rr rr        
000A5Dr 3               
000A5Dr 3               ;  ldy HFE_T1H               ; restore Y and return to calc offset to next line...
000A5Dr 3               ;  jsr DUMPREG
000A5Dr 3  60             rts
000A5Er 3               
000A5Er 3               ;
000A5Er 3               ;                 update a line on screen
000A5Er 3               ;
000A5Er 3               HFE_UPDATE:
000A5Er 3  A0 00          ldy #0
000A60r 3  A2 C3          ldx #HFE_CV
000A62r 3               hfe_u_loop0:         ; count zeros/tabs up to current cursor pos
000A62r 3  B1 C0          lda (HFE_BPTR),y
000A64r 3  F0 07          beq  HFEU_ZCNT
000A66r 3  C9 09          cmp #$09
000A68r 3  F0 03          beq  HFEU_ZCNT
000A6Ar 3  C8             iny
000A6Br 3  80 F5          bra hfe_u_loop0
000A6Dr 3               HFEU_ZCNT:
000A6Dr 3  C8             iny
000A6Er 3  98             tya
000A6Fr 3  18             clc
000A70r 3  65 C0          adc HFE_BPTR         ; update HFE_BPTR
000A72r 3  90 02          bcc HFEU_SKIP
000A74r 3  E6 C1          inc HFE_BPTR + 1
000A76r 3               HFEU_SKIP:
000A76r 3  A0 00          ldy #0
000A78r 3  8A             txa
000A79r 3  F0 03          beq HFEU_LFOUND
000A7Br 3  CA             dex
000A7Cr 3  80 E4          bra hfe_u_loop0
000A7Er 3               HFEU_LFOUND:
000A7Er 3  A5 C2          lda HFE_CH
000A80r 3  85 C6          sta HFE_T1           ; backup horizontal cursor pos
000A82r 3  A9 00          lda #0
000A84r 3  85 C2          sta HFE_CH          ; cuz we wanna draw from beginning of line
000A86r 3  20 rr rr       jsr hfe_place_cur   ; place the cursor
000A89r 3  20 rr rr       jsr hfe_draw_bline  ; and draw from the current HFE_PTR until line end
000A8Cr 3  A5 FE          lda HFE_T2
000A8Er 3  85 C0          sta HFE_BPTR
000A90r 3  A5 FF          lda HFE_T2 + 1       ; restore HFE_PTR and HFE_CH
000A92r 3  85 C0          sta HFE_BPTR
000A94r 3  A5 C6          lda HFE_T1
000A96r 3  85 C2          sta HFE_CH
000A98r 3  60             rts
000A99r 3               
000A99r 3               hfe_place_cur:
000A99r 3  A5 C2          lda HFE_CH
000A9Br 3  18             clc
000A9Cr 3  69 02          adc #2
000A9Er 3  85 F4          sta TEMP3
000AA0r 3  A5 C3          lda HFE_CV
000AA2r 3  18             clc
000AA3r 3  69 04          adc #4
000AA5r 3  85 F6          sta TEMP4
000AA7r 3  20 rr rr       jsr ANSISCR
000AAAr 3  60             rts
000AABr 3               ;
000AABr 3               ;   Forth heap subroutines
000AABr 3               ;
000AABr 3               FECPY:                    ; copy a z-termed string from an addr in TEMP1
000AABr 3  A0 00            ldy #0
000AADr 3  80 0B            bra FECPYLP
000AAFr 3               FECPY_TOC:
000AAFr 3  A4 C2            ldy HFE_CH
000AB1r 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1
000AB3r 3  38               sec
000AB4r 3  E5 C2            sbc HFE_CH
000AB6r 3  B0 02            bcs  FECPYLP
000AB8r 3  C6 F1            dec TEMP1+1
000ABAr 3               FECPYLP:                         ; assumes zero term'd string
000ABAr 3  B1 F0            lda (TEMP1),y
000ABCr 3  91 F2            sta (TEMP2),y
000ABEr 3  F0 0B            beq FEADDEND
000AC0r 3  98               tya
000AC1r 3  1A               inc
000AC2r 3  1A               inc
000AC3r 3  CD rr rr         cmp HFECOLS                  ; max of 63 chars, rest trunc'd off
000AC6r 3  F0 03            beq FEADDEND                 ; this will be fixed for something more flexible
000AC8r 3  C8               iny
000AC9r 3  80 EF            bra FECPYLP
000ACBr 3               FEADDEND:
000ACBr 3  C8               iny
000ACCr 3  A9 00            lda #0
000ACEr 3  91 F2            sta (TEMP2),y                ; write zero at end of line
000AD0r 3  88               dey
000AD1r 3  84 F0            sty TEMP1
000AD3r 3  64 F1            stz TEMP1+1
000AD5r 3  60               rts
000AD6r 3               ;
000AD6r 3               ;
000AD6r 3               FELIN:           ; CALC ROW ADDRESS IN BUFFER, from TEMP2, ends up in TEMP2
000AD6r 3  64 F3            stz TEMP2+1
000AD8r 3  A5 F2            lda TEMP2
000ADAr 3  29 0F            and #$0F                     ; max rows are 16, mask off anything else
000ADCr 3  18               clc
000ADDr 3  2A               rol
000ADEr 3  2A               rol
000ADFr 3  2A               rol                          ; multiply times 64
000AE0r 3  2A               rol
000AE1r 3  90 02            bcc  M40SK0
000AE3r 3  26 F3            rol TEMP2+1
000AE5r 3               M40SK0:
000AE5r 3  2A               rol
000AE6r 3  90 02            bcc  M40SK1
000AE8r 3  26 F3            rol TEMP2+1
000AEAr 3               M40SK1:
000AEAr 3  2A               rol
000AEBr 3  90 02            bcc  M40SK2
000AEDr 3  26 F3            rol  TEMP2+1
000AEFr 3               M40SK2:
000AEFr 3  18               clc
000AF0r 3  6D rr rr         adc HFEBUF
000AF3r 3  90 02            bcc  FEADDSK3
000AF5r 3  E6 F3            inc TEMP2+1
000AF7r 3               FEADDSK3:
000AF7r 3  85 F2            sta TEMP2
000AF9r 3  AD rr rr         lda HFEBUF+1
000AFCr 3  18               clc
000AFDr 3  65 F3            adc TEMP2+1                  ; dest address in TEMP2
000AFFr 3  85 F3            sta TEMP2+1
000B01r 3  60               rts
000B02r 3               
000B02r 3               ;
000B02r 3               HFE_END:
000B02r 3               ;
000B02r 3               ; end of hyf_edit.S
000B02r 3               ;
000B02r 3               
000B02r 2               HFE_EDIT_END:
000B02r 2               ;
000B02r 2               ;
000B02r 2               ROMCODEEND:                         ; end of all code in OS ROM
000B02r 2               ;
000B02r 2               ;....and here's the rest:
000B02r 2               ;       COPYTORAM, the RAM preamble, the heap, ftrain.s and bload.s
000B02r 2               ;
000B02r 2               ;------------------------------------------------------------------------
000B02r 2               .segment "FORTH_PAGED_ROM"
000000r 2               ;------------------------------------------------------------------------
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               ;    hyf_rom.s -- COPYTORAM
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               .include "hyf_rom.s"
000000r 3               ;
000000r 3               ;  COPYTORAM  - This HAS to sit at $A000 or the HyForth engine in OSROM will NOT find it.
000000r 3               ;    so....this always needs to be the first thing in the library image before the heap,
000000r 3               ;    therefore .org $A000, .include 'hyf_rom.s', .org $0800, ...(heap).
000000r 3               ;
000000r 3               COPYMAIN = COPYSTART
000000r 3               RAMST = RAMSTART
000000r 3               COPYENDS = ends - RAMSTART + COPYSTART
000000r 3               
000000r 3               COPYTORAM:
000000r 3  4C rr rr         jmp CTRSTART                     ; copies from mainoff thru endsoff to ramstart
000003r 3  rr               .byte >h_exit
000004r 3  rr               .byte <h_exit
000005r 3  rr               .byte >ends
000006r 3               CTRSTART:
000006r 3               ;
000006r 3               ;    the 10-23-26 1920 version
000006r 3               ;
000006r 3               ;    reworked to used ZP_TEMP_VEC, ZP_TEMP_VEC2, ZP_TEMP_VEC3
000006r 3               ;                     mainoff      endsoff       ramstart
000006r 3               ;
000006r 3  A9 0D 20 rr      WCRLF_np
00000Ar 3  rr A9 0A 20  
00000Er 3  rr rr        
000010r 3               ;    lda #1
000010r 3               ;    sta supprint               ; supress printing of progress
000010r 3  A9 A1            lda #>COPYMAIN
000012r 3  85 rr            sta ZP_TEMP_VEC+1
000014r 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_BYTE
000017r 3  A9 00            lda #<COPYMAIN
000019r 3  85 rr            sta ZP_TEMP_VEC
00001Br 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_BYTE
00001Er 3  A9 2B            lda #'+'
000020r 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
000023r 3  A9 21            lda #<COPYENDS
000025r 3  85 rr            sta ZP_TEMP_VEC2
000027r 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_BYTE
00002Ar 3  A9 B6            lda #>COPYENDS
00002Cr 3  85 rr            sta ZP_TEMP_VEC2+1
00002Er 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_BYTE
000031r 3                   ;
000031r 3  A9 rr            lda #>RAMST
000033r 3  85 rr            sta ZP_TEMP_VEC3+1
000035r 3  A9 rr            lda #<RAMST
000037r 3  85 rr            sta ZP_TEMP_VEC3
000039r 3  20 rr rr         jsr MEMCPY
00003Cr 3  A9 20            lda #$20
00003Er 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
000041r 3  A9 66            lda #'f'
000043r 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
000046r 3  A9 69            lda #'i'
000048r 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
00004Br 3  A9 6E            lda #'n'
00004Dr 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
000050r 3  A9 0D 20 rr      WCRLF_np
000054r 3  rr A9 0A 20  
000058r 3  rr rr        
00005Ar 3  60               rts
00005Br 3               
00005Br 3               
00005Br 2               ;
00005Br 2               ;
00005Br 2               COPYSTART := $A100              ; marks beginning of copy in ROM space
00005Br 2               ;-----------------------------------------------------------------------
00005Br 2               ;           BELOW ENDS UP IN RAM after COPYTORAM has run
00005Br 2               ;-----------------------------------------------------------------------
00005Br 2               .segment "FORTH_CODE"             ; at $0A00 now
000000r 2               RAMSTART:
000000r 2  4C rr rr         jmp forth_main             ; MAIN program start
000003r 2  4C rr rr         jmp reset
000006r 2               ;
000006r 2               ;        32 bytes reserved for HyFedit (see hyf_edit.s)
000006r 2               ;
000006r 2               HFE_RSV:
000006r 2               HFECOLS:        ; offsets to data in RAM
000006r 2  40               .byte $40    ; 64 columns w/ $00 or $09 in rightmost
000007r 2               HFETSIZE:
000007r 2               HFEROWS:
000007r 2  10               .byte $10    ; 16 rows - also use as length limit for title text
000008r 2               HFEBUF:
000008r 2  xx xx            .res 2       ; addr to buffer
00000Ar 2  xx xx xx xx      .res 6
00000Er 2  xx xx        
000010r 2               HFE_TITLE:
000010r 2  3C 2D            .byte "<-"
000012r 2               HFE_TTXT:
000012r 2  47 65 6E 65      .byte "Generic ScreenXX" ; exactly 16 bytes
000016r 2  72 69 63 20  
00001Ar 2  53 63 72 65  
000022r 2  2D 3E            .byte "->"
000024r 2  00 00            .byte 0, 0
000026r 2               ;--------------------------------------------------------------------
000026r 2               ;   startup autoload?
000026r 2               ;            load and run to 15 char command on startup
000026r 2               ;
000026r 2               STARTAUTO:
000026r 2  20 31 20 6C     .byte " 1 lib autoload"    ; for some reason works better with a space
00002Ar 2  69 62 20 61  
00002Er 2  75 74 6F 6C  
000035r 2  00              .byte 0
000036r 2               ;--------------------------------------------------------------------
000036r 2               ;      library jump table
000036r 2               ;
000036r 2               ;    see ftrain.s and bload.s for examples of library format details
000036r 2               ;
000036r 2               LASTLIB = 7
000036r 2               lib_jumptable:
000036r 2  5A B7            LIBENTRY (LT_FCORE + COPYSTART - RAMSTART)
000038r 2  0F BA            LIBENTRY (LT_FEXTRA + COPYSTART - RAMSTART)
00003Ar 2  B1 BB            LIBENTRY (LT_ANSI + COPYSTART - RAMSTART)
00003Cr 2  3E BD            LIBENTRY (LT_RAMTEST + COPYSTART - RAMSTART)
00003Er 2  6B BF            LIBENTRY (LT_BLOAD + COPYSTART - RAMSTART)
000040r 2  E4 BD            LIBENTRY (LT_EDITTEST + COPYSTART - RAMSTART)
000042r 2  86 BE            LIBENTRY (LT_SYSVARS + COPYSTART - RAMSTART)
000044r 2  00 00 00 00      .byte 0, 0, 0, 0   ; extra padding, dynamically add more libraries?
000048r 2  00 00 00 00      .byte 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0
00004Cr 2  00 00        
00004Er 2  00 00 00 00      .byte 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0    ; keep it to 16 entries if possible
000052r 2  00 00 00 00  
000056r 2               lib_jumps_end:
000056r 2               ;
000056r 2               ;--------------------------------------------------------------------
000056r 2               ;
000056r 2               ;    error/system msg jump table  - see upper.s for ERR messages
000056r 2               ;
000056r 2               LASTERR = 8
000056r 2               err_jumptable:
000056r 2  xx xx            .res 2                    ; padding for ???
000058r 2  rr rr            ERR_entry RPTR_ERR              ; RT stack full/empty  - error $01
00005Ar 2  rr rr            ERR_entry SPTR_ERR              ; DS stack full/empty  - error $02
00005Cr 2  rr rr            ERR_entry DIV_ERR               ; divide by zero - error $03
00005Er 2  rr rr            ERR_entry OOM_ERR               ; out of memory  - error $04
000060r 2  rr rr            ERR_entry UKW_ERR               ; no existing word - error $05
000062r 2  rr rr            ERR_entry SEC_ERR               ; writing to dangerous RAM areas - error $06
000064r 2  rr rr            ERR_entry SYS_ERR               ; error on return from SYSCALL - error $07
000066r 2  rr rr            ERR_entry PAD_ERR               ; PAD out of memory - error $08
000068r 2  00 00 00 00      .byte 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0   ; extra padding, dynamically add more messages
00006Cr 2  00 00        
00006Er 2  00 00 00 00      .byte 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0
000072r 2  00 00        
000074r 2  00 00            .byte 0, 0                     ; keep to 16 entries
000076r 2               err_jumps_end:
000076r 2               ;
000076r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000076r 2               ;        core and primitives - original AGSB hardcoded dictionary
000076r 2               ;
000076r 2               ;        loading order, what's where:
000076r 2               ;
000076r 2               ; HEAP
000076r 2               ;    core.s
000076r 2               ;  -----------------------------------------------------------
000076r 2               ;  @ ! lit [ ] , : :$ ; ;$ I .R .S xR xS ?R ?S
000076r 2               ;  dup swap rot over drop 0 to 9 $0 to $F %0 %1 < > <= >= = <>
000076r 2               ;  0= bool FALSE TRUE not neg and or nand xor
000076r 2               ;  + - key emit ord . .be .C spc cr c! c@ words w@
000076r 2               ;  in> >in rp sp x@ pp mp r> r> back here last
000076r 2               ;  bye exec reset abort debug
000076r 2               ;
000076r 2               ;    primitives.s - memory, printing, math
000076r 2               ;  ------------------------------------------------------------
000076r 2               ;  var cons malloc mlen mem@ pad@ pad! xpad
000076r 2               ;  hx2d dump .sz .rsz .psz .sn
000076r 2               ;  * / */ max min << >> cbit sbit tbit
000076r 2               ;
000076r 2               ;    hywords.s - ADDITIONS, HyFedit words, ANSI, memory extras
000076r 2               ;  -------------------------------------------------------------
000076r 2               ;  bload cload autoload lib libs free
000076r 2               ;  syscall disasm memcpy
000076r 2               ;  memw@ memwn@ wmemn! cmemn! rcmemn!
000076r 2               ;  rand rand32 rseed Acol Aclr Ascr
000076r 2               ;  ywrite ytest yinit
000076r 2               ;  ferc@ fecol! ferow! felin feadd feclr fedit febuf fedisp
000076r 2               ;  fa_mo@ fa_ms@
000076r 2               ;
000076r 2               ;  ---- and EXIT is always last ----
000076r 2               ;  exit
000076r 2               ;
000076r 2               ;
000076r 2               ; ROM and ENGINE
000076r 2               ;    upper.s
000076r 2               ;    hyf_edit.s
000076r 2               ;
000076r 2               ;----------------------------------------------------------------------
000076r 2               ;
000076r 2               ;  The 'primitive' primitives
000076r 2               ;
000076r 2               .include "core.s"
000076r 3               ;------------ CRITICAL CORE PRIMITIVES (AGSB and PGS) ----------------
000076r 3               ;   (the 'primitive' primitives)
000076r 3               ;  -----------------------------------------------------------
000076r 3               ;  @ ! lit [ ] , : :$ ; ;$ I .R .S xR xS ?R ?S
000076r 3               ;  dup swap rot over drop 0-9 $0-$F %0 %1 < > <= >= = <>
000076r 3               ;  0= bool FALSE TRUE not neg and or nand xor
000076r 3               ;  + - key emit ord . .be spc cr c! c@ words w@
000076r 3               ;  in> >in rp sp x@ pp mp r> r> back here last
000076r 3               ;  bye exec reset abort debug
000076r 3               ;  -----------------------------------------------------------
000076r 3               ;
000076r 3               ;   COMPILE (:), FINISH (;), FETCH (@), STORE (!)
000076r 3               ;      including:  keeps, this, next, finish
000076r 3               ;       ...and other stuff.
000076r 3               ;
000076r 3               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000076r 3               ;
000076r 3               ; ( -- ) ae exit forth
000076r 3  00 00 03 62  def_word "bye", "bye", 0
00007Ar 3  79 65        
00007Cr 3  A9 46            lda #'F'
00007Er 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
000081r 3  A9 57            lda #'W'
000083r 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
000086r 3  A9 2D            lda #'-'
000088r 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
00008Br 3  A9 rr            lda #>F_warm
00008Dr 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_BYTE
000090r 3  A9 rr            lda #<F_warm
000092r 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_BYTE
000095r 3  4C rr rr         jmp MON_START               ; jump to WOZMON
000098r 3               ;
000098r 3               ; ( -- ) ae abort
000098r 3  rr rr 05 61  def_word "abort", "abort_", 0
00009Cr 3  62 6F 72 74  
0000A0r 3  4C rr rr         jmp abort
0000A3r 3               ;
0000A3r 3               ; ( -- ) resets buffer to INBUF
0000A3r 3  rr rr 05 72  def_word "reset", "reset_", 0
0000A7r 3  65 73 65 74  
0000ABr 3  4C rr rr         jmp reset
0000AEr 3               ;
0000AEr 3               ; ( a -- ) execute a jump to a reference at top of data stack
0000AEr 3  rr rr 04 65  def_word "exec", "exec", 0
0000B2r 3  78 65 63     
0000B5r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_0
0000B8r 3  6C F0 00         jmp (TEMP1)        ; assumes an address on top of DS
0000BBr 3               
0000BBr 3               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
0000BBr 3               ; ( -- ) execute a jump to a reference at IP
0000BBr 3  rr rr 02 3A  def_word ":$", "docode", 0
0000BFr 3  24           
0000C0r 3  6C EC 00         jmp (INSTPTR)                 ;  DOCODE (thus the ':')
0000C3r 3               
0000C3r 3               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
0000C3r 3               ; ( -- ) execute a jump to next
0000C3r 3  rr rr 02 3B  def_word ";$", "donext", 0        ;  'next' (thus the ';')
0000C7r 3  24           
0000C8r 3  4C rr rr         jmp next
0000CBr 3               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
0000CBr 3               ;
0000CBr 3               ; ( w a -- ) ; [a] = w    (w is word, a is an address)
0000CBr 3  rr rr 01 21  def_word "!", "store", 0
0000CFr 3               storew:
0000CFr 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_1             ; get address, store in TEMP2
0000D2r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_0             ; get data, store in TEMP1
0000D5r 3  A2 F2            ldx #TEMP2              ;  [a]
0000D7r 3  A0 F0            ldy #TEMP1              ;   w
0000D9r 3  20 rr rr         jsr copyinto            ; copy TEMP2 stuff to addr in TEMP1 (opposite of @)..
0000DCr 3  4C rr rr         jmp next                ;            ...(see below)
0000DFr 3               ;
0000DFr 3               ;--------------------------------------------FETCH--------------------
0000DFr 3               ; ( a -- w ) ; w = [a]
0000DFr 3  rr rr 01 40  def_word "@", "fetch", 0      ; replace addr of data on top of DS, with data pointed to
0000E3r 3               fetchw:
0000E3r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_0             ; get addr from DS
0000E6r 3  A2 F0            ldx #TEMP1
0000E8r 3  A0 F2            ldy #TEMP2
0000EAr 3  20 rr rr         jsr copyfrom            ; copies data from [TEMP1] => TEMP2
0000EDr 3               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
0000EDr 3               ;            NEXT entry point for many AGSB primitives, and
0000EDr 3               ;            almost all other hard-coded words.
0000EDr 3               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
0000EDr 3                                           ; in fall through, this copies TEMP2 (in y) to TEMP1,
0000EDr 3                                           ; overwriting the address that was pulled from the stack.
0000EDr 3                                           ;
0000EDr 3                                           ; in theory, one could load y with any TEMPx and
0000EDr 3                                           ; call jump to 'copys' to finish off to 'next'.
0000EDr 3                                           ; in PRACTICE, AGSB didn't reuse this much.
0000EDr 3                                           ;
0000EDr 3               copys:                      ; copy from cell at y (zp) to TEMP1
0000EDr 3  B9 00 00         lda 0, y
0000F0r 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
0000F2r 3  B9 01 00         lda 1, y
0000F5r 3                                           ; 'keeps' is a shortcut to the 'second half' of the
0000F5r 3                                           ; (sta TEMP1) routine to return results; to push another
0000F5r 3                                           ; variable on the stack, do it explicitly.
0000F5r 3                                           ;
0000F5r 3                                           ; 'this' assumes 'keeps' has already been done.
0000F5r 3                                           ;  Almost every hard-coded word uses keeps, this, or next
0000F5r 3                                           ;  to end a word; 'next' ends with 'exit' of course.
0000F5r 3                                           ;
0000F5r 3               keeps:                      ; saves bytes since have to get here anyway
0000F5r 3  85 F1            sta TEMP1+1
0000F7r 3               this:                       ; same as above
0000F7r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spush_0             ; then push back on stack
0000FAr 3  4C rr rr         jmp next
0000FDr 3               ;
0000FDr 3               ;-----------------------IMMEDIATE, '[', ']', ','-----------------------
0000FDr 3  rr rr 01 49  def_word "I", "Imm", 0
000101r 3               wimm:                        ; jmp here if proccessing a compiled
000101r 3  A5 E5            lda LASTHEAP+1           ;   word that needs to run 'immediate'.
000103r 3  85 CD            sta TEMP9+1
000105r 3  A5 E4            lda LASTHEAP             ; get addr of 'last' compiled word, copy to TEMP4, add 2
000107r 3  18               clc
000108r 3  69 02            adc #2                   ; ..to find where length byte is...
00010Ar 3  85 CC            sta TEMP9
00010Cr 3  90 02            bcc IMMSKIP
00010Er 3  E6 CD            inc TEMP9+1
000110r 3               IMMSKIP:
000110r 3  A0 00            ldy #0
000112r 3  B1 CC            lda (TEMP9),y
000114r 3  09 80            ora #$80                 ; ...set bit 7 and store
000116r 3  91 CC            sta (TEMP9),y
000118r 3  4C rr rr         jmp next
00011Br 3               ;
00011Br 3  rr rr 81 5B  def_word "[", "leftbrack", FLAG_IMM       ; switch to 'interpret'
00011Fr 3  64 E0            stz STATUS
000121r 3  4C rr rr         jmp next
000124r 3               ;
000124r 3  rr rr 01 5D  def_word "]", "rtbrack", 0               ; switch back to 'compile'
000128r 3  A9 01            lda #1
00012Ar 3  85 E0            sta STATUS
00012Cr 3  4C rr rr         jmp next
00012Fr 3               ;
00012Fr 3               ;
00012Fr 3                                                        ; NOTE: 'lit' or similar will have pushed a value
00012Fr 3                                                        ; or address onto stack; 'comma' stores it inline in word
00012Fr 3                                                        ;
00012Fr 3  rr rr 01 2C  def_word ",", "xcomma", 0                ; pull data from top of stack, store at 'here' (NEXTHEAP)
000133r 3                                                        ; , increment 'here' to point to next cell.
000133r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_0                          ;
000136r 3  A0 00            ldy #0                               ;
000138r 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1
00013Ar 3  91 E6            sta (NEXTHEAP),y                     ; POP DS TO TEMP1, [here] = TEMP1
00013Cr 3  C8               iny
00013Dr 3  A5 F1            lda TEMP1+1
00013Fr 3  91 E6            sta (NEXTHEAP),y
000141r 3  A2 E6            ldx #NEXTHEAP                        ; here += 2
000143r 3  A9 02            lda #2
000145r 3  20 rr rr         jsr addwx
000148r 3  4C rr rr         jmp next
00014Br 3               
00014Br 3               ;--------------------------------------------SEMIS-----------------
00014Br 3  rr rr 81 3B  def_word ";", "semis",  FLAG_IMM
00014Fr 3  A5 FA            lda BACKHEAP
000151r 3  85 E4            sta LASTHEAP                ; bring back BACKHEAP to LASTHEAP
000153r 3  A5 FB            lda BACKHEAP + 1
000155r 3  85 E5            sta LASTHEAP + 1
000157r 3               
000157r 3  64 E0            stz STATUS                  ; set status to 'interpret' (presumably from 'compile')
000159r 3               
000159r 3               finish:                         ; compiled words must end with exit
000159r 3  A9 rr            lda #<exit
00015Br 3  85 EE            sta WORKREG
00015Dr 3  A9 rr            lda #>exit
00015Fr 3  85 EF            sta WORKREG + 1
000161r 3  20 rr rr         jsr wcomma                  ; change NEXTHEAP to point to addr of 'exit',
000164r 3                                               ; and make sure is last entry in code table for word...
000164r 3                                               ; as all good Forth compiled words should do.
000164r 3  4C rr rr         jmp next
000167r 3               ;
000167r 3               ;
000167r 3               ;------------------------------------------COMPILE------------------
000167r 3  rr rr 01 3A  def_word ":", "colon", 0
00016Br 3  A5 E6            lda NEXTHEAP
00016Dr 3  85 FA            sta BACKHEAP                ; backup NEXTHEAP to BACKHEAP
00016Fr 3  A5 E7            lda NEXTHEAP + 1
000171r 3  85 FB            sta BACKHEAP + 1
000173r 3               
000173r 3  A9 01            lda #1                      ; set status to 'compile'
000175r 3  85 E0            sta STATUS
000177r 3               
000177r 3               COMPHEADER:
000177r 3               ; copy LASTHEAP into (NEXTHEAP)
000177r 3  A0 E4            ldy #LASTHEAP
000179r 3  20 rr rr         jsr comma                    ; change NEXTHEAP to point to LASTHEAP  ('here' <= 'last')
00017Cr 3  20 rr rr         jsr token                    ; get first token, the name of new word
00017Fr 3  A0 00            ldy #0                       ; copy it to heap: length and name
000181r 3                                                ; code field comes with later proc
000181r 3               COMPLOOP:
000181r 3  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK), y
000183r 3  C9 20            cmp #$20
000185r 3  F0 0B            beq COMPEND
000187r 3  C0 00            cpy #0                       ; if this is the length field...
000189r 3  D0 02            bne COMCOPY
00018Br 3  09 40            ora #$40                     ; set bit six for 'compiled' word
00018Dr 3               COMCOPY:
00018Dr 3  91 E6            sta (NEXTHEAP), y
00018Fr 3  C8               iny
000190r 3  D0 EF            bne COMPLOOP
000192r 3               COMPEND:
000192r 3  98               tya                          ; and update NEXTHEAP  :  'here' incremented by length
000193r 3  A2 E6            ldx #(NEXTHEAP)
000195r 3  20 rr rr         jsr addwx                   ; 'here' now at CFA
000198r 3               
000198r 3               ;~~~~~~~~ all done....
000198r 3  4C rr rr         jmp next                     ; and then see below; compiled word will continue until ';'
00019Br 3               ;
00019Br 3               ;  That's the basic basics, now for the rest of the core...
00019Br 3               ;
00019Br 3               ;----------------------------------------------------------------------
00019Br 3               ;
00019Br 3               ;   DS and RT manipulation words
00019Br 3               ;
00019Br 3               
00019Br 3               
00019Br 3               
00019Br 3               ; ( -- ) ae list of data stack
00019Br 3  rr rr 02 2E  def_word ".S", "splist", 0      ; changed from %S
00019Fr 3  53           
0001A0r 3  A5 E8            lda DSPTR
0001A2r 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1

0008A2r 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
0008A4r 3  A5 F5            lda TEMP3+1
0008A6r 3  85 F1            sta TEMP1+1
0008A8r 3               
0008A8r 3               WORD_CONT:
0008A8r 3  A5 F2            lda TEMP2            ; update TEMP2 and TEMP3, advance in linked list
0008AAr 3  85 F4            sta TEMP3
0008ACr 3  A5 F3            lda TEMP2 + 1
0008AEr 3  85 F5            sta TEMP3 + 1
0008B0r 3  A0 00            ldy #0
0008B2r 3  B1 F4            lda (TEMP3), y
0008B4r 3  85 F2            sta TEMP2
0008B6r 3  C8               iny
0008B7r 3  B1 F4            lda (TEMP3), y
0008B9r 3  85 F3            sta TEMP2 + 1
0008BBr 3  A2 F4            ldx #(TEMP3)

0008C5r 3               WORD_END:

0008C6r 3  4C rr rr         jmp next
0008C9r 3               ;
0008C9r 3               ;  routines needed for 'words'
0008C9r 3               ;
0008C9r 3               ; print size and name
0008C9r 3               ;  TEMP1 has address of word-record + 2, y is
0008C9r 3               ; is keeping track of offset withi in.
0008C9r 3               ;
0008C9r 3               WRDSHOWNAME:
0008C9r 3  A9 3A            lda #':'
0008CBr 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
0008CEr 3               ;    lda (TEMP1), y
0008CEr 3               ;    jsr WRITE_BYTE       ; size and flag
0008CEr 3  A9 20            lda #$20
0008D0r 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
0008D3r 3  B1 F0            lda (TEMP1), y
0008D5r 3  29 3F            and #$3F           ; mask off top two bits
0008D7r 3  AA               tax                ; x now has length
0008D8r 3  85 FD            sta TEMP6          ; save length of name
0008DAr 3               
0008DAr 3               NAMELOOP:              ; name
0008DAr 3  C8               iny                ; first time through, (TEMP1),y advanced to first char of name
0008DBr 3  B1 F0            lda (TEMP1), y
0008DDr 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR

0008E1r 3  D0 F7            bne NAMELOOP
0008E3r 3  A9 20            lda #$20
0008E5r 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR

0008E9r 3               
0008E9r 3               ;----------------------------------------------------------------------
0008E9r 3               ; ( saddr -- waddr ) find a word by matching to string
0008E9r 3               ;
0008E9r 3               ;  TEMP5 will point to the following string in the input buffer
0008E9r 3               ; for now, you must match ALL chars in the name of the word; no partial
0008E9r 3               ; matches.  Addr of word will end up on stack.
0008E9r 3               ;
0008E9r 3               ; so this will be used like 'w@ words' which will return CFA of 'words'
0008E9r 3               ;  equivalent of Forth-79 word 'tick' (single quote)
0008E9r 3               ;
0008E9r 3  rr rr 02 77  def_word "w@", "wfind", 0
0008EDr 3  40           
0008EEr 3               WLOOKUP:
0008EEr 3  20 rr rr         jsr token            ; get following token
0008F1r 3  A5 F8            lda NXTTOK
0008F3r 3  85 FC            sta TEMP5            ;store addr of first char
0008F5r 3  A5 F9            lda NXTTOK + 1
0008F7r 3  85 FD            sta TEMP5 + 1
0008F9r 3               
0008F9r 3               ; load LASTHEAP
0008F9r 3  A5 E5            lda LASTHEAP + 1
0008FBr 3  85 F3            sta TEMP2 + 1
0008FDr 3  A5 E4            lda LASTHEAP
0008FFr 3  85 F2            sta TEMP2
000901r 3               
000901r 3               ; load NEXTHEAP
000901r 3  A5 E7            lda NEXTHEAP + 1
000903r 3  85 F5            sta TEMP3 + 1
000905r 3  A5 E6            lda NEXTHEAP
000907r 3  85 F4            sta TEMP3
000909r 3               
000909r 3               WMATCH_LOOP:
000909r 3  A5 F2            lda TEMP2       ; lsb linked list
00090Br 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
00090Dr 3  05 F3            ora TEMP2 + 1    ; check if TEMP2 = $0000, end if so
00090Fr 3  F0 3E            beq WMATCH_END
000911r 3  A5 F3            lda TEMP2 + 1    ; msb linked list
000913r 3  85 F1            sta TEMP1 + 1
000915r 3               
000915r 3               WMATCH_SKIP:               ; TEMP1 has address of word-record,
000915r 3               ;                          TEMP1) is link to previous record
000915r 3  A2 F0            ldx #TEMP1          ; advance TEMP1 to size + flag, name
000917r 3  A9 02            lda #2
000919r 3  20 rr rr         jsr addwx
00091Cr 3  A0 00            ldy #0
00091Er 3  20 rr rr         jsr WNAMEMATCH       ; check if match; carry set, YES
000921r 3  B0 37            bcs WMATCHFOUND
000923r 3               
000923r 3  C8               iny                  ; update TEMP1 again, point at CFA
000924r 3  98               tya
000925r 3  A2 F0            ldx #TEMP1
000927r 3  20 rr rr         jsr addwx
00092Ar 3  A5 F4            lda TEMP3           ; instead of printing refs, just advance TEMP1 to TEMP3
00092Cr 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
00092Er 3  A5 F5            lda TEMP3+1
000930r 3  85 F1            sta TEMP1+1
000932r 3               
000932r 3               WMATCH_CONT:
000932r 3  A5 F2            lda TEMP2            ; update TEMP2 and TEMP3, advance in linked list
000934r 3  85 F4            sta TEMP3
000936r 3  A5 F3            lda TEMP2 + 1
000938r 3  85 F5            sta TEMP3 + 1
00093Ar 3  A0 00            ldy #0
00093Cr 3  B1 F4            lda (TEMP3), y
00093Er 3  85 F2            sta TEMP2
000940r 3  C8               iny
000941r 3  B1 F4            lda (TEMP3), y
000943r 3  85 F3            sta TEMP2 + 1
000945r 3  A2 F4            ldx #(TEMP3)
000947r 3  A9 02            lda #2
000949r 3  20 rr rr         jsr addwx
00094Cr 3  4C rr rr         jmp WMATCH_LOOP
00094Fr 3               WMATCH_END:
00094Fr 3  64 F0            stz TEMP1
000951r 3  64 F1            stz TEMP1 + 1
000953r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spush_0
000956r 3  18               clc              ; clean return
000957r 3  4C rr rr         jmp next
00095Ar 3               
00095Ar 3               WMATCHFOUND:
00095Ar 3  20 rr rr         jsr spush_0      ; push address of record + 2
00095Dr 3  A0 00            ldy #0
00095Fr 3  B1 F0            lda (TEMP1),y
000961r 3  29 3F            and #$3F
000963r 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
000965r 3  64 F1            stz TEMP1 + 1
000967r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spush_0      ; push length
00096Ar 3  18               clc
00096Br 3  4C rr rr         jmp next
00096Er 3               ;
00096Er 3               ;  TEMP1 has addr of string to test, TEMP2 is current word-record addr + 2
00096Er 3               ;
00096Er 3               WNAMEMATCH:
00096Er 3  B1 F0            lda (TEMP1), y
000970r 3  29 3F            and #$3F
000972r 3  AA               tax
000973r 3               WMATCHLOOP:              ; name
000973r 3  C8               iny                ; first time through, (TEMP1),y advanced to first char of name
000974r 3  B1 FC            lda (TEMP5), y     ; addr of string
000976r 3  D1 F0            cmp (TEMP1), y     ; addr of current name
000978r 3  D0 05            bne  WMATCH_NO
00097Ar 3  CA               dex
00097Br 3  D0 F6            bne WMATCHLOOP
00097Dr 3  80 02            bra WMATCH_YES
00097Fr 3               WMATCH_NO:
00097Fr 3  18               clc
000980r 3  60               rts
000981r 3               WMATCH_YES:
000981r 3  38               sec
000982r 3  60               rts
000983r 3               ;
000983r 3               ;
000983r 3               ;  end of core.s

001596r 3                               ;.byte       $41, $41, $44, $44, $48, $48, $41, $41

00159Er 3

00159Er 3                               ;.byte       $51, $4A, $48, $58, $4A, $56, $4A

0015A2r 3  49 55 49     
0015A5r 3               
0015A5r 3               SOUND_TEST:
0015A5r 3                               ; Set basic FM patch (simple sine-like sound)
0015A5r 3  A2 20                        ldx         #$20
0015A7r 3  A9 C7                        lda         #$C7
0015A9r 3  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
0015ACr 3               
0015ACr 3  A2 80                        ldx         #$80
0015AEr 3  A9 1F                        lda         #$1F
0015B0r 3  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
0015B3r 3               
0015B3r 3  A2 E0                        ldx         #$E0
0015B5r 3  A9 0F                        lda         #$0F
0015B7r 3  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
0015BAr 3               
0015BAr 3  A2 21                        ldx         #$21
0015BCr 3  A9 C7                        lda         #$C7
0015BEr 3  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
0015C1r 3               
0015C1r 3  A2 81                        ldx         #$81
0015C3r 3  A9 1F                        lda         #$1F
0015C5r 3  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
0015C8r 3               
0015C8r 3  A2 E1                        ldx         #$E1
0015CAr 3  A9 0F                        lda         #$0F
0015CCr 3  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
0015CFr 3               
0015CFr 3  A9 00                        lda         #$00    ; Channel 0
0015D1r 3  A2 rr                        ldx         #<GUITAR_PATCH
0015D3r 3  A0 rr                        ldy         #>GUITAR_PATCH
0015D5r 3  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_LOADPATCH
0015D8r 3               
0015D8r 3  A9 01                        lda         #$01    ; Channel 1
0015DAr 3  A2 rr                        ldx         #<GUITAR_PATCH
0015DCr 3  A0 rr                        ldy         #>GUITAR_PATCH
0015DEr 3  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_LOADPATCH
0015E1r 3               
0015E1r 3  A2 60                        ldx         #$60    ; Total Level (volume) for operator 1, Channel 0
0015E3r 3  A9 00                        lda         #$00    ; Max Volume
0015E5r 3  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
0015E8r 3               
0015E8r 3  A2 61                        ldx         #$61    ; Total Level (volume) for operator 1, Channel 1
0015EAr 3  A9 00                        lda         #$00    ; Max Volume
0015ECr 3  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
0015EFr 3               
0015EFr 3  A9 rr                        lda         #<SCOM_NOTES_0
0015F1r 3  85 rr                        sta         YMN0L
0015F3r 3  A9 rr                        lda         #>SCOM_NOTES_0
0015F5r 3  85 rr                        sta         YMN0H
0015F7r 3               
0015F7r 3  A9 rr                        lda         #<SCOM_NOTES_1
0015F9r 3  85 rr                        sta         YMN1L
0015FBr 3  A9 rr                        lda         #>SCOM_NOTES_1
0015FDr 3  85 rr                        sta         YMN1H
0015FFr 3               
0015FFr 3  A0 00                        ldy         #0          ; counter for channel 0
001601r 3               @play_loop:
001601r 3                               ; 0. set outer counter to 0
001601r 3                               ; 1. key off channel 0
001601r 3                               ; 1a.  key off on channel 1
001601r 3                               ; 2. set note to next note in channel 0 list
001601r 3                               ; 3. key on on channel 0
001601r 3                               ; 4. one delay
001601r 3                               ; 5. notes for rest of sequence on channel 1
001601r 3                               ; 5a.  reset counter to 0
001601r 3                               ; 5b.  key off on channel 1
001601r 3                               ; 5c.  set note on channel 1
001601r 3                               ; 5d.  key on on channel 1
001601r 3                               ; 5e.  one delay
001601r 3                               ; 5f.  increment counter
001601r 3                               ; 5g.  loop until 7 notes have been played on channel 1
001601r 3                               ; 6.  increment outer counter to play next start note on channel 0
001601r 3                               ; 7.  loop back to 1 until outer count reaches 8 (8 sequences have played)
001601r 3                               ; Key Off (silence previous note)
001601r 3               
001601r 3  A2 08                        ldx         #$08    ; Key On/Off register
001603r 3  A9 00                        lda         #$00    ; CH0 off
001605r 3  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
001608r 3               
001608r 3  A2 08                        ldx         #$08    ; Key On/Off register
00160Ar 3  A9 01                        lda         #$01    ; CH1 off
00160Cr 3  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
00160Fr 3               
00160Fr 3                               ; Set frequency (KC)
00160Fr 3  A2 28                        ldx         #$28    ; KC register for CH0
001611r 3  B1 rr                        lda         (YMN0L),y
001613r 3  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
001616r 3               
001616r 3                               ; Key On
001616r 3  A2 08                        ldx         #$08    ; Key On/Off register
001618r 3  A9 78                        lda         #$78    ; CH0 on
00161Ar 3  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
00161Dr 3               
00161Dr 3                               ; Delay 8 64th notes in length
00161Dr 3  A9 08                        lda         #8
00161Fr 3  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_DELAY_64
001622r 3               
001622r 3  5A                           phy
001623r 3  A0 00                        ldy         #0
001625r 3  80 07                        bra         @skip_ch1_off   ; already off at the start of the loop
001627r 3               
001627r 3               @next_ch1:
001627r 3  A2 08                        ldx         #$08    ; Key On/Off register
001629r 3  A9 01                        lda         #$01    ; CH1 off
00162Br 3  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
00162Er 3               
00162Er 3               @skip_ch1_off:
00162Er 3                               ; Set frequency (KC)
00162Er 3  A2 29                        ldx         #$29    ; KC register for CH1
001630r 3  B1 rr                        lda         (YMN1L),y
001632r 3  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
001635r 3               
001635r 3                               ; Key On
001635r 3  A2 08                        ldx         #$08    ; Key On/Off register
001637r 3  A9 79                        lda         #$79    ; CH1 on
001639r 3  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
00163Cr 3               
00163Cr 3                               ; Delay 8 64th notes in length
00163Cr 3  A9 08                        lda         #8
00163Er 3  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_DELAY_64
001641r 3               
001641r 3  C8                           iny
001642r 3  C0 07                        cpy         #7
001644r 3  D0 E1                        bne         @next_ch1
001646r 3               
001646r 3                               ; Next note on CH0
001646r 3  7A                           ply                 ; pull CH0 count
001647r 3  C8                           iny
001648r 3  C0 08                        cpy         #8      ; 8 bars
00164Ar 3  D0 B5                        bne         @play_loop
00164Cr 3               
00164Cr 3                               ; Stop sound
00164Cr 3  A2 08                        ldx         #$08
00164Er 3  A9 00                        lda         #$00
001650r 3  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
001653r 3               
001653r 3  A2 08                        ldx         #$08
001655r 3  A9 01                        lda         #$01
001657r 3  4C rr rr                     jmp         YM_WRITE
00165Ar 3               
00165Ar 3

00165Ar 3               ; end of sound.s

00165Ar 3               ;
00165Ar 3               ;  training data
00165Ar 3               ;
00165Ar 3               ;
00165Ar 3               ;    LIBRARY header in standard format (reachable from lib_jumptable entry)
00165Ar 3               ;
00165Ar 3               ;    LT_xxxx label, followed by 'sz' string, followed by 'C' or 'B' type
00165Ar 3               ;    ..and then calculated ROM address of start of library, bigendian.
00165Ar 3               ;
00165Ar 3               ;    Library entries can be ':' definitions or regular commands.
00165Ar 3               ;
00165Ar 3               ;   the 'sys_word' macro only works with names that are 'defined' labels
00165Ar 3               ;    i.e. NOT labels for actual physical addresses.   LBLX = <number> vs LBLX:
00165Ar 3               ;
00165Ar 3               .macro sys_word name
00165Ar 3               makelabel "sv_", name
00165Ar 3                 .byte .sprintf( "%i cons s_%s", .ident (name), name )
00165Ar 3                 .byte 0
00165Ar 3               .endmacro

001DF1r 3               ;
001DF1r 3               ;    examples of how to inject labeled memory addresses (which aren't available in macros)
001DF1r 3               ;
001DF1r 3  24           .byte "$"
001DF2r 3  46           .byte ((.lobyte(TEMP1) / 16) <= 9 ) * ((.lobyte(TEMP1) / 16) | $30) + ((.lobyte(TEMP1) / 16) > 9 ) * ((.lobyte(TEMP1) / 16) + $37)
001DF3r 3  30           .byte ((.lobyte(TEMP1) & $0F) <= 9 ) * ((.lobyte(TEMP1) & $0F) | $30) + ((.lobyte(TEMP1) & $0F) > 9 ) * ((.lobyte(TEMP1) & $0F) + $37)
001DF4r 3  20 63 6F 6E  .byte " cons s_T1"
001DF8r 3  73 20 73 5F  
001DFCr 3  54 31        
001DFEr 3  00           .byte 0
001DFFr 3  24           .byte "$"
001E00r 3  46           .byte ((.lobyte(TEMP2) / 16) <= 9 ) * ((.lobyte(TEMP2) / 16) | $30) + ((.lobyte(TEMP2) / 16) > 9 ) * ((.lobyte(TEMP2) / 16) + $37)
001E01r 3  32           .byte ((.lobyte(TEMP2) & $0F) <= 9 ) * ((.lobyte(TEMP2) & $0F) | $30) + ((.lobyte(TEMP2) & $0F) > 9 ) * ((.lobyte(TEMP2) & $0F) + $37)
001E02r 3  20 63 6F 6E  .byte " cons s_T2"
001E06r 3  73 20 73 5F  
001E0Ar 3  54 32
