001E6Br 2               ;
001E6Br 2               ;-----------------------------------------------------------------------
001E6Br 2               ;                            BINARY LIBRARIES
001E6Br 2               ;
001E6Br 2               .include "bload.s"
001E6Br 3               ;
001E6Br 3               ;  BLOAD test code
001E6Br 3               ;
001E6Br 3               ;  ALL binary libraries loadable with 'bload' MUST be Forth words,
001E6Br 3               ;   (not jsr subroutines or jmp destinations), fully relocatable.  That means NO direct jmp/jsr's
001E6Br 3               ;   to internal labels.  Indirect jmps ($6C / $7C) to local locations can be used
001E6Br 3               ;   once the word is loaded, with smart code.   Branches will work fine.
001E6Br 3               ;
001E6Br 3               ;   jsr's and jmp's to core primitive labels (next, keeps, this, etc) or
001E6Br 3               ;   OS ROM ($E000 - $FFFF) locations will also work.
001E6Br 3               ;
001E6Br 3               ;   These words will be loaded into the RAM heap, so there CAN be self-modding
001E6Br 3               ;   sections that will help words to configure themselves.  Be creative!
001E6Br 3               ;
001E6Br 3               ;  Format below must be rigidly followed, with length of name followed
001E6Br 3               ;   by that many bytes for the name.  'bit #0' ($89 $00) or $22 $00 (undocumented)
001E6Br 3               ;   must be the first instruction in any native code word loaded past
001E6Br 3               ;   'exit'.  Termination must be $00 $00 for each word, with "END"
001E6Br 3               ;   to designate the end of a 'library'.
001E6Br 3               ;
001E6Br 3               ;    LIBRARY header in standard format (reachable from lib_jumptable entry)
001E6Br 3               ;
001E6Br 3               ;    LT_xxxx label, followed by 'sz' string, followed by 'C' or 'B' type
001E6Br 3               ;    ..and then calculated ROM address of start of first loadable word, bigendian.
001E6Br 3               ;
001E6Br 3               LT_BLOAD:
001E6Br 3  42 4C 4F 41  .byte "BLOAD"
001E6Fr 3  44           
001E70r 3  00           .byte 0
001E71r 3  42           .byte "B"
001E72r 3  BF           .byte >(bload0 + COPYSTART - RAMSTART)
001E73r 3  74           .byte <(bload0 + COPYSTART - RAMSTART)
001E74r 3               bload0:
001E74r 3               ;
001E74r 3  06           .byte 6           ; length of name
001E75r 3  42 4C 74 65  .byte "BLtest"
001E79r 3  73 74        
001E7Br 3  22                .byte $22
001E7Cr 3  00                .byte 0
001E7Dr 3  48 A9 0D 20       WCRLF
001E81r 3  rr rr A9 0A  
001E85r 3  20 rr rr 68  
001E89r 3  A9 54             lda #'T'
001E8Br 3  20 rr rr          jsr WRITE_CHAR
001E8Er 3  A9 45             lda  #'E'
001E90r 3  20 rr rr          jsr WRITE_CHAR
001E93r 3  A9 53             lda  #'S'
001E95r 3  20 rr rr          jsr WRITE_CHAR
001E98r 3  A9 54             lda  #'T'
001E9Ar 3  20 rr rr          jsr WRITE_CHAR
001E9Dr 3  48 A9 0D 20       WCRLF
001EA1r 3  rr rr A9 0A  
001EA5r 3  20 rr rr 68  
001EA9r 3  4C rr rr          jmp next
001EACr 3  00 00        .byte 0, 0
001EAEr 3  07           .byte 7           ; length of name
001EAFr 3  42 4C 74 65  .byte "BLtest2"
001EB3r 3  73 74 32     
001EB6r 3  22                .byte $22
001EB7r 3  00                .byte 0
001EB8r 3  48 A9 0D 20       WCRLF
001EBCr 3  rr rr A9 0A  
001EC0r 3  20 rr rr 68  
001EC4r 3  A9 54             lda #'T'
001EC6r 3  20 rr rr          jsr WRITE_CHAR
001EC9r 3  A9 45             lda #'E'
001ECBr 3  20 rr rr          jsr WRITE_CHAR
001ECEr 3  A9 53             lda #'S'
001ED0r 3  20 rr rr          jsr WRITE_CHAR
001ED3r 3  A9 54             lda #'T'
001ED5r 3  20 rr rr          jsr WRITE_CHAR
001ED8r 3  A9 59             lda #'Y'
001EDAr 3  20 rr rr          jsr WRITE_CHAR
001EDDr 3  48 A9 0D 20       WCRLF
001EE1r 3  rr rr A9 0A  
001EE5r 3  20 rr rr 68  
001EE9r 3  4C rr rr          jmp next
001EECr 3  00 00        .byte 0, 0
001EEEr 3  45 4E 44     .byte "END"                  ; required to end library!
001EF1r 3               bload0_end:
001EF1r 3               
001EF1r 2               ;
001EF1r 2               ;  end of hyforth.s
001EF1r 2               ;
001EF1r 2               
001EF1r 1               .include "wozmon.s"
001EF1r 2               ;
001EF1r 2               ;   Taken mostly from Steve Wozniak's Apple 1 Monitor for the 6502, or WOZMON
001EF1r 2               ;
001EF1r 2               .debuginfo
001EF1r 2               ;
001EF1r 2               ;          No longer here; renamed WOZ_IN, moved to bios.s
001EF1r 2               ;
001EF1r 2               ;.segment "BUFFERS"
001EF1r 2               ;IN:
001EF1r 2               ;                .res            $100
001EF1r 2               ;
001EF1r 2               .segment "WOZMON"
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               ; usually ends up at $FE00  (see os_rom_C02.cfg)
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               ; WOZMON Entrypoint
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               MON_START:
000000r 2  D8                           cld                             ; Clear decimal arithmetic mode.
000001r 2  58                           cli                             ; Enable interrupts
000002r 2  64 rr                        stz             ZP_D_STATE      ; Byte mode, not DISAM mode
000004r 2  80 0B                        bra             @is_start
000006r 2               
000006r 2               @not_cr:
000006r 2  C9 08                        cmp             #$08            ; backspace
000008r 2  F0 16                        beq             @is_backspace
00000Ar 2  C9 1B                        cmp             #$1B            ; escape
00000Cr 2  F0 03                        beq             @is_start
00000Er 2  C8                           iny                             ; Advance text index.
00000Fr 2  10 12                        bpl             @get_next_char  ; Auto ESC if line longer than 127.
000011r 2               
000011r 2               @is_start:
000011r 2  A9 0D                        lda #$0D
000013r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr WRITE_CHAR
000016r 2  A9 0A                        lda #$0A
000018r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr WRITE_CHAR
00001Br 2               
00001Br 2               @get_line:
00001Br 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             WRITE_PROMPT
00001Er 2  A0 01                        ldy             #1              ; Initialize text index.
000020r 2               
000020r 2               @is_backspace:
000020r 2  88                           dey                             ; Back up text index.
000021r 2  30 F8                        bmi             @get_line       ; Beyond start of line, reinitialize.
000023r 2               
000023r 2               @get_next_char:
000023r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             READ_CHAR
000026r 2  90 FB                        bcc             @get_next_char
000028r 2  99 rr rr                     sta             WOZ_IN,y            ; Add to text buffer.
00002Br 2  C9 0D                        cmp             #$0D
00002Dr 2  D0 D7                        bne             @not_cr
00002Fr 2  A0 FF                        ldy             #$FF            ; Reset text index.  Will iny shortly...
000031r 2  A9 00                        lda             #$00            ; For ZP_WM_XAM mode.
000033r 2  AA                           tax                             ; .X=0.
000034r 2               
000034r 2               @set_block:
000034r 2  0A                           asl
000035r 2               
000035r 2               @set_store:
000035r 2  0A                           asl                             ; Leaves $7B if setting STOR mode.
000036r 2               
000036r 2               @set_mode:
000036r 2  85 rr                        sta             ZP_WM_MODE      ; $00 = ZP_WM_XAM, $74 = STOR, $B8 = BLOK ZP_WM_XAM.
000038r 2               
000038r 2               @skip_delim:
000038r 2  C8                           iny                             ; Advance text index.
000039r 2               
000039r 2               @next_item:
000039r 2  B9 rr rr                     lda             WOZ_IN,y            ; Get character.
00003Cr 2  C9 0D                        cmp             #$0D         ; CR?
00003Er 2  F0 DB                        beq             @get_line       ; Yes, done with this line.
000040r 2  C9 2E                        cmp             #'.'
000042r 2  90 F4                        bcc             @skip_delim     ; Skip delimiter.
000044r 2  F0 EE                        beq             @set_block      ; Set BLOCK ZP_WM_XAM mode.
000046r 2  C9 3A                        cmp             #':'
000048r 2  F0 EB                        beq             @set_store      ; Yes, set STOR mode.
00004Ar 2  C9 4D                        cmp             #'M'
00004Cr 2  10 0A                        bpl             @check_rtow
00004Er 2  C9 4B                        cmp             #'K'
000050r 2  30 0E                        bmi             @nothing
000052r 2  49 4B                        eor             #'K'         ; 'K' resets DASTATE to 0
000054r 2  85 rr                        sta             ZP_D_STATE
000056r 2  80 E0                        bra             @skip_delim
000058r 2               
000058r 2               @check_rtow:
000058r 2  C9 52                        cmp             #'R'
00005Ar 2  F0 3E                        beq             @run_prog
00005Cr 2  80 02                        bra  @nothing
00005Er 2               ;                bcc             @not_tuvw
00005Er 2               ;                cmp             #'X'        ; R, S, T, U, V, or W
00005Er 2               ;                bcs             @not_tuvw
00005Er 2               ;                cmp             #'S'               ; no 'SPAWN' for now
00005Er 2               ;                bne             @not_spawn
00005Er 2               ;                jmp             SPAWN_TASK        ; see bios.s - do nothing
00005Er 2               ;
00005Er 2               ;@not_spawn:                            ; TUVW falls through to here
00005Er 2               ;                clc
00005Er 2               ;                adc             #($F0 - 'T'); T=FFF0, U=FFF1, V=FFF2, W=FFF3
00005Er 2               ;                sta             ZP_WM_HVP       ;
00005Er 2               ;                lda             #$FF            ;
00005Er 2               ;                sta             ZP_WM_HVP + 1   ;
00005Er 2               ;                iny                             ; skip the mnemonic
00005Er 2               ;               bra             @not_hex_or_escape
00005Er 2               
00005Er 2               @bra_is_start:
00005Er 2  80 B1                        bra             @is_start       ; can't branch from top to botthom w/o this
000060r 2               
000060r 2               ;@not_tuvw:
000060r 2               @nothing:
000060r 2  84 rr                        sty             ZP_Y_SAVE       ; Save Y for comparison
000062r 2  86 rr                        stx             ZP_WM_HVP       ; $00 -> Low byte of HPV
000064r 2  86 rr                        stx             ZP_WM_HVP + 1   ; ...and High byte.
000066r 2               
000066r 2               @next_hex:
000066r 2  B9 rr rr                     lda             WOZ_IN,y            ; Get character for hex test.
000069r 2  49 30                        eor             #'0'            ; Map digits to $0-9.
00006Br 2  C9 0A                        cmp             #$0A            ; Digit?
00006Dr 2  90 06                        bcc             @is_digit       ; Yes.
00006Fr 2  69 88                        adc             #$88            ; Map letter "A"-"F" to $FA-FF.
000071r 2  C9 FA                        cmp             #$FA            ; Hex letter?
000073r 2  90 11                        bcc             @not_hex        ; No, character not hex.
000075r 2               
000075r 2               @is_digit:
000075r 2  0A                           asl                             ; LSD to MSD of A.
000076r 2  0A                           asl
000077r 2  0A                           asl
000078r 2  0A                           asl
000079r 2  A2 04                        ldx              #4              ; Shift count.
00007Br 2               
00007Br 2               @hex_shift:
00007Br 2  0A                           asl                             ; Hex digit left, MSB to carry.
00007Cr 2  26 rr                        rol             ZP_WM_HVP       ; Rotate into LSD.
00007Er 2  26 rr                        rol             ZP_WM_HVP + 1   ; Rotate into MSD's.
000080r 2  CA                           dex                             ; Done 4 shifts?
000081r 2  D0 F8                        bne             @hex_shift      ; No, loop.
000083r 2  C8                           iny                             ; Advance text index.
000084r 2  D0 E0                        bne             @next_hex       ; Always taken. Check next character for hex.
000086r 2               
000086r 2               @not_hex:
000086r 2  C4 rr                        cpy             ZP_Y_SAVE       ; Check if HPV empty (no hex digits).
000088r 2  F0 D4                        beq             @bra_is_start   ; Yes, back to start.
00008Ar 2               
00008Ar 2               @not_hex_or_escape:
00008Ar 2  24 rr                        bit             ZP_WM_MODE      ; Test ZP_WM_MODE byte.
00008Cr 2  50 15                        bvc             @not_store      ; B6=0 is STOR, 1 is XAM and BLOCK XAM.
00008Er 2  A5 rr                        lda             ZP_WM_HVP       ; LSD's of hex data.
000090r 2  92 rr                        sta             (ZP_WM_ST)      ; Store to current 'store index'.
000092r 2  E6 rr                        inc             ZP_WM_ST        ; Increment store index.
000094r 2  D0 A3                        bne             @next_item      ; Get next item (no carry).
000096r 2  E6 rr                        inc             ZP_WM_ST + 1    ; Add carry to 'store index' high order.
000098r 2               
000098r 2               @to_next_item:
000098r 2  80 9F                        bra             @next_item      ; Get next command item.
00009Ar 2               
00009Ar 2               @run_prog:
00009Ar 2  A9 rr                        lda             #>(MON_START-1)   ; push Wozmon entry point on stack
00009Cr 2  48                           pha
00009Dr 2  A9 rr                        lda             #<(MON_START-1)
00009Fr 2  48                           pha
0000A0r 2  6C rr rr                     jmp             (ZP_WM_XAM)       ; then jump to program
0000A3r 2               @not_store:
0000A3r 2  30 29                        bmi             @examine_next   ; B7 = 0 for XAM, 1 for BLOCK XAM.
0000A5r 2  A2 02                        ldx             #2              ; Byte count.
0000A7r 2               
0000A7r 2               @set_addr:
0000A7r 2  B5 rr                        lda             ZP_WM_HVP - 1,x ; Copy hex data to
0000A9r 2  95 rr                        sta             ZP_WM_ST - 1,x  ;   'store index'.
0000ABr 2  95 rr                        sta             ZP_WM_XAM - 1,x ; And to 'ZP_WM_XAM index'.
0000ADr 2  CA                           dex                             ; Next of 2 bytes.
0000AEr 2  D0 F7                        bne             @set_addr       ; Loop unless X = 0.
0000B0r 2               
0000B0r 2               @print_next_addr:
0000B0r 2  A9 0D                        lda #$0D
0000B2r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr WRITE_CHAR
0000B5r 2  A9 0A                        lda #$0A
0000B7r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr WRITE_CHAR
0000BAr 2  A5 rr                        lda ZP_WM_XAM+1
0000BCr 2  20 rr rr                     jsr WRITE_BYTE
0000BFr 2  A5 rr                        lda ZP_WM_XAM                    ; Print 'examine index'
0000C1r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr WRITE_BYTE
0000C4r 2  A9 3A                        lda #':'
0000C6r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr WRITE_CHAR                   ; ...+ COLON
0000C9r 2               @print_data:
0000C9r 2  5A                           phy
0000CAr 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             DISASM_WM       ; DISASM
0000CDr 2  7A                           ply
0000CEr 2               
0000CEr 2               @examine_next:
0000CEr 2  64 rr                        stz             ZP_WM_MODE      ; 0 -> ZP_WM_MODE (XAM mode).
0000D0r 2  A5 rr                        lda             ZP_WM_XAM
0000D2r 2  C5 rr                        cmp             ZP_WM_HVP       ; Compare 'examine index' to hex data.
0000D4r 2  A5 rr                        lda             ZP_WM_XAM + 1
0000D6r 2  E5 rr                        sbc             ZP_WM_HVP + 1
0000D8r 2  B0 BE                        bcs             @to_next_item   ; Not less, so no more data to output.
0000DAr 2  38                           sec
0000DBr 2  A5 rr                        lda             ZP_D_EXBYTES
0000DDr 2  65 rr                        adc             ZP_WM_XAM
0000DFr 2  85 rr                        sta             ZP_WM_XAM
0000E1r 2  90 02                        bcc             :+
0000E3r 2  E6 rr                        inc             ZP_WM_XAM + 1
0000E5r 2               :
0000E5r 2  A5 rr                        lda             ZP_D_STATE      ; if disassembling, always print the address
0000E7r 2  D0 C7                        bne             @print_next_addr
0000E9r 2  A5 rr                        lda             ZP_WM_XAM       ; Check low-order 'examine index' byte
0000EBr 2  29 07                        and             #7              ; For MOD 8 = 0
0000EDr 2  F0 C1                        beq             @print_next_addr
0000EFr 2  80 D8                        bra             @print_data
0000F1r 2               ;
0000F1r 2               ;          end of wozmon.s
0000F1r 2               ;
0000F1r 2               
0000F1r 1               ;.include "mmu.s"
0000F1r 1               .include "shell.s"
0000F1r 2               .debuginfo
0000F1r 2               .segment "SHELL"
000000r 2               
000000r 2               SHELL_MAIN:
000000r 2  20 rr rr                  jsr COPYTORAM                  ; COPYTORAM
000003r 2  4C rr rr                  jmp RAMSTART                   ; forth_main -> F_cold
000006r 2                ;           jsr     MON_START
000006r 2               
000006r 1               .include "utes.s"
000006r 2               ;
000006r 2               ;  utes.s        -   math routines, Forth/OS utilities, sound init/core
000006r 2               ;
000006r 2               .debuginfo
000006r 2               .segment "UTES"
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               ; usually ends up at $E400 (see os_rom_C02.cfg)
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               ;-----------------------------------------------------------------------
000000r 2               ;SOUND_INIT, YM_LOADPATCH, YM_WRITE
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               ; zero out all YM-2151 registers $28-$FF
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               SOUND_INIT:
000000r 2  48                           pha
000001r 2  DA                           phx
000002r 2  A9 00                        lda         #0
000004r 2  A2 14                        ldx         #$14        ; turn off the clocks
000006r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
000009r 2  A2 28                        ldx         #$28
00000Br 2               
00000Br 2               @write_z:
00000Br 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
00000Er 2  B0 0D                        bcs         @error
000010r 2  E8                           inx
000011r 2  D0 F8                        bne         @write_z
000013r 2  A2 03                        ldx         #IRQ_NUMBER_ONBOARD_SOUND
000015r 2  A9 rr                        lda         #<SOUND_IRQ_HANDLER
000017r 2  A0 rr                        ldy         #>SOUND_IRQ_HANDLER
000019r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         IRQ_SET_VECTOR
00001Cr 2  18                           clc
00001Dr 2               
00001Dr 2               @error:
00001Dr 2  FA                           plx
00001Er 2  68                           pla
00001Fr 2  60                           rts
000020r 2               
000020r 2               
000020r 2               SOUND_IRQ_HANDLER:
000020r 2                               ; check which
000020r 2  40                           rti
000021r 2               ;
000021r 2               ;         zero page stuff for sound, in zero.s
000021r 2               ;YMN0L = ZP_TEMP_VEC3
000021r 2               ;YMN0H = YMN0L + 1
000021r 2               ;YMN1L = ZP_TEMP_VEC4
000021r 2               ;YMN1H = YMN1L + 1
000021r 2               ;
000021r 2               ;AZP0L = ZP_TEMP_VEC
000021r 2               ;AZP0H = AZP0L + 1
000021r 2               ;YMTMP1 = ZP_TEMP_VEC2
000021r 2               ;YMTMP2 = YMTMP1 + 1
000021r 2               ;
000021r 2               YM_TIMEOUT = 64
000021r 2               
000021r 2               ; Write value in A to YM-2151 register in X
000021r 2               YM_WRITE:
000021r 2  78                           sei
000022r 2  5A                           phy
000023r 2  A0 40                        ldy         #YM_TIMEOUT
000025r 2               
000025r 2               @ym_wait1:
000025r 2  88                           dey
000026r 2  30 18                        bmi         @timeout
000028r 2  2C rr rr                     bit         YM_DATA
00002Br 2  30 F8                        bmi         @ym_wait1
00002Dr 2  8E rr rr                     stx         YM_REG
000030r 2  A0 40                        ldy         #YM_TIMEOUT
000032r 2               
000032r 2               @ym_wait2:
000032r 2  88                           dey
000033r 2  30 0B                        bmi         @timeout
000035r 2  2C rr rr                     bit         YM_DATA
000038r 2  30 F8                        bmi         @ym_wait2
00003Ar 2  8D rr rr                     sta         YM_DATA
00003Dr 2  18                           clc
00003Er 2  80 01                        bra         @cleanup
000040r 2               
000040r 2               @timeout:
000040r 2  38                           sec
000041r 2               
000041r 2               @cleanup:
000041r 2  7A                           ply
000042r 2  58                           cli
000043r 2  60                           rts
000044r 2               
000044r 2               YM_LOADPATCH:
000044r 2                               ; Make re-entrant safe by protecting tmp and pointer variables from interrupt
000044r 2  08                           php
000045r 2  78                           sei
000046r 2               
000046r 2                               ; and #$07 ; mask channel to range 0..7
000046r 2  86 rr                        stx AZP0L
000048r 2  84 rr                        sty AZP0H
00004Ar 2  18                           clc
00004Br 2  69 20                        adc #$20 ; first byte of patch goes to YM:$20+channel
00004Dr 2  AA                           tax
00004Er 2               
00004Er 2  B2 rr                        lda (AZP0L)
000050r 2  29 3F                        and #$3F
000052r 2               
000052r 2                               ;sta ymtmp1
000052r 2                               ;lda ymshadow,x
000052r 2                               ;and #$C0 ; L+R bits for YM channel
000052r 2                               ;ora ymtmp1 ; Add the patch byte without L+R
000052r 2  09 C0                        ora #$C0;
000054r 2               
000054r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr YM_WRITE
000057r 2  B0 16                        bcs @fail
000059r 2  A0 00                        ldy #0
00005Br 2  8A                           txa         ; YM_WRITE preserves X (YM register)
00005Cr 2                               ; Now skip over $28 and $30 by adding $10 to the register address.
00005Cr 2                               ; C guaranteed clear by successful ym_write
00005Cr 2  69 10                        adc #$10
00005Er 2  AA                           tax         ; set up for loop
00005Fr 2               @next:
00005Fr 2  8A                           txa
000060r 2                               ; C guaranteed clear by successful YM_WRITE
000060r 2  69 08                        adc #$08
000062r 2  B0 0E                        bcs @success
000064r 2  C8                           iny
000065r 2  AA                           tax
000066r 2  B1 rr                        lda (AZP0L),y
000068r 2  5A                           phy         ; YM_WRITE clobbers .Y
000069r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr YM_WRITE
00006Cr 2  7A                           ply
00006Dr 2  90 F0                        bcc @next
00006Fr 2               @fail:
00006Fr 2  28                           plp         ; restore interrupt flag
000070r 2  38                           sec
000071r 2  60                           rts         ; return C set as failed patch write.
000072r 2               @success:
000072r 2  28                           plp         ; restore interrupt flag
000073r 2  18                           clc
000074r 2  60                           rts
000075r 2               
000075r 2               ; YM_DELAY_64 - Delay subroutine for musical note lengths
000075r 2               ; A: number of 64th-note delays to wait (a 64th note @120 BPM = ~ 111,861 clock cycles @ 3.57955 MHz)
000075r 2               YM_DELAY_64:
000075r 2  DA                           phx
000076r 2  5A                           phy
000077r 2  85 rr                        sta         YMTMP1
000079r 2               
000079r 2               @far_outer:
000079r 2  A9 01                        lda         #1          ; 2 cycles
00007Br 2  A2 4B                        ldx         #75
00007Dr 2  A0 8E                        ldy         #142
00007Fr 2  80 04                        bra         @inner
000081r 2               
000081r 2               @outer:
000081r 2  A2 00                        ldx         #0          ; 2 cycles
000083r 2               
000083r 2               @mid:
000083r 2  A0 00                        ldy         #0          ; 2 cycles
000085r 2               
000085r 2               @inner:
000085r 2  88                           dey                     ; 2 cycles
000086r 2  D0 FD                        bne         @inner      ; 3 cycles when branching, 2 when not
000088r 2  CA                           dex                     ; 2 cycles
000089r 2  D0 F8                        bne         @mid        ; 3 cycles when branching, 2 when not
00008Br 2               
00008Br 2                               ; Fine-tuning at end of inner loop
00008Br 2  3A                           dec                     ; 2 cycles
00008Cr 2  D0 F3                        bne         @outer      ; 3 cycles when branching, 2 when not
00008Er 2  C6 rr                        dec         YMTMP1      ; 3 cycles
000090r 2  D0 E7                        bne         @far_outer  ; 3 cycles when branching, 2 when not
000092r 2  7A                           ply
000093r 2  FA                           plx
000094r 2  60                           rts                     ; 6 cycles
000095r 2               ;
000095r 2               ;  end of sound utilities
000095r 2               ;
000095r 2               ;-----------------------------------------------------------------------
000095r 2               ;
000095r 2               ;-------------------------------------------------------------
000095r 2               ;                MATH routines
000095r 2               ;         with MULT16 / DIV16, signs handled by calling word.
000095r 2               ;         we just do the math here.
000095r 2               ;
000095r 2               ;    transition to Z_MATH1,2,3,4 to replace use of TEMP1,2,3,4
000095r 2               ;
000095r 2               ;
000095r 2               MULT16:                             ; 16 x 16 multiply; Z_MATH1 and Z_MATH2 are #'s, Z_MATH1 will be result
000095r 2  64 B6            stz Z_MATH3                       ; with Z_MATH3 as high bytes
000097r 2  64 B7            stz Z_MATH3+1
000099r 2  A2 11            ldx #17
00009Br 2  18               clc
00009Cr 2               MULTLOOP:
00009Cr 2  66 B7            ror Z_MATH3+1                     ; RIGHT.  if you need to go backwards, go backwards stupid fuck.
00009Er 2  66 B6            ror Z_MATH3
0000A0r 2  66 B3            ror Z_MATH1+1
0000A2r 2  66 B2            ror Z_MATH1
0000A4r 2  90 0D            bcc MULTDECCNT
0000A6r 2  18               clc
0000A7r 2  A5 B4            lda Z_MATH2
0000A9r 2  65 B6            adc Z_MATH3
0000ABr 2  85 B6            sta Z_MATH3
0000ADr 2  A5 B5            lda Z_MATH2+1
0000AFr 2  65 B7            adc Z_MATH3+1
0000B1r 2  85 B7            sta Z_MATH3+1
0000B3r 2               MULTDECCNT:
0000B3r 2  CA               dex
0000B4r 2  D0 E6            bne MULTLOOP
0000B6r 2  60               rts
0000B7r 2               
0000B7r 2               ;
0000B7r 2               ;
0000B7r 2               ;           16 / 16 divide; Z_MATH1 and Z_MATH2 #'s - Z_MATH3 is 'overflow'
0000B7r 2               ;           Z_MATH2 divisor, Z_MATH1 dividend, Z_MATH1 + 3 = result + remainder
0000B7r 2               DIV16:
0000B7r 2  64 B6            stz Z_MATH3
0000B9r 2  64 B7            stz Z_MATH3+1
0000BBr 2  A2 10            ldx #16
0000BDr 2               UDIVLP:
0000BDr 2  26 B2            rol Z_MATH1
0000BFr 2  26 B3            rol Z_MATH1+1
0000C1r 2  26 B6            rol Z_MATH3
0000C3r 2  26 B7            rol Z_MATH3+1
0000C5r 2               UDIVCHK:
0000C5r 2  38               sec
0000C6r 2  A5 B6            lda Z_MATH3
0000C8r 2  E5 B4            sbc Z_MATH2
0000CAr 2  A8               tay
0000CBr 2  A5 B7            lda Z_MATH3+1
0000CDr 2  E5 B5            sbc Z_MATH2+1
0000CFr 2  90 04            bcc UDIVCNT
0000D1r 2  84 B6            sty Z_MATH3
0000D3r 2  85 B7            sta Z_MATH3+1
0000D5r 2               UDIVCNT:
0000D5r 2  CA               dex
0000D6r 2  D0 E5            bne UDIVLP
0000D8r 2  26 B2            rol Z_MATH1
0000DAr 2  26 B3            rol Z_MATH1+1
0000DCr 2  60               rts
0000DDr 2               ;
0000DDr 2               ;        32 / 16 divide; Z_MATH2-Z_MATH1 / Z_MATH3 #'s - Z_MATH4 is 'overflow'
0000DDr 2               ;        -> Z_MATH2-Z_MATH1 + Z_MATH4 = result + remainder
0000DDr 2               DIV32:
0000DDr 2  64 B8            stz Z_MATH4
0000DFr 2  64 B9            stz Z_MATH4+1
0000E1r 2  A2 10            ldx #16
0000E3r 2               UDIV32LP:
0000E3r 2  26 B2            rol Z_MATH1
0000E5r 2  26 B3            rol Z_MATH1+1
0000E7r 2  26 B4            rol Z_MATH2
0000E9r 2  26 B5            rol Z_MATH2+1
0000EBr 2  26 B8            rol Z_MATH4
0000EDr 2  26 B9            rol Z_MATH4+1
0000EFr 2               UDIV32CHK:
0000EFr 2  38               sec
0000F0r 2  A5 B8            lda Z_MATH4
0000F2r 2  E5 B6            sbc Z_MATH3
0000F4r 2  A8               tay
0000F5r 2  A5 B9            lda Z_MATH4+1
0000F7r 2  E5 B7            sbc Z_MATH3+1
0000F9r 2  90 04            bcc UDIV32CNT
0000FBr 2  84 B8            sty Z_MATH4
0000FDr 2  85 B9            sta Z_MATH4+1
0000FFr 2               UDIV32CNT:
0000FFr 2  CA               dex
000100r 2  D0 E1            bne UDIV32LP
000102r 2  26 B2            rol Z_MATH1
000104r 2  26 B3            rol Z_MATH1+1
000106r 2  26 B4            rol Z_MATH2
000108r 2  26 B5            rol Z_MATH2+1
00010Ar 2  60               rts
00010Br 2               ;
00010Br 2               ;     galois32o - LSFR psuedo-random # generator
00010Br 2               ;
00010Br 2               ;  -- boilerplate --
00010Br 2               ; 6502 LFSR PRNG - 32-bit
00010Br 2               ; Brad Smith, 2019
00010Br 2               ; http://rainwarrior.ca
00010Br 2               ;
00010Br 2               ;
00010Br 2               galois32o:
00010Br 2                   ; rotate the middle bytes left
00010Br 2  A4 DD            ldy RSEED+2                     ; will move to RSEED+3 at the end
00010Dr 2  A5 DC            lda RSEED+1
00010Fr 2  85 DD            sta RSEED+2
000111r 2                   ; compute RSEED+1 ($C5>>1 = %1100010)
000111r 2  A5 DE            lda RSEED+3                     ; original high byte
000113r 2  4A               lsr
000114r 2  85 DC            sta RSEED+1                     ; reverse: 100011
000116r 2  4A               lsr
000117r 2  4A               lsr
000118r 2  4A               lsr
000119r 2  4A               lsr
00011Ar 2  45 DC            eor RSEED+1
00011Cr 2  4A               lsr
00011Dr 2  45 DC            eor RSEED+1
00011Fr 2  45 DB            eor RSEED+0                     ; combine with original low byte
000121r 2  85 DC            sta RSEED+1
000123r 2                   ; compute RSEED+0 ($C5 = %11000101)
000123r 2  A5 DE            lda RSEED+3                     ; original high byte
000125r 2  0A               asl
000126r 2  45 DE            eor RSEED+3
000128r 2  0A               asl
000129r 2  0A               asl
00012Ar 2  0A               asl
00012Br 2  0A               asl
00012Cr 2  45 DE            eor RSEED+3
00012Er 2  0A               asl
00012Fr 2  0A               asl
000130r 2  45 DE            eor RSEED+3
000132r 2  84 DE            sty RSEED+3                     ; finish rotating byte 2 into 3
000134r 2  85 DB            sta RSEED+0
000136r 2  60               rts
000137r 2               ;-------------------------------------------------------------------
000137r 2               ;                        NUMBER CONVERSIONS
000137r 2               ;
000137r 2               DEC2ASCII:       ;  X is # - return as two digits in Z_MATH3, Z_MATH3+1
000137r 2  A9 30            lda #'0'
000139r 2  85 B7            sta Z_MATH3+1
00013Br 2  8A               txa
00013Cr 2  85 B6            sta Z_MATH3
00013Er 2               D2ASCLOOP:
00013Er 2  38               sec
00013Fr 2  E9 0A            sbc #$0A
000141r 2  90 04            bcc D2ASCNEXT
000143r 2  E6 B7            inc Z_MATH3+1
000145r 2  80 F7            bra D2ASCLOOP
000147r 2               D2ASCNEXT:
000147r 2  18               clc
000148r 2  69 3A            adc #$3A
00014Ar 2  85 B6            sta Z_MATH3
00014Cr 2  60               rts
00014Dr 2               ;

000151r 2  30 14                    bmi             @negative

000155r 2  30 05                    bmi             @pos_loop
000157r 2  A2 0A                    ldx             #10

00015Er 2  30 06                    bmi             @pos_end
000160r 2  E8                       inx

000166r 2

000169r 2  10 02                    bpl             @neg_start

00016Dr 2

000177r 2  30 14                    bmi             @negative
000179r 2  C9 64                    cmp             #100
00017Br 2  30 04                    bmi             @positive
00017Dr 2  E9 64                    sbc             #100
00017Fr 2  A2 0A                    ldx             #10
000181r 2

0001B1r 2               ;-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
0001B1r 2               ;                                   MEMCPY
0001B1r 2               ;    load mainoff ZP_TEMP_VEC -> ZP_TEMP_VEC2, ZP_TEMP_VEC3 is end of copy
0001B1r 2               ;     with current ROM offsets (main = start, ends = finish)
0001B1r 2               ;     and ramstart with destination corresponding to main.
0001B1r 2               ;     Set 'supprint' <> 0 (TEMP5 $FC) to suppress printing of progress bar from Forth
0001B1r 2               ;
0001B1r 2               ;    reworked to used ZP_TEMP_VEC, ZP_TEMP_VEC2, ZP_TEMP_VEC3
0001B1r 2               ;                     mainoff      endsoff       ramstart
0001B1r 2               ;
0001B1r 2               ;
0001B1r 2               ;
0001B1r 2               ; Dan's BIOS Memory Copy
0001B1r 2               ; was ZP_TEMP_VEC: From, ZP_TEMP_VEC2: To, .A.Y: LSB/MSB size
0001B1r 2               ;
0001B1r 2               ;
0001B1r 2               MEMCPY_AY:                     ; to obviate Dan's version and have a length initialized version
0001B1r 2  48               pha
0001B2r 2  A5 rr            lda MC_ENDA                           ; dest in AY version
0001B4r 2  85 rr            sta MC_DEST                           ; copy to dest
0001B6r 2  A5 rr            lda MC_ENDA+1
0001B8r 2  85 rr            sta MC_DEST+1
0001BAr 2  68               pla
0001BBr 2  18               clc
0001BCr 2  65 rr            adc MC_SRCA                           ; add source addr
0001BEr 2  85 rr            sta MC_ENDA
0001C0r 2  98               tya
0001C1r 2  65 rr            adc MC_SRCA+1
0001C3r 2  85 rr            sta MC_ENDA+1
0001C5r 2  B0 2C            bcs CPY_RTS                           ; someone fucked up, bail out
0001C7r 2               MEMCPY:
0001C7r 2  DA               phx                                   ; preserve X
0001C8r 2  A0 00            ldy  #0
0001CAr 2               COPYLOOP:
0001CAr 2  B1 rr            lda (MC_SRCA),y                       ; source address
0001CCr 2  91 rr            sta (MC_DEST),y                       ; dest address
0001CEr 2  A5 rr            lda MC_SRCA
0001D0r 2  C5 rr            cmp MC_ENDA                            ; compare to end address LSB
0001D2r 2  D0 06            bne SKIP1
0001D4r 2  A5 rr            lda MC_SRCA+1                          ; and the MSB
0001D6r 2  C5 rr            cmp MC_ENDA+1
0001D8r 2  F0 17            beq CPY_XRTS
0001DAr 2               SKIP1:
0001DAr 2  A5 FC            lda supprint                           ; suppress progress bar if supprint != 0
0001DCr 2  D0 05            bne SKIP2
0001DEr 2  A9 2E            lda #'.'
0001E0r 2  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
0001E3r 2               SKIP2:
0001E3r 2  E6 rr            inc MC_SRCA                           ; increment source addr
0001E5r 2  D0 02            bne SKIP3
0001E7r 2  E6 rr            inc MC_SRCA+1
0001E9r 2               SKIP3:
0001E9r 2  E6 rr            inc MC_DEST                            ; increment dest
0001EBr 2  D0 02            bne SKIP4
0001EDr 2  E6 rr            inc MC_DEST+1
0001EFr 2               SKIP4:
0001EFr 2  80 D9            bra COPYLOOP
0001F1r 2               CPY_XRTS:
0001F1r 2  18               clc                                    ; all went well?
0001F2r 2  FA               plx                                    ; bring back X
0001F3r 2               CPY_RTS:
0001F3r 2  60               rts

00024Fr 2  0D 0A 00             .byte $0D, $0A, 0

00025Dr 2               DPFCHECK:

00025Fr 2  A9 0D 20 rr      WCRLF_np
000263r 2  rr A9 0A 20

000269r 2               DUMPLP0:

00026Cr 2  F0 06            beq   DUMPCON

000271r 2  C8               iny

000276r 2  18               clc
000277r 2  69 06            adc   #6
000279r 2  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_BYTE      ; print stack pointer b4 jump
00027Cr 2  A9 2F            lda #'/'
00027Er 2  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
000281r 2  E8               inx                         ; -5
000282r 2  E8               inx                         ; -4
000283r 2  E8               inx                         ; -3
000284r 2  E8               inx                         ; -2
000285r 2  E8               inx                         ; -1
000286r 2  BD 00 01         lda   $0100,x               ; get PC LSB
000289r 2  A8               tay
00028Ar 2  E8               inx                         ; 0
00028Br 2  BD 00 01         lda $0100,x
00028Er 2  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_BYTE        ; get PC MSB
000291r 2  98               tya

000295r 2  A9 2F            lda #'/'
000297r 2  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
00029Ar 2                       ;   whew
00029Ar 2                       ;
00029Ar 2  CA               dex                         ; -1
00029Br 2  CA               dex                         ; -2
00029Cr 2  BD 00 01         lda   $0100,x               ; get status byte
00029Fr 2  A0 08            ldy   #8
0002A1r 2               DUMPLP2:
0002A1r 2  2A               rol
0002A2r 2  48               pha
0002A3r 2  90 04            bcc   DUMPSKx               ; NV-BDIZC
0002A5r 2  A9 31            lda   #'1'

0002ABr 2               DUMPSKy:

0002B0r 2  D0 EF            bne   DUMPLP2
0002B2r 2  A9 2F            lda #'/'

0002B7r 2  CA               dex                         ; -3
0002B8r 2  BD 00 01         lda $0100,x
0002BBr 2  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_BYTE              ; print A
0002BEr 2  A9 2F            lda #'/'

0002C3r 2  CA               dex                         ; -4
0002C4r 2  BD 00 01         lda $0100,x

0002E0r 2  A0 00            ldy   #0
0002E2r 2               DUMPLP1:                        ; print message
0002E2r 2  B9 rr rr         lda   DUMPMSG1, y
0002E5r 2  F0 06            beq   DREGLP1
0002E7r 2  20 rr rr         jsr   WRITE_CHAR
0002EAr 2  C8               iny

0002EDr 2               DREGLP1:
0002EDr 2  64 B0            stz   Z_DEBUG               ; clear for dumping pages below
0002EFr 2  20 rr rr         jsr   READ_CHAR             ; wait for a key
0002F2r 2  90 F9            bcc   DREGLP1
0002F4r 2  C9 73            cmp   #'s'              ; print out stacks?
0002F6r 2  D0 03            bne   DREGSK3
0002F8r 2  20 rr rr         jsr   DUMPSTACK
0002FBr 2               DREGSK3:
0002FBr 2  C9 7A            cmp   #'z'              ; print out zp?
0002FDr 2  D0 03            bne  DREGSK5
0002FFr 2  20 rr rr         jsr  DUMPZP
000302r 2               DREGSK5:
000302r 2  C9 74            cmp   #'t'              ; print out INBUF?
000304r 2  D0 03            bne  DREGSK4
000306r 2  20 rr rr         jsr  DUMPTIB
000309r 2               DREGSK4:
000309r 2  C9 78            cmp   #'x'
00030Br 2  D0 03            bne   DREGELSE
00030Dr 2  20 00 FE         jsr   $FE00                 ; go to Wozmon if necc
000310r 2               DREGELSE:
000310r 2  C9 0D            cmp   #$0D                 ; continue on enter
000312r 2  F0 03            beq   DREGNXT
000314r 2  4C rr rr         jmp   DPFCHECK              ; otherwise print eveything again
000317r 2               DREGNXT:
000317r 2  7A               ply                         ; and restore everything
000318r 2  FA               plx
000319r 2  68               pla
00031Ar 2  28               plp
00031Br 2  60               rts

00031Cr 2               ;   subs for particular pages

000320r 2  85 B1            sta Z_DEBUG+1
000322r 2  4C rr rr         jmp DUMPPDBG
000325r 2               DUMPSTACK:                          ; dump DS/RT stack area
000325r 2  08               php
000326r 2  48               pha

000329r 2  85 B1            sta Z_DEBUG+1
00032Br 2  4C rr rr         jmp DUMPPDBG
00032Er 2               DUMPZP:                             ; dump ZP
00032Er 2  08               php
00032Fr 2  48               pha
000330r 2  64 B1            stz Z_DEBUG+1
000332r 2  4C rr rr         jmp DUMPPDBG
000335r 2               ;

000339r 2               DUMPLOOP:

00033Br 2  65 B0            adc Z_DEBUG
00033Dr 2  48               pha
00033Er 2  90 04            bcc DUMPSK2
000340r 2  A5 B1            lda Z_DEBUG+1
000342r 2  1A               inc
000343r 2  2C               .byte $2C                    ; skip 2 bytes
000344r 2               DUMPSK2:

000346r 2  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_BYTE

000352r 2               DPLOOP2:

000357r 2  A9 20            lda #$20
000359r 2  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR              ; print space between columns
00035Cr 2  C8               iny
00035Dr 2  98               tya
00035Er 2  29 0F            and #$0F                    ; 00001111
000360r 2  F0 02            beq  DPSKIP
000362r 2  80 EE            bra  DPLOOP2
000364r 2               DPSKIP:

00036Cr 2  E9 10            sbc #$10                    ; subtract 16 to rewind the line
00036Er 2  A8               tay
00036Fr 2               DPPLOOP:

000371r 2  C9 20            cmp #$20

000000r 2               RESET_VECTOR_START:
000000r 2  A9 00                    lda     #0                    ; ZERO the W register
000002r 2  8D F3 FF                 sta     W_REGISTER
000005r 2  D8                       cld                                                     ; We don't like decimal mode
000006r 2  8D F0 FF                 sta                 T_REGISTER                          ; Make sure task 0 is selected
000009r 2  85 00                    sta                 $00                                 ; Init RAM Bank selector
00000Br 2  85 01                    sta                 $01                                 ; Init ROM Bank selector
00000Dr 2  A2 FF                    ldx                 #$FF                                ; Init stack pointer
00000Fr 2  9A                       txs
